# Kṛṣṇa Bhakti Rāsa Kadamba

## Chapter I

*Śrī Rāma-Kṛṣṇābhyāṁ namaḥ*

**Maṅgalācaranam**

*sādvaitaṁ sāvadhūtaṁ ca saganam sundara-priyam sarvāvatāra bījaṁ taṁ Kṛṣṇa Caitanyamāśrāye (1.1)*

**Перевод:**

**I take shelter of the seed of all avatāras, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, and His associate’s Śrī Advaita, the avadhūta Śrī Nityānanda, Śrī Sundarānanda and his dear associates.**

*sarāmaṁ naumi gopālaṁ gopāla-caranaṁ prabhum sundarānanda-gopālaṁ parni gopālakaṁ tathā (1.2)*

**Перевод:**

**I always offer my respectful obeisances unto all the gopālas (in the disciplic succession from Sundarānanda Ṭhākura) who are my shelters, such as my spiritual master (and father) Gopāla-Caraṇa, and his parama-guru, Parni-gopāla (Sundarānanda Ṭhākura ’s direct disciple).**

*ajñāna timirāṇdasye tyadyaiḥ padyaiḥ purātanaiḥ namāmi guru-govindau vaiṣṇavān bhagavat priyān (1.3)*

**Перевод:**

**We are always situated in ignorance, having given up the shelter of their feet since time immemorial, Therefore, I offer my respects unto Lord Govinda, and my guru who is very dear to the Lord and His topmost pure devotees.**

Gautamīya-Tantra states:

*oṁ aj’ñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ (1.4)*

**Перевод:**

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who with the torchlight of knowledge has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance.\***

*akhaṇḍamaṇdalākāraṁ vyāptaṁ yena carācaram tat padaṁ darśitaṁ yena tasmai śrī gurave namaḥ (1.5)*

*gurur brahmā gurur viṣṇu rityārdiḥ (1.6)*

**Перевод:**

**Both Lord Brahmā and Lord Viṣṇu are our gurus as shown by their behavior.**

*ajñāna timire dhandha, jīvagana chila andha, jihon kaila andhakāra nāsa jñāna rupa anjanadiyā, caksu dhandha ghucāiyā, kṛpā kari karila prakāśa (1.7)*

**Перевод:**

**All living entities were situated in darkness, before meeting that person who applied the medicine of transcendental knowledge which forced open their eyes. Their mercy made the manifestation of their spiritual life possible.**

*caksudāna kailā jihon, janmadātā punaḥ tihon, janme janme tihon hau gati tānra pādapadma yuge, praṇati kariye āge, yāha haite haya suddha mati (1.8)*

**Перевод:**

**He, who gave me this vision, is the same person who has given me my life again. Life after life may he be my goal. Unto his lotus feet I have offered my obeisances, by his mercy I get purified intelligence.**

*akhaṇḍa maṇḍala vyāpta, jihon sarvabhūta gata, trijagate ye haya kārana yāhān haite carācara, vyāpta brahma maṇḍala, sakalera ādi sanātana (1.9)*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme Lord is all pervading, the origin of all living entities, the cause of the three worlds, primeval, eternal, the source of Brahman and the creator of both the animate and inanimate worlds.**

*sei Kṛṣṇa pada jihon, dekhāila guru tihon, tāṅhāra carane kari nati yāṅhāra karunā vale, bhava-sindhu tari hele, parināme dena Kṛṣṇa bhakti (1.10)*

**Перевод:**

**He who showed me that Supreme Lord is the spiritual master and I offer my humble obeisances unto his lotus feet. By his strength I can cross the ocean of material existence. He ultimately imparts pure devotional service to Kṛṣṇa.**

*guru brahmā viṣṇu guru, maheśvara kalpataru, paraṁ brahma guru dayāmaya viṣṇu ādi devi devā, gayā gaṅgā kṣetra sevā, gurura adhika kichu naya (1.11)*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahma, Lord Viṣṇu and Lord Śiva and the Supreme Brahman are all like desire trees and non-different from the most merciful spiritual master. Service to Viṣṇu, to the demigods, and visiting the holy places are not equal to the service of the spiritual master.**

*guru gati guru mati, guru satya guru pati, guru bandhu bāndhava svajana guru pitā guru mātā, guru bhakti patha dātā, guru sarva siddhira kārana (1.12)*

**Перевод:**

**The spiritual master is the only goal of life, object of thought, the eternal truth, the protector, the friend, the relative, the father, the mother, the bestower of devotional service and the cause of all perfection.**

*śrī guru vandanā kori, praṇāma kariye hari, bhāgavata pāde brahma stuti bighna bināsana hetu, bhavābdhi tarana setu, ei padye kariye praṇati (1.13)*

**Перевод:**

**After offering prayers to the spiritual master I offer my humble obeisances to Lord Hari in order to destroy the impediments and to cross the material ocean following in the footsteps of Lord Brahmā who offered his prayers to Lord Kṛṣṇa in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam 10.14.1 :**

*Śrī-brahmovāca*  
*naumīḍya te ’bhra-vapuṣe taḍid-ambarāya guñjāvataṁsa-paripiccha-lasan-mukhāya vanya-sraje kavala-vetra-viṣāṇa-veṇu-lakṣma-śriye mṛdu-pade paśupāṅgajāya (1.14)*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahmā said: My dear Lord, You are the only worshipable Lord, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and therefore I offer my humble obeisances and prayers just to please You. O son of the king of the cowherds, Your transcendental body is dark blue like a new cloud, Your garment is brilliant like lightning, and the beauty of Your face is enhanced by Your guñjā earrings and the peacock feather on Your head. Wearing garlands of various forest flowers and leaves, and equipped with a herding stick, a buffalo horn and a flute, You stand beautifully with a morsel of food in Your hand.**

*noumi kriyā upādāna, īḍya samvodhan gaṇa, viriñci sāksāte stuti kare meghatulya bāpu jāra, taḍitā vasana tāra , guñjā-mālā suśobhita sire (1.15)*

**Перевод:**

**The word ‘noumi’ or (meaning) ‘I offer my obeisances’ is a verb and the word ‘idya’ or ‘the praiseworthy’ is an address. Thus Lord Brahmā prays directly to Lord Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa’s body is like new cloud. His garment resembles the color of a lightning and His head is decorated with guñjā-mālā.**

*tāhe śikhi pākhā jāni, sulakṣita mukhakhāni, vanamālā birājita tanu bāma haste subechinna, saphala sadadhi anna, jathare sabhita betra benu (1.16)*

**Перевод:**

**He wears peacock feather on His head. His face is enchanting and all auspicious. He wears a garland of forest flowers. He is holding fruits and a pot of rice mixed with yogurt in His left hand. A stick and a flute are beautifully tucked in His belly.**

*mṛdu su-komala pada, karayuge subhāgada, paśupa aṅgajā bhāra haya jātite vyutpatti kari, pā sabha rakṣaṇe bali, paśu śabda dhenu gaṇe kaya (1.17)*

**Перевод:**

**His lotus feet are soft and tender and He wears beautiful armlets on His hands. He was born from the protector of the cows or Nanda Mahārāja. The word ‘paśu’ means the cows and the word ‘pā’ means to protect.**

*gosvāmīra vyākhyā śuna, paśupā yāhake kana, paśupa kahiye maṇḍarāje paśuna pāti vyākhyā iti, aṅge jāta Kṛṣṇa tathi, līlā hetu sādhujana kāje (1.18)*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear the explanation of the word ‘paśupa’ given by the Gosvāmī The word ‘paśupā’ refers to Nanda Mahārāja. In order to manifest His wonderful sweet pastimes to benefit the devotees, Lord Kṛṣṇa was born from Nanda Mahārāja who protected the cows.**

*nandātmaja vraje hari, gopabeśa aṅgikāri, vihāra karilā naralīlā narākṛti dekhi vane, bismaya devatāgaṇe, sei līlā daśame barṇilā (1.19)*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Hari appeared as the son of Nanda in Vraja and enjoyed pastimes in a human form as a cowherd boy. Seeing His human form, the demigods were astonished. These pastimes are described in the tenth canto of Śrīmad Bhāgavatam.**

*prārabdha karma khaṇḍana, bahu bighna-bināśana, govinda smaraṇa mātra haya grantha samāpti hetu, bhavasindhu pāra setu, nātirūpa maṅgalācaraṇa (1.20)*

**Перевод:**

**Simply by remembering the lotus feet of Govinda all one’s reactions of fruitive activities and the obstacles on the path of spiritual life are destroyed. Therefore in order to complete this book and to cross beyond the ocean of material existence, I am offering obeisances in the form of ‘maṅgalācaraṇa’.**

*sei Kṛṣṇa pada dhuli, sire laiyā kutuholi, gaṇapati pranāma samaye tri jagatera bighna yata, khaṇḍe yevā tāhe nata, bighna-bināśa tāhe kahe (1.21)*

**Перевод:**

**Taking the dust from the lotus feet of that Kṛṣṇa on his head, Gaṇapati destroys the impediments of the three worlds. So anyone who offers obeisances at the feet of Gaṇapati will surpass all the impediments.**

*bighna bināyaka bali, laiyā yāra pāda-dhūli, gaṇapati hailā pradhāna sei nandātmaja hari, tāhāke pranāma kari, ādi-puruṣa bhāgavana (1.22)*

**Перевод:**

**Gaṇapati became renowned as the destroyer of the obstacles because he accepted the dust from the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa on his head. I offer my humble obeisances unto that Lord Kṛṣṇa, the son of Mahārāja Nanda and the primeval Lord.**

It is mentioned in ‘yāmala’ as follows -- (Brahmā Saṁhitā 5. 50)

*yat pāda-pallaba-yugaṁ vinidhāya kumbha-dvandve praṇāma-samaye sa gaṇādhirājaḥ vighnān vihantum alam asya jagat-trayasya govindam ādi-puruṣāṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi (1.23)*

**Перевод:**

**“I adore that primeval Lord Govinda, whose lotus feet are always held by Gaṇeśa upon the pair of tumuli protruding from his elephant head in order to obtain power for his function of destroying all the obstacles on the path of progress of the three worlds.”**

*bānchā kalpatarumaya, patita pāvana haya, sādhu sānta Vaiṣṇava gosāi kṛpāra samudra bara, vaiṣṇava ṭhākura mora, sata nati tānhā sabāra thānhi (1.24)*

**Перевод:**

**The peaceful Vaiṣṇava devotees are desire trees and deliverers of the fallen souls. They are the ocean of mercy and my eternal Lords. Hence I offer my repeated obeisances at their lotus feet.**

*ṭhākura Vaiṣṇava gaṇa, kalpavṛkṣa rupa hona, tabe mora kānhu nāhi bhaya āra eka kathā śuni, patita pavna jāni, ihāte bharasā mane haya (1.25)*

**Перевод:**

**Since the great vaiṣṇavas are like the wish-fulfilling trees therefore I have nothing fear. I have a great faith in them because they are the deliverers of the fallen souls.**

*āmi ta patitajana, duṣṭamati akiñcana, patita pāvanera diye dāya patita pāvana jihon, uddhāribe more tihon, kṛpā-sindhu jāni kahi tāya (1.26)*

**Перевод:**

**I am the most fallen, evil minded, poor and thus a fit candidate to be delivered. The deliverer of the fallen souls will certainly deliver me because I am confident that he is the ocean of compassion.**

*kalpataru yānrā hona, bāñchā pūrṇa bara dena, bāñcita māgiyā niba bara ohe Kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇa, sabhāra pāye nivedana, bāñchita āmara siddha kara (1.27)*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees who are just like desire trees, give benediction to fulfill one’s desires. Therefore, of devotees of Kṛṣṇa, I appeal at the feet of all of you to kindly benedict me to fulfill my desire.**

*Vaiṣṇava mahimā āmi, ki barṇibā kivā jāni, yāṅra guna purāṇe bākhāne tīrtha avagāha kāle, se tīrtha kṛtārtha bale, tīrtha tīrtha haya sei dine (1.28)*

**Перевод:**

**What do I know about the glories of the Vaiṣṇavas and how will I describe them? The Purāṇas describe the qualities of the Vaiṣṇavas. When the Vaiṣṇavas take a holy dip in the holy water of a holy place, then the personification of that holy place becomes satisfied and the holy place actually becomes holy from that moment.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states 1.13.10:

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni svāntaḥ-sthena gadābhṛtā (1.29)*

**Перевод:**

**“My Lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage.**

*godāvari ādi kata, jalamaya tīrtha yata, śilādimayi deva gaṇa darśana karite nare, pāpa ghucāite nāre, bahukāle karaye khaṇḍana (1.30)*

**Перевод:**

**Many holy rivers such as Godāvari and the deity forms of the demigods cannot purify the living entities just by their darśana, for they take a long time to destroy their sins.**

*sādhu saṅga samāgane, pāpa khaṇḍe tataksane , ei hetu mahimā apāra sāksāt devatā maya, kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇa haya, tāhā sabhe āge namaskāra (1.31)*

**Перевод:**

**But as soon as the living entities see a devotee their sinful reactions are destroyed. Therefore their glories are insurmountable. The devotees of Kṛṣṇa are as good as the demigods. So first of all I offer my humble obeisances at the lotus feet of the devotees.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states 10.48.31:

*na hy am-mayāni tīrthāni na devā mṛc-chilā-mayāḥ te punanty uru-kālena darśanād eva sādhavaḥ (1.32)*

**Перевод:**

**No one can deny that there are holy places with sacred rivers, or that the demigods appear in deity forms made of earth and stone. But these purify the soul only after a long time, whereas saintly persons purify just by being seen.**

*tāhe ei, vibaraṇa śāstre kare nirūpaṇa, sādhu āra tīrthera mahimā tīrtha snāna avagāhe, sarva pāpa kṣayahaye, nāhi ghuce kumati vāsanā (1.33)*

**Перевод:**

**It has been ascertained by the scriptures that both the devotees and the holy places are glorious. Although bathing in holy water destroys ones sins, it cannot eliminate the desire for committing sinful activities.**

*sādhu saṅga guna ithi, hṛdi khaṇḍe duṣṭamati , nirmala hṛdaya hayajāni sādhusaṅga ei phala, citta haya nirmala, pāpa tāpa khaṇḍaye āpani (1.34)*

**Перевод:**

**But the benefit of associating with the devotees is that it immediately vanquishes the seed of sinful activities and the heart becomes pure. This is the result of ‘sādhusaṅga’. The sinful propensity and the threefold miseries are automatically destroyed.**

*guru kṛṣṇa vaiṣṇava-gaṇe, bhinna bhāva kari māne, se jana pāsaṇḍi daṇḍya haya tāra śāsti kare yane, vaiṣṇava ye nāhi māne, viṣṇu-rūpa vaiṣṇava niścaya (1.35)*

**Перевод:**

**One who considers that the spiritual master, Lord Kṛṣṇa and the Vaiṣṇavas are different, is certainly an atheist and is fit to be punished by Yamarāja. Know it for certain that the Vaiṣṇavas are manifestations of Viṣṇu.**

*ei mora nivedane, guru kṛṣṇa vaiṣṇava sthāne, iṣṭa siddhi karaha nirbighne bhāṣā chande grantha-khāni, siddha kara guṇamani, kṛṣṇa līlā gāi prema magne 1.36*

**Перевод:**

**This is my request to my spiritual master, Lord Kṛṣṇa and the Vaiṣṇavas: Please bless me so that I can attain my goal of life. O possessors of transcendental qualities, kindly fulfill my attempt to write this book with sublime language, so that I can sing the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa and be merged in ecstasy.**

*kumati ghucibe yabe, kṛṣṇa bhakti haiba tabe, ei hetu mane prabhodhiye mane he bāndhava mora, carane dhariye tora, danta tṛṇe tore nivediye (1.37)*

**Перевод:**

**When ones mind is purified then he develops devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. This is my only solace. Therefore my friend, O Mind, I hold your feet. Taking a straw in my mouth, I request you.**

*yateka indriya-gaṇa, tora bhṛtya-tulya haña, tumi tāhe hao adhirāja tumi se sahāya haile, tomā base sabhe chale, tomā haite siddha haya kāja (1.38)*

**Перевод:**

**O Mind, all the senses are like your servants and you are their commanding master. If you are favorable then all other senses are automatically controlled. One can achieve perfection by your help.**

*megha nijabasa naya, sadā vāyubasa haya, temati indriya tomāra basa tore mora e-minati, asat-patha chāḍa mati, sādhu-pathe nā diyā apayaśa (1.39)*

**Перевод:**

**Just as the clouds are always controlled by the wind so similarly, you control the senses. So please give up the sinful path, O mind. Do not pollute the path of the devotees. This is my request.**

*sādhu saṅga saṅgati kari, akapaṭe bhaja hari, viśvās kariyā guru pāya sādhu kṛpāvāna haile, saṁsāra taribe hele, sarva siddhi Vaiṣṇava kṛpāya (1.40)*

**Перевод:**

**Worship Lord Hari in the association of the devotees without any duplicity. Keep faith in the words of the spiritual master. By the mercy of the devotees one can easily cross the ocean of material existence and achieve all perfection.**

*maṅgalācaraṇa āra, mana-śiksā bāraṁbāra, tripadī caṇḍete ārambhila nija-gaṇa jānāite, śloka bhāgi bhāsā gite, e-nayanānanda biracila (1.41)*

**Перевод:**

**I, Śrī Nayanānanda have thus composed the maṅgalācaraṇa and teachings to the mind in tripadī meter just to help the readers understand about the above quoted saṅskṛta verses.**

*vañchā-kalpatarubhyaś ca kṛpā-sindhubhya eva ca patitānāṁ pāvanebhyo vaiṣṇavebhyo namo namaḥ (1.42)*

**Перевод:**

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto all the Vaiṣṇava devotees of the Lord. They are just like desire trees who can fulfill the desires of everyone, and they are full of compassion for the fallen conditioned souls.**

*mane prabodhiye puna śuna ārabāra, yadi vañchā thāke bhava sindhu haite pāra bhaja kṛṣṇa smara kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa nāma nāya, kṛṣṇa bhakta saṅga kari kṛṣṇa guna gāi (1.43)*

**Перевод:**

**Please hear attentively once again. If you desire to cross the ocean of material existence then please worship Kṛṣṇa, remember Kṛṣṇa, chant the holy names of Kṛṣṇa, and associate with the devotees of Kṛṣṇa while singing about His transcendental qualities.**

*anāyāse japa tapa kṛṣṇa sevā bine, saṁsāra taribe yadi thāke e-bhavane sarva karma tyāga kari kara kṛṣṇa sevā, kāya mane śrī kṛṣṇera anugata habā (1.44)*

**Перевод:**

**If you want to remain at home yet be delivered from material existence without undergoing austerity, chanting mantras or visiting holy places, then give up all fruitive activities and serve Kṛṣṇa with your body and mind.**

*bhāgavate kahena brahmā sāksāt bhāgavane, āpanāke dainya kari kare nivedane tomāra anukampā haya yei savajane, se yadi bhuñjāye āpana pūrba prāktane (1.45)*

*kānho nāhi jāya sei tīrtha nāhi kare, ghare thāke śyāmatanu cintaye antare tanu mana vākyakrame yevā kare nati anāyāse mukti-pāda tāra haya gati (1.46)*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahmā prays directly to Lord Kṛṣṇa in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam with great humility wherein he says: Even if one enjoys the result of his previous deeds but if he is blessed by you, then he can easily attain liberation, although he does not visit holy places. He remembers Kṛṣṇa and offers Him obeisances with his body, mind and speech.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states 10.14.8:

*tat te ’nukampāṁ su-samīkṣamāno bhuñjāna evātma-kṛtaṁ vipākam hṛd-vāg-vapurbhir vidadhan namas te jīveta yo mukti-pade sa dāya-bhāk (1.47)*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Lord, one who earnestly waits for You to bestow Your causeless mercy upon him, all the while patiently suffering the reactions of his past misdeeds and offering You respectful obeisances with his heart, words and body, is surely eligible for liberation, for it has become his rightful claim.**

*sarva tīrtha snāna haya ghare ta basiyā, kṛṣṇa nāma līlā śune mane niṣṭhā haiyā kṛṣṇa nāma kṛṣṇa līlā kṛṣṇa guṇādi kīrtana, bhakti śāstra bhāgavata purāṇa vacana jekhāne kṛṣṇera kathā sekhāne sarvatīrthī, gaṅgā yamunā godāvarī sarasvatī (1.48)*

**Перевод:**

**The bhakti śāstra, Bhāgavata Purāṇa concludes that one should sing the glories of Kṛṣṇa’s names, pastimes, and qualities with firm faith. Wherever the topics of Kṛṣṇa are discussed, even while sitting at home, is said to have already taken bath in all the holy places and holy rivers headed by the Gaṅgā, Yamunā, Godavarī and Sarasvatī.**

Śrīdhara Swami states:

*“tatraiva gaṅgā yamunā ca tatra godāvarī tatra sarasvatī ca sarvāni tirthāni vasanti tatra acyuta udāra kathā prasaṅgaḥ” (1.49)*

**Перевод:**

**“Wherever the magnanimous topics of Kṛṣṇa are discussed, all the reservoirs of water headed by the Gaṅgā, Yamunā, Godavarī and Sarasvatī are present.”**

*kṛṣṇa kathā prasaṅga bhaktera kīrtana, sarva tīrtha saha kṛṣṇa ṭānhā āgamana (1.50)*

*bhakta rave dhenu jena nā raya anya sthāne, aiche kṛṣṇa kare gati bhakta janāra sthāne*

**Перевод:**

**As soon as the devotees of Kṛṣṇa begin to glorify Him, then He comes along with all the tīrthas to appear there. Just as a cow cannot remain unmoved when her calf calls, similarly Kṛṣṇa is spontaneously attracted towards His devotees.**

Skanda Purāṇa states:

*“yatra yatra mahipāla vaiṣṇava bartate kathā, tatra tatra harir yāti goryathā suta vatsalā”.*

**Перевод:**

**“O king, wherever the vaiṣṇavas are engaged in discussing the topics of Hari the Lord immediately goes there, just as an affectionately attached cow goes to her calf.” (1.51)**

*ataeva kṛṣṇapāda sarvāśraya kara, tānhā bahi keha nāhi sevaka vatsala sei padmalocana bine saṁsāra sāgare, dukhira dukha ghucāite āra keha nāre (1.52)*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore one should take shelter of the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. There is no one who is more affectionate towards His servant. Only the lotus eyed Lord is able to vanquish the miseries of material existence.**

*sunīti kahila dhruve ei kathā sāra, hari bine dukha chettā nāhi keha āra (1.53)*

**Перевод:**

**Sunīti the mother of Dhruva Mahārāja, spoke to her son that no one else but Hari is capable of destroying one’s miseries.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states: 4.8.23

*nānyad tataḥ padma-palāśa-locanād duḥkha-cchidaṁ te mṛgayāmi kañcanayo mṛgyate hasta-gṛhīta-padmayā śriyetarair aṅga vimṛgyamāṇayā (1.54)*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Dhruva, as far as I am concerned, I do not find anyone who can mitigate your distress but the Supreme Personality of Godhead, whose eyes are like lotus petals. Many demigods such as Lord Brahmā seek the pleasure of the goddess of fortune, but the goddess of fortune herself, with a lotus flower in her hand, is always ready to render service to the Supreme Lord.**

*ke āche emana jāni dayāmaya āra, hena prabhu chāḍiyā sarana nibe tāra harisama dayāmaya kāṅhu nāhi dekhi, puraṇe bekata tāra pūtanā sākṣī (1.55)*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore after knowing about such a merciful Lord, who will take shelter of someone else? One cannot find anyone more mercifuḷ then Lord Hari for this is confirmed in the Śrīmad Bhāgavatam by the incident of Pūtanā.**

*aribhāve naṣṭa karite gelā jāni, śiśubuddhe kṛṣṇake karila kole āni biṣa stana dila mukhe māribāra tare, stanapāna kari hari badhilena tāre (1.56)*

**Перевод:**

**Pūtanā came as an enemy to kill baby Kṛṣṇa. She took Him on her lap and fed Him milk from her poison smeared breast, Kṛṣṇa drank Her breast milk and killed her.**

*ante tāya gati śuna balite bismaya, mātrigati dila kṛṣṇa tāhāte niścaya kole kari aṅganāte pāila mātrigati, emana dayāla prabhu āra pāba kati (1.57)*

**Перевод:**

**Please hear about her amazing fate. She was awarded the position of the Lord’s mother. She achieved this position simply because she took Kṛṣṇa on her lap in the courtyard of the Lord’s house. Where can one get such a merciful Lord?**

*sneha kari anukṣaṇe jenā seve tāre. tāhāra vidhāna puna ke kahite pāre (1.58)*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, who can describe the good fortune of one who serves the Lord with affection?**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states: 3.2.23

*aho bakī yaṁ stana-kāla-kūṭaṁ jighāṁsayāpāyayad apy asādhvī lebhe gatiṁ dhātry-ucitāṁ tato ’nyaṁ kaṁ vā dayāluṁ śaraṇaṁ vrajema (1.59)*

**Перевод:**

**Alas, how shall I take shelter of one more merciful than He who granted the position of mother to a she-demon \[Pūtanā\] although she was unfaithful and she prepared deadly poison to be sucked from her breast?**

*ki kahiba sei prabhura nāmera mahimā. nāmābhāse mukta haya ei jāni simā (1.60)*

**Перевод:**

**What can I describe about the glories of the holy names of the Lord? One is certainly liberated by chanting the holy names of the Lord even in the stage of nāmābhāsa, or the dim reflection of the holy names?**

*saṇketa rahasya chale stabhakrame jāni, helāte vā konajana Krṣṇa nāma śuni, patita haiyā vā vākyera skhalane, astrādite biddha haiyā karaye smarane, sarpādira bhaye kimbā avaśa haiyā, nāmābhāse laya nāma vastu nā bujhiyā, tathāpi tāhāte nāhi yama adhikāra, ye laya āmāra prabhura nāma ekabāra (1.61)*

**Перевод:**

**If one chants and hears the holy names of Kṛṣṇa even once, as an intimation, joke, on some pretext, through deceptive words of consolation, with negligence, or who thinks Kṛṣṇa as being fallen, a slipping of words, while being pierced with a weapon, due to fear of snakes, in an unconscious state, or if one chants the holy names without understanding the purport of the mantra, such persons are freed from the control of Yamarāja.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states 6.2.14-15:

*sāṇketyaṁ pārihāsyaṁ vā stobhaṁ helanam eva vā vaikuṇṭha-nāma-grahaṇam aśeṣāgha-haraṁ viduḥ patitaḥ skhalito bhagnaṁ sandaṣṭas tapta āhataḥ harir ity avaśenāha pumān nārhati yātanāḥ (1.62-63)*

**Перевод:**

**One who chants the holy name of the Lord is immediately freed from the reactions of unlimited sins, even if he chants indirectly \[to indicate something else\], jokingly, for musical entertainment, or even neglectfully. This is accepted by all the learned scholars of the scriptures. If one chants the holy name of Hari and then dies because of an accidental misfortune, such as falling from the top of a house, slipping and suffering broken bones while traveling on the road, being bitten by a serpent, being afflicted with pain and high fever, or being injured by a weapon, one is immediately absolved from having to enter hellish life, even though he is sinful.**

*āpanāte muktyabhimāni jāni jevā jana, kṛṣṇapāda padmayuge mati hina hañā seha yadi bahu dukhe uchhapada pāya, nā bhajiyā kṛṣṇapāda adhapāte jāya (1.64)*

**Перевод:**

**If someone out of pride considers himself a liberated soul and thus forgets the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, he certainly falls down due to not worshipping the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. Thus even though such a person may advance to a materially high position by dint of their austerities nevertheless they degrade.**

*anyera śaraṇe māhi bhavābdhi tarana, ata eva anya sevāya kibā prayojana (1.65)*

**Перевод:**

**One cannot cross beyond the ocean of material existence by taking shelter of anyone else (then Kṛṣṇa). What is the need of serving anyone else?**

*ei bhava sindhu tare kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇa, abhakta gṛhāṇdakūpe sadā paḍyā rana (1.66)*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of Kṛṣṇa easily surpass this ocean of material existence, whereas the non-devotees remain fallen in the dark well of material life.**

*kūpete paḍile paśura nā haya uddhāra, kṛṣṇa ye nā bhaje loka taiche jāni tāra (1.67)*

**Перевод:**

**Just as an animal cannot be delivered if it is fallen in a well, similarly one who does not worship Kṛṣṇa meets the same fate.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states 10.2.32:

*ye ’nye ’ravindākṣa vimukta-māninas tvayy asta-bhāvād aviśuddha-buddhayaḥ āruhya kṛcchreṇa paraṁ padaṁ tataḥ patanty adho ’nādṛta-yuṣmad-aṅghrayaḥ (1.68)*

**Перевод:**

**\[Someone may say that aside from devotees, who always seek shelter at the Lord’s lotus feet, there are those who are not devotees but who have accepted different processes for attaining salvation. What happens to them? In answer to this question, Lord Brahmā and the other demigods said:\] O lotus-eyed Lord, although non-devotees who accept severe austerities and penances to achieve the highest position may think themselves liberated, their intelligence is impure. They fall down from their position of imagined superiority because they have no regard for Your lotus feet.**

*ataeva bhāgavate kahe devagana, kṛṣṇa bine bhavasindhu nā haya tarana (1.69)*

**Перевод:**

**The demigods state in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam that without the mercy of Kṛṣṇa one cannot be liberated from material existence.**

*paripūrṇa kāma kṛṣṇa śānta dayāmaya, tānra padāśraya kaile sarva siddhi haya (1.70)*

**Перевод:**

**Lord kṛṣṇa is self satisfied, peaceful and full of compassion. One who takes shelter of His lotus feet achieves all perfection.**

*hena kṛṣṇa chāḍiyā anyera kare āsā, saṁsāra samudra pāra haite bharasā, jāhāja kariya tyāga sindhu taribāre, bāhubale tariba sindhu hena citte kare (1.71)*

**Перевод:**

**People foolishly abandon such a merciful Lord Kṛṣṇa and keep faith in others to help cross the material ocean. They give up the ship and think that by their own strength they will cross the ocean of material existence.**

*svalaṇgula dhariyā jāya sindhu taribāre, nā pāre tarite ātmāghāti haiyā mare (1.72)*

**Перевод:**

**They want to cross the ocean by holding a dogs tail, but they become a failure and in the process kill themselves.**

*kukura haite sindhu jena nahe pāra, ataeva anyāśraye nāhika uddhāra (1.73)*

**Перевод:**

**As one cannot cross the ocean with the help of a dog, so one cannot be delivered if he takes shelter of someone else.**

*kṛṣṇāśrita bine ei saṁsāra sāgara, pāra nāhi haya keha kahila nirbhara (1.74)*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, take it for granted that unless one takes shelter of Kṛṣṇa, he is not delivered from the ocean of material existence.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states 6.9.21:

*Śrī-devā ūcuḥ vāyv-ambarāgny-ap-kṣitayas tri-lokā brahmādayo ye vayam udvijantaḥ harāma yasmai balim antako ’sau bibheti yasmād araṇaṁ tato naḥ (1.75)*

**Перевод:**

**The demigods said: The three worlds are created by the five elements—namely ether, air, fire, water and earth—which are controlled by various demigods, beginning from Lord Brahm?. Being very much afraid that the time factor will end our existence, we offer presentations unto time by performing our work as time dictates. The time factor himself, however, is afraid of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Therefore let us now worship that Supreme Lord, who alone can give us full protection.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states 6.2.18:

*ajñānād athavā jñānād uttamaśloka-nāma yat saṅkīrtitam aghaṁ puṁso dahed edho yathānalaḥ (1.76)*

**Перевод:**

**As a fire burns dry grass to ashes, so the holy name of the Lord, whether chanted knowingly or unknowingly, burns to ashes, without fail, all the reactions of one’s sinful activities.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam states 10.51.46:

*labdhvā jano durlabham atra mānuṣaṁ kathañcid avyaṇgam ayatnato ’nagha pādāravindaḥ na bhajaty asan-matir gṛhāndha-kūpe patito yathā paśuḥ (1.77)*

**Перевод:**

**That person has an impure mind who, despite having somehow or other automatically obtained the rare and highly evolved human form of life, does not worship Your lotus feet. Like an animal that has fallen into a blind well, such a person has fallen into the darkness of a material home.**

*kṛṣṇa kathādi vimukhajana nindi atisaya, socyāti socya sei bhāgavate kaya (1.78)*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are averse to discussing the topics of Kṛṣṇa are declared in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam to be condemned and most degraded.**

Vidura in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam 3.5.14 states:

*tāñ chocya-śocyān avido ’nuśoce hareḥ kathāyāṁ vimukhān aghena kṣiṇoti devo ’nimiṣas tu yeṣām āyur vṛthā-vāda-gati-smṛtīnām (1.79)*

**Перевод:**

**O sage, persons who because of their sinful activities are averse to the topics of Transcendence and thus ignorant of the purpose of the Mahābhārata \[Bhagavad-gītā\] are pitied by the pitiable. I also pity them because I see how their duration of life is spoiled by eternal time while they involve themselves in presentations of philosophical speculation, theoretical ultimate goals of life, and different modes of ritual.**

*durlabha manuṣyadeha pāiyā ye rājana, śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇāravinda āśraya nā hona sei jana socya ati ātmāra vañcaka , adhogati haya tāra śuna prabhura śloka (1.80)*

**Перевод:**

**If one does not take shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī kṛṣṇa even after receiving the rare human form of life, O King, is certainly condemned, degraded and the killer of the self. Just listen to prabhu’s (Lord Śiva’s) śloka :**

The statement of Lord Śiva to Lord Kṛṣṇa found in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam 10.63.41 supports this principle:

*deva-dattam imaṁ labdhvā nṛ-lokam ajitendriyaḥ yo nādriyeta tvat-pādau sa śocyo hy ātma-vañcakaḥ (1.81)*

**Перевод:**

**One who has attained this human form of life as a gift from God, yet who fails to control his senses and honor Your feet, is surely to be pitied, for he is only cheating himself.**

*ātma vañcaka śocya sei sava haya, kṛṣṇa kathā tyāga kari anya kathā kaya (1.82)*

**Перевод:**

**Those who reject the topics of Kṛṣṇa and indulge in topics unrelated to Kṛṣṇa are fallen and are cheating themselves.**

*tāhā sabhāra adhogati e-hena niścaya, kṛṣṇa yaśa kīrtana bine anya kathā kaya (1.83)*

**Перевод:**

**Due to indulging in gossip rather then in glorifying Kṛṣṇa, such people certainly degrade.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam 3.15.23 states:

*yan na vrajanty agha-bhido racanānuvādāc chṛṇvanti ye ’nya-viṣayāḥ kukathā mati-ghnīḥ yās tu śrutā hata-bhagair nṛbhir ātta-sārās tāṁs tān kṣipanty aśaraṇeṣu tamaḥsu hanta*

**Перевод:**

**It is very much regrettable that unfortunate people do not discuss the description of the Vaikuṇṭha planets but engage in topics which are unworthy to hear and which bewilder one’s intelligence. Those who give up the topics of Vaikuṇṭha and take to talk of the material world are thrown into the darkest region of ignorance. (1.84)**

*kṛṣṇa bhakti phala śuna apāra mahimā, bhāgavate brahmā ukti daśame barṇanā (1.85)*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear the unlimited glories of the results of devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. Lord brahmā prays to Lord Kṛṣṇa in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam.**

*ei bhava sindhu ati duṣṭara apāra, yāhāte aneka ripu āche durācara (1.86)*

**Перевод:**

**This ocean of material existence is formidable and insurmountable where various fierce enemies are present.**

*kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇa sindhu tare anāyāse, bhaktera duṣkṛtī kṛṣṇa kṛpāvale nāse (1.87)*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of Kṛṣṇa easily cross the ocean of material existence. Kṛṣṇa destroys all the sinful reactions of His devotees though His mercy.**

*ye āśrena śrī kṛṣṇera pāda padma bhelā, se pāya parama pada anye kari helā (1.88)*

**Перевод:**

**One who takes shelter of the boat-like lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa can actually attain the supreme destination by surpassing all other temporary insignificant goals.**

*tāhāra parampada vipada nā haya, kṛṣṇa ye nāśraye tāra vipada niścaya (1.89)*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme abode of the lord is devoid of any danger. But one who does not surrender to the lord, certainly faces great dangers.**

Śrīmad Bhāgavatam 10.14.58 states:

*samāśritā ye pada-pallava-plavaṁ mahat-padaṁ puṇya-yaśo murāreḥ bhavāmbudhir vatsa-padaṁ paraṁ padaṁ padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadāṁ na teṣām (1.90)*

**Перевод:**

**“For one who has accepted the boat of the lotus feet of the Lord, who is the shelter of the cosmic manifestation and is famous as Mukunda, or the giver of mukti, the ocean of the material world is like the water contained in a calf’s footprint. paraṁ padam, or the place where there are no material miseries, or Vaikuṇṭha, is his goal, not the place where there is danger in every step of life.”**

*ambhuja locana kṛṣṇa akhila saṁsthāna, sei pāda padma sadā yevā kare dhyāna, kṛṣṇa pāda kari bhelā sindhu tari jāya, ei bhava sindhu jāni vatsa pāda prāya (1.91)*

**Перевод:**

**The lotus-eyed lord Kṛṣṇa is the support of the entire creation. Anyone who always remembers His lotus feet easily cross beyond the ocean of material existence by the help of His boat-like lotus feet. Such a person considers this material ocean as a hoof-print of a calf.**

*śrī kṛṣṇa caritra yaśe jagata pavitra, hena cariba nāhi dekhi śuni yatra (1.92)*

**Перевод:**

**The glorious characteristics of Kṛṣṇa purify the entire world. Such characteristics are not found or heard in anyone else.**

*yadyapi apūba kathā dharmādi varṇana, kṛṣṇa yaśa binā sei kaka tīrtha sama (1.93)*

**Перевод:**

**Although the descriptions about the religious principles are matchless, yet if they are devoid of the descriptions of Kṛṣṇa’s characteristics then it is like the crow in a holy place.**

This is confirmed in the statement of Nārada muni to Śrī Vyāsadeva found in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam 10.2.30,

*tvayy ambujākṣākhila-sattva-dhāmni samādhināveśita-cetasaike tvat-pāda-potena mahat-kṛtena kurvanti govatsa-padaṁ bhavābdhim (1.94)*

**Перевод:**

**O lotus-eyed Lord, by concentrating one’s meditation on Your lotus feet, which are the reservoir of all existence, and by accepting those lotus feet as the boat by which to cross the ocean of nescience, one follows in the footsteps of mahājanas \[great saints, sages and devotees\]. By this simple process, one can cross the ocean of nescience as easily as one steps over the hoofprint of a calf.**

as well as in the statement of Śrī Śukadeva Gosvāmī in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam 12.4.40 as follows:

*saṁsāra-sindhum ati-duṣṭaram uttitīrṣor nānyaḥ plavo bhāgavataḥ puruṣottamasya līlā-kathā-rasa-niṣevaṇam antareṇa puṁso bhaved vividha-duḥkha-davārditasya (1.95)*

**Перевод:**

**For a person who is suffering in the fire of countless miseries and who desires to cross the insurmountable ocean of material existence, there is no suitable boat except that of cultivating devotion to the transcendental taste for the narration of the Supreme Personality of Godhead’s pastimes.**

*pṛth'ivīte janmiyā jārā bhaje bhagavāna, tānhāra kṛtārtha jāṇrā kṛṣṇa nāma gāṇa (1.96)*

**Перевод:**

**Those who worship Kṛṣṇa and chant His holy names in this world are glorious.**

Vāmana Bharadvājiya tantra states:

*“pṛthivyāṁ kati vā lokā nā jātā kati vā mṛtaḥ muktāste tu nā sandeho ye harer nāma kirtakāh” (1.97)*

**Перевод:**

**“In this material world people are taking birth and are dying, but those who chant the holy names of Hari certainly become liberated. There is no doubt about it.”**

*ghora kali-yuge sarva dharma naṣṭajana sei kṛtārtha jihon kṛṣṇa parāyaṇā (1.98)*

**Перевод:**

**In this terrible age of Kali people are generally devoid of religious principles, but glorious are those who are devoted to Lord Kṛṣṇa.**

śāstra states:

*“ghore kali-yuge prāpte sarvadharma vivarjite vāsudevaparā martāste kṛtārthā nā saṁśaya” (1.99)*

**Перевод:**

**“All religious principles are absent in the age of Kali, but people of this world who are devotees of Vāsudeva are undoubtedly glorious.”**

*harināma saṅkīrtana parāyaṇā jārā, hari pūjā tatpara kali yuge jārā tāhāra kṛtārtha haya kali-yuge jāni, hari binā kali-yuge gati nāhi jāni (1.100)*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are attached to the chanting of the holy names of Hari and are engaged in worshipping Him in this age of Kali actually attain perfection. There is no alternative for deliverance in this age of Kali other then Hari.**

Bṛhad Nāradīya Purāṇa states:

*harināma parā ye ca hari kīrtana tatparā hari pūjā parā ye ca te kṛtārthā kalau yuge 1.101)*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are attached to chanting the holy names, glorifying the topics and engaged in worshipping Lord Hari in this age of Kali are the most glorious persons.**

*sarvārādhya bhagavānera pūjya sabhākāra, kṛṣṇa sama devatā nā dekhe keha āra (1.102)*

**Перевод:**

**There is no one superior then, or equal to, Lord Kṛṣṇa. He is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and worshipable by all.**

*vedera apara kona śāstra nāhi āra kṛṣṇa sama deva nāhi ei sārrodhāra (1.103)*

**Перевод:**

**There is no literature equal to the Vedas. There is no lord equal to Lord Kṛṣṇa. This is the essence of the truth.**

Varāha purāṇa states:

*‘ratanti hy purāṇāni bhūyo bhūyo varānane nāsti vedāt paraṁ śāstram nā deva keśavat paraḥ (1.104)*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful one, it is stated in abundance in the purāṇas that there is no literature superior to the vedas and there is no lord superior to Keśava.**

*tāra janma biphala manuṣya janma pāiyā govinda ye narādhila āpanā vañciyā (1.105)*

**Перевод:**

**One who has received the human form of life yet did not engage in worshipping Govinda, his life is useless and thus he has simply cheated himself.**

Brahmā vaivarta purāṇa states:

*tadāpi aphalatāṁ jātaṁ tesāṁ dehābhimānināṁ varākāṇām anāśṛitya govinda caraṇāmbujaṁ (1.106)*

**Перевод:**

**The life of a person who identifies his body as his self and does not take shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī Govinda is useless.**

*hena kṛṣṇa chāḍi jevā anya deva bhaje gangājala tyāga kari kupajale maje (1.107)*

*gangātire tṛṣṇārtā haiyā jalera lāgiyā muḍha loka jala khāi kupake khania (1.108)*

*ataeva krsna pada samsraya kara tnha bahi keha nahi sevaka va sie padma locana bine samsara sagare dukhira dukha ghucaite ara*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore one should take shelter of the lotus feet of Krsna. There is none else more affectionate towards His servants then Him. Only the lotus eyed Lord is able to vanquish the miseries of material existence.**

*suniti kahila dhruve ei katha sara haribine dukhachetta na keha ar*

**Перевод:**

**Suniti the mother of Dhruva Maharaja spoke to her son that only Lord Hari and none else is capable of destroy one’s miseries. As found in SB (4.8.23)**

*nānyaṁ tataḥ padma-palāśa-locanād duḥkha-cchidaṁ te mṛgayāmi kañcana yo mṛgyate hasta-gṛhīta-padmayā śriyetarair aṅga vimṛgyamāṇayā*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Dhruva, as far as I am concerned, I do not find anyone who can mitigate your distress but the Supreme Personality of Godhead, whose eyes are like lotus petals. Many demigods such as Lord Brahmā seek the pleasure of the goddess of fortune, but the goddess of fortune herself, with a lotus flower in her hand, is always ready to render service to the Supreme Lord.**

*ke ache emana jani dayamaya ara hena prabhu chadiya saram nibe tara harisama dayamaya kanhu nahi dekhi purane bekata tara putana saksi*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore after knowing about such a merciful Lord, who will take shelter of someone else? One cannot find anyone more merciful then Lord Hari for this is confirmed in SB by the incident of Putana.**

*aribhave nasta karite gela jani sisubuddhe krsnake karila kale ani bisa stana dila mukhe maribara tare stanapana kari hari badhilena tare*

**Перевод:**

**Putana same as an enemy to kill baby Krsna. She took Him on her lap and feed Him her brest which was smeared with poison, but Lord Krsna drank her breast milk and killed her.**

*ante taya gati suna balite bismaya matrigati dila krsna tahat niscaya kale kari anganate paila matrigati emana dayala prabhuara paba kati*

**Перевод:**

**Please hear about her amazing fate. She was awarded the position as the Lord’s mother. She achieved the position of a mother simply by taking Krsna on her lap in the courtyard of the Lord’s house. Where can one find such a merciful Lord?**

*sneha kari anuksane jiva seve tare tahara vidhan purna ke kahite ...*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, if one serves Him with affection, who can describe his good fortune?**

This is confirmed in SB (3.2.23)

*aho bakī yaṁ stana-kāla-kūṭaṁ jighāṁsayāpāyayad apy asādhvī lebhe gatiṁ dhātry-ucitāṁ tato ’nyaṁ kaṁ vā dayāluṁ śaraṇaṁ vrajema*

**Перевод:**

**Alas, how shall I take shelter of one more merciful than He who granted the position of mother to a she-demon \[Pūtanā\] although she was unfaithful and she prepared deadly poison to be sucked from her breast?**

*ki kahiba sei prabhura namera mahima namabhase mukta haya ei jani sima*

**Перевод:**

**How can I describe the glories of the holy names of the Lord? One is certainly liberated by chanting the holy names of the Lord even on the stage of namabhasa or reflection of the holy names.**

*sanketa rahasya chale stabha krame jani helate va kana jana krsna nama suni atita haiya va vakyera skhalane astradite biddha haiya karaye smaram sarpadira bhaye kimba avasa haiya namabhase laya nama vastu na bujhiya tathapi tahate nahi yama ashikara ye laya amara prabhura nama ekabara*

**Перевод:**

**If one chants and hears the holy names of Krsna even once with an intimation jo... on some pretex, through deceptive words of consolation or woth negligence, or remembers Krsna being fallen slipping of words or being piearced by weapon or due to the fear of snakes etc. in an uncoscious stage or chants the holy names without understanding the purport of the matter is freed from the control of Yamaraja.**

It is stated in SB (6.2.14-15)

*sāṅketyaṁ pārihāsyaṁ vā stobhaṁ helanam eva vā vaikuṇṭha-nāma-grahaṇam aśeṣāgha-haraṁ viduḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who chants the holy name of the Lord is immediately freed from the reactions of unlimited sins, even if he chants indirectly \[to indicate something else\], jokingly, for musical entertainment, or even neglectfully. This is accepted by all the learned scholars of the scriptures.**

*patitaḥ skhalito bhagnaḥ sandaṣṭas tapta āhataḥ harir ity avaśenāha pumān nārhati yātanāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**If one chants the holy name of Hari and then dies because of an accidental misfortune, such as falling from the top of a house, slipping and suffering broken bones while traveling on the road, being bitten by a serpent, being afflicted with pain and high fever, or being injured by a weapon, one is immediately absolved from having to enter hellish life, even though he is sinful.**

*apanate muktyabhimani jani jiva jana krsna pada padmayuge mata hina haya seha yadi bahu dukhe uchhapada paya na bhajiya krsna pada adhapada jaya*

**Перевод:**

**If someone out of pride considers himself a liberated soul and thus forgets the lotus feet of Krsna he certainly falls down due to not worshipping the lotus feet of Krsna. Though such person advances to a higher position by dint of their austerities nevertheless they degrade.**

*anyera sarane nahi bhavabdhi tarana ataeva anya sevaya kiba praya*

**Перевод:**

**One cannot cross beyond the ocean of material existence by taking shelter of anyone else. Then what is the need of serving anyone else?**

*ei bhava sindhu tare krsna bhakta gana abhakta grihandhakupe sada padya rana*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of Krsna easily surpass this ocean of material existence where as nondevotees remain fallen in the dark well of material life.**

*kupeta padile pasura na haya uddhara krsna ye na bhaje loka taiche jani tara*

**Перевод:**

**Just as an animal cannot be delivered if fallen in a well, similarly if one does not worships Krsna meets the same fate.**

In SB (10.2.32)

*ye ’nye ’ravindākṣa vimukta-māninas tvayy asta-bhāvād aviśuddha-buddhayaḥ āruhya kṛcchreṇa paraṁ padaṁ tataḥ patanty adho ’nādṛta-yuṣmad-aṅghrayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**\[Someone may say that aside from devotees, who always seek shelter at the Lord’s lotus feet, there are those who are not devotees but who have accepted different processes for attaining salvation. What happens to them? In answer to this question, Lord Brahmā and the other demigods said:\] O lotus-eyed Lord, although nondevotees who accept severe austerities and penances to achieve the highest position may think themselves liberated, their intelligence is impure. They fall down from their position of imagined superiority because they have no regard for Your lotus feet.**

*ataeva bhagavate kahe deva gana krsna bine bhava sindhu na haya tarana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore the demigods state in SB that without the mercy of Krsna one cannot be liberated from material existence.**

*paripurna kama krsna santa dayamaya tanra padasraya kaile sarva siddhi haya*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna is self satisfied peaceful and full of compassion. If one takes shelter of His lotus feet achieves all perfections.**

*hena krsna chadiya anyera kare asa samsara samudra para haite bharasa jahaja kariya tyaga sindhu taribare bahubale tariba sindhu hena citte kare*

**Перевод:**

**People abandon such a merciful Lord Krsna and keep faith on others to help cross the material ocean. They give up the ship and think that by their own strength they will cross the ocean of material existence.**

*svalangula dhariya jaya sinshu raibare na pare tarite atma ghati haiya mare*

**Перевод:**

**They wont to cross the ocean by holding a dog’s tail but they fail and in the process kill themselves.**

*kukura haite sinchu jena nahe para ataeva anyasraye nahika uddhara*

**Перевод:**

**As one cannot cross the ocean with the help of a dog, so one cannot be delivered if he will take shelter of somebody else.**

*krsnarita bine ei samsara sagara para nahi haya keha kahila nirbhara*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore take it for granted that unless one takes shelter of Krsna, he is not delivered from ocean of material existence.**

This is confirmed in SB:

*śrī-devā ūcuḥ vāyv-ambarāgny-ap-kṣitayas tri-lokā brahmādayo ye vayam udvijantaḥ harāma yasmai balim antako ’sau bibheti yasmād araṇaṁ tato naḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The demigods said: The three worlds are created by the five elements—namely ether, air, fire, water and earth—which are controlled by various demigods, beginning from Lord Brahmā. Being very much afraid that the time factor will end our existence, we offer presentations unto time by performing our work as time dictates. The time factor himself, however, is afraid of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Therefore let us now worship that Supreme Lord, who alone can give us full protection.(6.9.21)**

*ajñānād athavā jñānād uttamaśloka-nāma yat saṅkīrtitam aghaṁ puṁso dahed edho yathānalaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**As a fire burns dry grass to ashes, so the holy name of the Lord, whether chanted knowingly or unknowingly, burns to ashes, without fail, all the reactions of one’s sinful activities.(6.2.18)**

*labdhvā jano durlabham atra mānuṣaṁ kathañcid avyaṅgam ayatnato ’nagha pādāravindaṁ na bhajaty asan-matir gṛhāndha-kūpe patito yathā paśuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**That person has an impure mind who, despite having somehow or other automatically obtained the rare and highly evolved human form of life, does not worship Your lotus feet. Like an animal that has fallen into a blind well, such a person has fallen into the darkness of a material home.(10.51.46)**

*krsna kathadi vimukha jana nandi atisaya socyati socya sei bhagavate kaya*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are averse to discussing the topics of Krsna are declared by SB as condemned and most degraded.**

This is confirmed in SB:

*tāñ chocya-śocyān avido ’nuśoce hareḥ kathāyāṁ vimukhān aghena kṣiṇoti devo ’nimiṣas tu yeṣām āyur vṛthā-vāda-gati-smṛtīnām*

**Перевод:**

**O sage, persons who because of their sinful activities are averse to the topics of Transcendence and thus ignorant of the purpose of the Mahābhārata \[Bhagavad-gītā\] are pitied by the pitiable. I also pity them because I see how their duration of life is spoiled by eternal time while they involve themselves in presentations of philosophical speculation, theoretical ultimate goals of life, and different modes of ritual. (3.5.14)**

*durlabha manusya deha paiya ye rajana sri krsna caranaravinda asraya na hana sei jana socya ati atmara vancaka adhogati haya tara suna prabhura sloka*

**Перевод:**

**If one does not take shelter of the lotus feet of Sri Krsna even after receiving the rare human form of life, o King, is certainly condemed, degraded and killer of the self.**

The statement of Lord Siva toward Lord Krsna found in SB (10.63.41) supports this principle.

*deva-dattam imaṁ labdhvā nṛ-lokam ajitendriyaḥ yo nādriyeta tvat-pādau sa śocyo hy ātma-vañcakaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who has attained this human form of life as a gift from God, yet who fails to control his senses and honor Your feet, is surely to be pitied, for he is only cheating himself.**

*atma vancaka socya sei seva haya krsna katha tyaga hati anya katha kaya*

**Перевод:**

**Those who reject the topics of Krsna and indulges in topics unrelated to Krsna are fallen and cheating themselves.**

*taha sabhara adhogati e hena niscaya krsna yasa kirtana bine anya katha kaya*

**Перевод:**

**Due to indulging in gossip rather then glorifying Krsna such people certainly degrade.**

It is stated in SB (3.15.23):

*eṣā ghoratamā velā ghorāṇāṁ ghora-darśanā caranti yasyāṁ bhūtāni bhūteśānucarāṇi ha*

**Перевод:**

**This particular time is most inauspicious because at this time the horrible-looking ghosts and constant companions of the lord of the ghosts are visible.**

*krsna bhakti phala suna apara mahima bhagavate brahma ukti dasame barnana*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear the unlimited glories of the results of devotional service to Krsna. Lord Brahma prays to Lord Krsna in SB**

*ei bhava sindhu ati dustara apara yahate aneka ripu ache duracara*

**Перевод:**

**This ocean of material existence is formidable and insurmount... wherein various fierce enemies are present.**

*krsna bhakta gana sindhu tare anayase bhaktera duskriti krsna kripavale nase*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of Krsna easily cross the ocean of material existence. Krsna destroys all the sinful reactions of His devotees through His mercy.**

*ye asrena sri krsnera pada padma bhala se paya parama pada anye kari hela*

**Перевод:**

**One who takes shelter of the boat like lotus feet of Krsna can actually attain the supreme destination by surpassing all other temporary insignificant goals.**

*tahara paramapada vipada na haya krsna ye nasraye tara vipada niscaya*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme abode of the Lord is devoid of any danger. But one who does not surrunder to the Lord however certainly face great dangers.**

It is mentioned in SB (10.14.58):

*samāśritā ye pada-pallava-plavaṁ mahat-padaṁ puṇya-yaśo murāreḥ bhavāmbudhir vatsa-padaṁ paraṁ padaṁ padaṁ padaṁ yad vipadāṁ na teṣām*

**Перевод:**

**For those who have accepted the boat of the lotus feet of the Lord, who is the shelter of the cosmic manifestation and is famous as Murāri, the enemy of the Mura demon, the ocean of the material world is like the water contained in a calf’s hoof-print. Their goal is paraṁ padam, Vaikuṇṭha, the place where there are no material miseries, not the place where there is danger at every step.**

*ambuja locana krsna akhila sansthana sei pada padma sada yeva kare dhana Krsna pada kari bhela sindhu tari jaya ei bhava sindhu jani vatsa pada praya*

**Перевод:**

**The lotus eyed Lord Krsna is the support of the entire creation anyone who aleays remembers His lotus feet easily cross beyond the ocean of material existence by the help of His boat like lotus feet. Such a person considers this material ocean as a hoof print of a calf.**

*sri krsna carita yase jagata pavitra hena caritra nahi dekhi suni yatra*

**Перевод:**

**The glorious characterictics of Krsna purfy the entire world. Such characteristics are not found or heard in anyone else.**

*yadyapi apuvba katha dharmadi varnana krsna yasa bina sei kaka tirtha sama*

**Перевод:**

**Although the descriptions about the religious principles are matchless yet if they are devoid of the description of Krsna’s characteristics it is like the crow in a holy place.**

This is confirmed in the statement of Narada Muni to Sri Vyasadeva found in SB (10.2.30) as well as in the statement of Sri Sukadeva Goswami in SB (12.4.40):

*tvayy ambujākṣākhila-sattva-dhāmni samādhināveśita-cetasaike tvat-pāda-potena mahat-kṛtena kurvanti govatsa-padaṁ bhavābdhim*

**Перевод:**

**O lotus-eyed Lord, by concentrating one’s meditation on Your lotus feet, which are the reservoir of all existence, and by accepting those lotus feet as the boat by which to cross the ocean of nescience, one follows in the footsteps of mahājanas \[great saints, sages and devotees\]. By this simple process, one can cross the ocean of nescience as easily as one steps over the hoofprint of a calf.**

*saṁsāra-sindhum ati-dustaram uttitīrṣor nānyaḥ plavo bhagavataḥ puruṣottamasya līlā-kathā-rasa-niṣevaṇam antareṇa puṁso bhaved vividha-duḥkha-davārditasya*

**Перевод:**

**For a person who is suffering in the fire of countless miseries and who desires to cross the insurmountable ocean of material existence, there is no suitable boat except that of cultivating devotion to the transcendental taste for the narrations of the Supreme Personality of Godhead’s pastimes.**

*prithibite janmiya jara bhaje bhagavana tanhara kritartha janra krsna nama gana*

**Перевод:**

**Those who worship Krsna and chant His holy names in this world are glorious.**

As stated in Vamana Bhardvajiya tantra as follows:

*prithivyam kati va loka na jata kati va mrtah muktaste tu na sandeha ye harera nama kirtakah*

**Перевод:**

**In this material world people are taking birth and dying, but those who chant the holy names of Hari certainly become liberated. There is no doubt about it.**

*ghora kali yuge sarva dharma nastajana sei se kritartha jihon krsna parayana*

**Перевод:**

**In this terrible age of Kali people are generally devoid of religious principles, but glorious are those who are devoted to Lord Krsna.**

This is confirmed in the scripture as follows:

*ghore kaliyuge prapte sarvadharma vivarjite vasudeva para martaste kritartha na samsaya*

**Перевод:**

**All religious principles are absent in the age of Kali. But people of this world who are devotees of Vasudeva are undoubtedly glorious .**

*harinama sankirtan parayana jara hari puja tatpara kaliyuge jara tahara kritartha haya kaliyuge jani hai bina kaliyuge gati nahi jani*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are attached to the chanting of the holy names of Hari and are engaged in worshiping Him in this age of Kali actually attain perfaction there is no alternative for deliverance in this age of Kali other then Hari.**

It is described in Brhat Naradia Purana as follows:

*harinama para ye ca hari kirtana tatpara haripuja para ye ca te kirtartha kalau yuge*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are attached to chanting the holi names glorifying the topics of Hari and engaged in worshiping Him in this age of Kali are the most glorious persons.**

*sarvaradhya bhagavana pujiya sabhakara krsna sama devata nadekhe keha ara*

**Перевод:**

**There is no one superior or equal to Lord Krsna. He is the supreme personality of Godhead and worshipable by all.**

*vedera apara kona sastra nahi ara krsna sama deva nahi ei saroddhara*

**Перевод:**

**There is no literature equal to the vedas and there is no Lord wqual to Lord Krsna. This is the essence of the truth.**

It is found in Varaha Purana as follows:

*ratanti hy puranani bhuyo bhuyo varanane nasti vedat param sastram na deva kesavat parah*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful one, it is stated in abundance in the Puranas that there is no literature superior to the vedas and there is no Lord superior to Kesava**

*tara janma biphala manusya janma paiya govinda ye naradhila apana vanciya*

**Перевод:**

**One who has received the human form of life did not engage in worshipping Govinda, his life is useless and thus he has simply cheated himself.**

This is confirmed in Brahma Vaivarta Purana as follows:

*tadapi aphalatam yatam dehabhimaninam varakanam anasritya govinda caranambujam*

**Перевод:**

**The life of a person who identifies his body as self and does not take shelter of the lotus feet of Sri Govinda is useless.**

Furthermore it is described by Dhruva Maharaja in Skanda Purana as follows (has to be translated):

*hena krsna chadi jiva anya deva bhaje ganga jala tyaga kari kupajale maje*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who rejects the worship of such a personality like Krsna is compared with a person who rejects the water of the ganges and takes pleasure in the water of the well.**

*ganga tire trisnarta haiya jalera lagiya mudha loka jala khai kupake khaniya*

**Перевод:**

**Such a foolish thirsty person sits on the bank of the ganges but desire a well to drink water.**

This is mentioned in Mahabharata as follows:

*vasudeva parityajya ya anyam devamupasate trisita jahnavi tire kupam khanati durmati*

**Перевод:**

**A person who gives up the worship of vasudeva and engages in the worship of the demigods is similar to a thirsty man who sits on the bank of the ganges yet dings a well to quench his thirst.**

More ever in the conversation between Lord Brahma and Narada in Skanda Purana it is stated that

*vasudevam parityajya ya anyam devam upasate tyakta amrtam sa mudhtma bhunkte halahalam visam*

**Перевод:**

**One who abandons Vasudeva and worships other demigods is certainly foolish. He simply rejects nectar and drinks poison.**

*Keha kahe kara ithe devata nindana kintu ninda nahe sastre svarupa kathana*

**Перевод:**

**Someone may argue that you are criticizing the demigods. But this is not a criticism but facts based on the sastras.**

*sarva radhya bhagavan sabhara bhajana kinty kona devatara na kari nindana*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme Lord is worshipable for everyone. But we do not blaspheme the demigods on any way.**

*avajna na kari kara na kari nindana mahatera ninda haya naraka karana*

**Перевод:**

**We do not intend to disrespect or blaspheme anyone, for blaspheme the great souls cuses one to go to hell.**

*samanya manusya ninda hayata visama devatara ninda kare kona naradham*

**Перевод:**

**Even blaspheming an ordinary man is dangerous. Then which fallen soul will dare to blaspheme the demigods?**

*svarupa kathane jani ninda nahi haya sarva sastre kahe iha vishnu sarvasraya*

**Перевод:**

**There is no harm or offense in speaking the conclusion of the sastras. All scriptures establish Lord Visnu as the ultimate shelter of everyone.**

*visnu sama kahi yadi anya deva gane paramartha ‘cyuta hona sastrera sasane*

**Перевод:**

**If one considers the demigods to be the same level with the Lord Visnu then according to the injunctions of the sastra such a person is considered fallen from spiritual life.**

This is confirmed by the statement of Parvati towards Lord Siva found in Skanda Purana as follows:

*aho sarvesvara visnu sarva devottamottamah bhavadadi guru mudhai samanyamiva laksyate*

**Перевод:**

**Alas he is a fool! Who considers Visnu the Lord of Lords, greater then then the greatest demigods is equal to the demigods headed by Lord Siva.**

*trigunatmaka samabhaava kaha kona jana brahma visnu mahesvara sama tina guna*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahma Lord Visnu and Lord Siva are the three predominating deities of the three material modes.**

*sattva rajah tamah haya prakritira guna gunasrita haiya purusa tina rupa hona*

**Перевод:**

**The three modes of material nature are goodness, passion and ignorance. The purusa incarnation of the supreme Lord takes these forms such as Brahma, Visnu and Siva to control these modes.**

*sthityadi nimitta hana purusa tin murti palana samhara sristi tina rupa gati*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme Lord manifests as three purusa incarnations to create maintain and anihilate this cosmic manifestation.**

*palane se visnu rupa sristyeprajapati rudra rupe samhara karaye sarva ksiti*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme lord maintains the entire creation as Lord Visnu, creates as Lord Brahma and annihilates as Lord Siva.**

*yadyapi se tina murti purusera hona tahatu sattva tanu srestha nirupana*

**Перевод:**

**Although these three features belong to the purusa incarnation yet Lord Visnu or the predominating deity of goodness is established as the topmost.**

*sattva tanu vasudeva kalyana dayaka manusyera sreya hetu sei se nayaka*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Vasudeva is the imbodiment of pure goodness and all auspicious. He is the cause of the living entities ultimate benefit.**

*vasudeva bine dekha mukti nahi haya jnana sadhya mukti pada kahila niscaya*

**Перевод:**

**Without reaching the platform of vasudeva or pure goodness one cannot attain libaration. The platform of libaration is subjected to knowledge.**

*sattvat samjayate jnanam gitaya kahila jnanera sulabha mukti sastre nirupila*

**Перевод:**

**It is stated in BG that one becomes knowledgeble when he comes to the platform of goodness. Therefore libaration is dependent on aquiring knowledge. This is the verdict of the sastra.**

This is confirmed in SB 1.2.23:

*sattvaṁ rajas tama iti prakṛter guṇās tair yuktaḥ paraḥ puruṣa eka ihāsya dhatte sthity-ādaye hari-viriñci-hareti saṁjñāḥ śreyāṁsi tatra khalu sattva-tanor nṛṇāṁ syuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The transcendental Personality of Godhead is indirectly associated with the three modes of material nature, namely passion, goodness and ignorance, and just for the material world’s creation, maintenance and destruction He accepts the three qualitative forms of Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva. Of these three, all human beings can derive ultimate benefit from Viṣṇu, the form of the quality of goodness.**

*sattvatanu visnudeva sreyah sabhakara prakriti jata nahe visnura avatara*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Visnu is the imbodiment of pure goodness and the best of all the incarnations of Lord Visnu are not products of matter.**

*nirguna prakritira para visnudeva hana tanre yeva bhaje seha hayata nirguna*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Visnu is transcendental to the three modes of material nature. He is situated beyond the material creation. Whoever worships Him certainly becomes transcendental.**

As described in SB 10.88.5:

*harir hi nirguṇaḥ sākṣāt puruṣaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ sa sarva-dṛg upadraṣṭā taṁ bhajan nirguṇo bhavet*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Hari, however, has no connection with the material modes. He is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, the all-seeing eternal witness, who is transcendental to material nature. One who worships Him becomes similarly free from the material modes.**

*sattvike bistara kari sattva tanu hana prakrijata visnura murti kabhu nana*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Visnu appears in the platform of pure goodness by His own will and not a product of this material world.**

*ataeva bhagavana kahila arjune sattva rajah tawah bhava e tina bidhane*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore the supreme Lord had instructed Arjuna about the three modes of material nature.**

*ama haite sei tina bhavera utpanna amara kakhana naya prakritira janma*

**Перевод:**

**The three modes of material nature are born from me and I am not born from them.**

*tina bhave ami nai mora vasa tina ataeva tinaguna amara adhina*

**Перевод:**

**I am not present in the three material modes rather they are controlled by me. Therefore these modes are subordinated to me.**

This is confirmed by the Lord in BG 7.12:

*ye caiva sāttvikā bhāvā rājasās tāmasāś ca ye matta eveti tān viddhi na tv ahaṁ teṣu te mayi*

**Перевод:**

**Know that all states of being—be they of goodness, passion or ignorance—are manifested by My energy. I am, in one sense, everything, but I am independent. I am not under the modes of material nature, for they, on the contrary, are within Me.**

*prakritira gunayuta brahma surapati rajogune sristikarta e tina jagati*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahma the best of the demigods is under the modes of material nature. He creates the three worlds through the mode of passion.**

*narayanera nabhi padme brahmara janama janna mrtyu che tara sastrete niyam*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahma was born from the lotus that had sprouted from the navel of Lord Narayana. According to the scriptures Lord Brahma is subjected to birth and death.**

*daivi ekattara yuge eka manvantara eka indra patana haya ihara bhitara*

**Перевод:**

**The duration of a Manu is about seventy one times four yugas. During the period of each Manu a new Indra the King of heaven is born.**

*cauddya indra patana haya brahmara divase tatakala punah brahmara ratrera prakase.*

**Перевод:**

**In each day of Brahma fourteen Indras are born. The same is duration of Brahma’s night.**

*brahmara ratrite haya pralaya upasthiti sarva jaya tahate kare sthiti*

**Перевод:**

**During the night of Brahma a partial annihilation takes place and all ingredients of creation are withdrawn.**

*eimate dina ratri paksa masa ganana tahara dvdasa mase batsara kalpana*

**Перевод:**

**In this way the days, nights, fortnights and months of Brahma are calculated. Twelve such months are one year of Brahma.**

*sata varsa brahmara ayu parimana maha visnura nisvase braham laya jana*

**Перевод:**

**The duration of Brahma’s life is one hundred years when Maha Visnu inhales Brahma is annihilated.**

*mahavisnura svase brahmanda aganita utpanna pralaya haiche nimese sata sata*

**Перевод:**

**Innumerable universes are being created and annihilated at every moment during the inhaling and exhaling of MahaVisnu**

*ataeva vinasi phala brahamadite dekhe avinasi visnupada svasata bali lekhe*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahma and other demigods are fit for destruction. Only Lord Visnu is indestucteble and eternal.**

*ataeva brahmadi deva nahe visnu sama visnu sam haya tanra avataragana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore Lord Brahma and other demigods are not equal to Lord Visnu. The incarnations of Visnu are equal to Lord Visnu.**

It is stated in Varaha Purana (has to be translated):

*bhagyavana jivaloka svadharama sadhane brahma pada paya sei nitha acarane*

**Перевод:**

**If a fortunate soul follows his occupational duties faithfull he becomes qualified to attain the position of Brahma.**

*sata janma svadharam nistha haiya bhagyavana sei jiva tabe jani brahma pada pana*

**Перевод:**

**If a fortunate soul firmly follows his occupational duties for one hundred years then he achieved the position of Brahma.**

*ei katha bhagavate kahila pancanana pracetas upakhyane ei vivarana*

This is described by Lord Siva in SB when he instructed the Pracetas.

*brahmatva paiya jiva punah more paya amara padavi paiya visnavake pai*

**Перевод:**

**After attaining the position of Brahma a living entity again achieves ... Then he becomes known as a vaisnava.**

*ami jei mata temata sei haya ataeva brahmadi sama visnu nahi haya*

**Перевод:**

**“He becomes as good as me” therefore the position of Lord Visnu is never be compared with that of the demigods.**

*visnu mantra japiya vaisnava mahesvara ataeva vaisnava pada haya paratpara*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Siva has become famous as a vaisnava by chanting Visnu mantra’s. So the position of a Vaisnava is the highest.**

Lord Siva speaks to the Pracetas in SB 4.24.29:

*sva-dharma-niṣṭhaḥ śata-janmabhiḥ pumān viriñcatām eti tataḥ paraṁ hi mām avyākṛtaṁ bhāgavato ’tha vaiṣṇavaṁ padaṁ yathāhaṁ vibudhāḥ kalātyaye*

**Перевод:**

**A person who executes his occupational duty properly for one hundred births becomes qualified to occupy the post of Brahmā, and if he becomes more qualified, he can approach Lord Śiva. A person who is directly surrendered to Lord Kṛṣṇa, or Viṣṇu, in unalloyed devotional service is immediately promoted to the spiritual planets. Lord Śiva and other demigods attain these planets after the destruction of this material world.**

*kona kalpe bhagyavana jiva brahma haye kona kalpe yadi punyakari nahi rahe tabe mahavisnu amsa brahmarupa hana rajogune ei visva karaye srijana*

**Перевод:**

**In some kalpas a fortunate living entities become Brahma but if no pious and suitable candidate is found is some kalpas then MahaVisnu in His plenary portion takes the form of Brahma and creates the universes through mode of passion.**

This is stated in Padma Purana:

*bhavet kvacit mahakalpe brahma jiva api upasangi kvacit atra maha visnur brahmattvam prati padyate*

**Перевод:**

**In some kalpa a living entity by virture of his piety attains the position of Brahma . However when no such qualified person available in any kalpa then Maha Visnu takes the position of Brahma.**

*rudra hana ekadasa tamoguna yuta brahmara lalate janma sastrete likhita*

**Перевод:**

**There are eleven Rudras who are controllers of the mode of ignorance. It is written in the scriptures that Rudra was born from the between eyebrows of Brahma.**

*kona kalpe kalagni rudra samkarsanamsa haya samgare rahe to tihen tamaguna maya*

**Перевод:**

**In some kalpas sankarsana assumes the form of Kalagni Rudra since He destroys the universe He is the controller of the mode of ignorance.**

As stated in the scripture:

*vider lalatat janmasya kadacit kamala pateh kalagni rudrah kalpate bhave sankarsanadapi*

**Перевод:**

**Rudra is born from the forehead of Brahma. But seldom at the end of a Kalpa Lord Sankarsana appears a Kalagni Rudra.**

*sakti yukta siva hana gunatma apane kana kalpe samhara hetu haya tamagune*

**Перевод:**

**Actually Lord Siva who is endowed with great power is transcendental, but in some kalpa he mixed with mode of ignorance for the purpose of destruction.**

It is stated in SB 10.88.3

*śrī-śuka uvāca śivaḥ śakti-yutaḥ śaśvat tri-liṅgo guṇa-saṁvṛtaḥ vaikārikas taijasaś ca tāmasaś cety ahaṁ tridhā*

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Śukadeva said: Lord Śiva is always united with his personal energy, the material nature. Manifesting himself in three features in response to the entreaties of nature’s three modes, he thus embodies the threefold principle of material ego in goodness, passion and ignorance.**

*jihon gunatita hara sadasiva nama tamogandha rahita tihon sakale pradhana*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Siva who is also known as Hara and Sadasiva is person beyond the three modes of material nature. He is totally aloof from the mode of ignorance hence he is the greatest of all.**

*sri krsnera angarupa sei tanu hana brahma samhita granthe tahara vivarana*

**Перевод:**

**He is described in Brahma Samhita as a bodily limb of Lord Krsna**

In an explaintory stanza found in SB 10.88.3.it is mentioned (has to be translated):

*sadasiva murti hana sri krisnera tanu brahma samhitaya dekha kahiyachena puna*

**Перевод:**

**The form of Sadasiva is a bodily limb of Krsna. This is further confirmed in Brahma Samhita.**

Lord Śiva is described in the Brahma-saṁhitā (5.45):

*kṣīraṁ yathā dadhi vikāra-viśeṣa-yogāt sañjāyate na hi tataḥ pṛthag asti hetoḥ yaḥ śambhutām api tathā samupaiti kāryād govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi*

**Перевод:**

**“Milk changes into yogurt when mixed with a yogurt culture, but actually yogurt is constitutionally nothing but milk. Similarly, Govinda, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, assumes the form of Lord Śiva for the special purpose of material transactions. I offer my obeisances at Lord Govinda’s lotus feet.”**

*sei sadasiva dhama brahmanda upari goloka samipe adhah sadasiva puri*

**Перевод:**

**The abode of Sadasiva is situated above the material universe. It is near and below Goloka.**

*nitya satya sthana sei nahi tara ksaya nityananda sukha tanha nahika amaya*

**Перевод:**

**His abode is eternal, spiritual and inexhaustible. It is full of eternal happiness and devoid of any disease.**

It is mentioned in the tantra as follows (have to be translated):

*brahma rudra adi kari yata deva gana praya visnu deva haite gune nyuna hana*

**Перевод:**

**All the demigods such as Lord Brahma and Lord Siva are naturally inferior then Lord Visnu in qualities.**

*ataeva dekha kahe bhagavata purane mumuksu sava aradhila avatara gane*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore you can see that it is mentioned in SB and in the Puranas that persons who are desirous of liberation worships the incarnations of the Lord.**

*bhuta pati devaganera chadi aradhana narayanera santamsa kala karila sadhana*

**Перевод:**

**They abandoned the service of the demigods headed by Lord Siva and engaged in the service of Narayana or His plenary portions.**

*mukti data bhagavana visnu avatari ataeva mikti hetu bhajila tanhari*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Visnu alone is the bestower of liberation. That is why He is worshipped for obtaining liberation.**

As stated in SB 1.2.26:

*mumukṣavo ghora-rūpān hitvā bhūta-patīn atha nārāyaṇa-kalāḥ śāntā bhajanti hy anasūyavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are serious about liberation are certainly nonenvious, and they respect all. Yet they reject the horrible and ghastly forms of the demigods and worship only the all-blissful forms of Lord Viṣṇu and His plenary portions.**

*gunavatarete hana tina nirupam brahma visnu mahesvara sristadi karana*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahma Lord Visnu and Lord Siva are the three gunavataras of the Supreme Lord. They create, maintain and annihilate this entire cosmic manifestation.**

*purusu haite haya tinera utpatti yanhara nisvase haya brahmandera sthiti*

**Перевод:**

**These three qualitative incarnations are born from the purusa incarnation of Narayan. All theuniverses rest in the breathing air of this Purusa.**

*koti koti brahmanda kare jatayata jahara nisvasa pathe utpatti nipata*

**Перевод:**

**Millions of universes are emanating from and then entering into the body of Mahavisnu, the purusa incarnation through His breathing.**

*sei maha visnu hana brahmandera karana tihon hana govindera kalate ganana*

**Перевод:**

**This Maha Visnu is the cause of the universes. He is accepted as the portion of the plenary portion of Lord Govinda.**

*kalate sodasa bhaga kahila purane svayam rupa nanda suta bhaja vrndavane*

**Перевод:**

**It is mentioned in the Puranas that Govinda the son of Nanda and the Supreme personality of Godhead is complete. He is full of all opulences. One should worship Him in Vrndavana.**

As stated in the Brahma-saṁhitā (5.48):

*yasyaika-niśvasita-kālam athāvalambya jīvanti loma-vila-jā jagad-aṇḍa-nāthāḥ viṣṇur mahān sa iha yasya kalā-viśeṣo govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi*

**Перевод:**

**“The Mahā-Viṣṇu, into whom all the innumerable universes enter and from whom they come forth again simply by His breathing process, is a plenary expansion of Kṛṣṇa. Therefore I worship Govinda, Kṛṣṇa, the cause of all causes.”**

*brahmastuti bhagavate dasame varnana apanake dainya kari kariche stavana*

**Перевод:**

**With great humility Lord Brahma offered prayers to Lord Krsna, which are described in tenth canto of SB in the following way**

*O he prabhu kripamaya sri nanda nandana sarva bhave ami tomara lainu sarana*

**Перевод:**

**O merciful Lord, the son of Maharaja Nanda I take shelter of Your lotus feet by all respect.**

*tumi sarva avatari karanera karana sri stadi nimitt kara trirupa dharana*

**Перевод:**

**You are the source of all incarnations and the cause of all causes. You manifest in three different forms in order to create, maintain and annihilate.**

*brahma haiya kara tumi visvya utpatti palanarthe visnurupe jagate kara sthiti*

**Перевод:**

**You take the form of Lord Brahma and create the universe and in order to maintain you remain within the universe as Lord Visnu.**

*rudrarupa haiya kara jagat samhara ami ki janiba prabhu mahima tomara*

**Перевод:**

**You annihilate the universe in the form of Rudra. How can I know Your glories, o Lord.**

*mora dasa nivediye suna bhagavan tomara prabhave mora ei abhimana*

**Перевод:**

**O Supreme Personality of Godhead, please hear. I admit my fault my pride is due to the influence of Your illusory energy.**

*visvera sristi karta e kari garima tomara maya mugdha haiya pasari apana*

**Перевод:**

**I am very proud of being the creator of this universe. Being bewildered by Your illusory energy I forget my constitutional position.**

*bhautika deha mora mahadadi yuta pritivyapa teja vayu akasadigata*

**Перевод:**

**I possess a material body made of material elements such as earth, water, fire, air and ether.**

*sapta vitasti deha nija parimane svbechhamaya tumi prabhu kena toma jane*

**Перевод:**

**The measurement of my body is about ninty six fingers tall according to my fingers. But you are Supremely independent. Who can understand you?**

*ei brahmandera karta mora abhimana tomake na cini prabhu kari gopa jnana*

**Перевод:**

**I am puffed up because I am the creator of this universe. I can not recognize You. I considered You as an ordinaty cowherd boy.**

*emata brahmanda koti koti aganita lomavivare tomara kare gata yata*

**Перевод:**

**Inumerable such universes are created and wounded through the pore of Your skin.**

*gavaksa dvare jiva paramanu rupe brahmanda gana taiche tomara loma kupe*

**Перевод:**

**As the particles of atom found floating in the sunshine reflected through a window, similarly the universes are floating through the pores of Your Body.**

It is stated in SB 10.14.11:

*kvāhaṁ tamo-mahad-ahaṁ-kha-carāgni-vār-bhū-saṁveṣṭitāṇḍa-ghaṭa-sapta-vitasti-kāyaḥ kvedṛg-vidhāvigaṇitāṇḍa-parāṇu-caryā-vātādhva-roma-vivarasya ca te mahitvam*

**Перевод:**

**What am I, a small creature measuring seven spans of my own hand? I am enclosed in a potlike universe composed of material nature, the total material energy, false ego, ether, air, water and earth. And what is Your glory? Unlimited universes pass through the pores of Your body just as particles of dust pass through the openings of a screened window.**

*krsna mahimara ora na paya caturmukha ei katha kahena dekha pariksite suka*

**Перевод:**

**The four headed Lord Brahma does not find any limit to the glories of Lord Krsna. This is explained by Sukadeva Gosvami to Maharaja Pariksit.**

*brahma kahena nahi jani sri krsna mahima munigana nahi jane yara guna sima*

**Перевод:**
