**Lord Brahma said:” I donot know the glories of Sri Krsna even the great sages do not know the limit of His glories”.**

*sankadi nahi jane anyera ki daya sahasta vadane ananta jara guna gaya*

**Перевод:**

**What to speak of others even the four Kumaras headed by Sanaka do not know Krsna’s glories. Lord Ananta sings His glories with a thousand mounth.**

*guna gaiya gunera anta nahi pana ei katha bhagavate catur mukha gana*

**Перевод:**

**Though Lord Ananta continually glorifies Lord Krsna still He does not find any end to this.**

This is stated by the four headed Brahma in SB 2.7.41:

*nāntaṁ vidāmy aham amī munayo ’gra-jās te māyā-balasya puruṣasya kuto ’varā ye gāyan guṇān daśa-śatānana ādi-devaḥ śeṣo ’dhunāpi samavasyati nāsya pāram*

**Перевод:**

**Neither I nor all the sages born before you know fully the omnipotent Personality of Godhead. So what can others, who are born after us, know about Him? Even the first incarnation of the Lord, namely Śeṣa, has not been able to reach the limit of such knowledge, although He is describing the qualities of the Lord with ten hundred faces.**

*brahma varuna indra rudra pavana ityadi devata jake kariche stavana munigana samavede gaya nirabadhi antarmana haiya jare bhave yogi suasura gana jake dhane nahi pana sakalera aradhya krsna bhagavate pramana*

**Перевод:**

**He who is prayed by the demigods such as Lord Brahma, Varuna, Indra, Rudra adn pavana, He who is costantly glorified by the sages throughthe songs of the Sama Veda, He who is maditated upon by the introspective yogis and He who is not available to the demigods in their maditation, is the worshipable Supreme Lord of everyone Sri Krsna. This is evident by SB 12.13.1**

*yaṁ brahmā varuṇendra-rudra-marutaḥ stunvanti divyaiḥ stavair vedaiḥ sāṅga-pada-kramopaniṣadair gāyanti yaṁ sāma-gāḥ dhyānāvasthita-tad-gatena manasā paśyanti yaṁ yogino yasyāntaṁ na viduḥ surāsura-gaṇā devāya tasmai namaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Sūta Gosvāmī said: Unto that personality whom Brahmā, Varuṇa, Indra, Rudra and the Maruts praise by chanting transcendental hymns and reciting the Vedas with all their corollaries, pada-kramas and Upaniṣads, to whom the chanters of the Sāma Veda always sing, whom the perfected yogīs see within their minds after fixing themselves in trance and absorbing themselves within Him, and whose limit can never be found by any demigod or demon—unto that Supreme Personality of Godhead I offer my humble obeisances.**

*sarvaradhya sarvamula krsnera avatara sakalera vandaniya sakalera para*

**Перевод:**

**The incarnations of Krsa who is the origin of all the incarnations are worshipable, prayed and Lord of everyone.**

*ramchandre kare stuti deva pancanana tahara pramana suna adhyatma ramayana*

**Перевод:**

**The five headed Lord Siva offered prayers to Sri Ramacandra which is described in adhyatma Ramayana as follows:**

*lanka vijaya haile sarva deva si stuti nati puja kari sakale prasamsi*

**Перевод:**

**When Sri Ramacandra conquered Lanka, all the demigods came and offered prayers to Him. They also offered obeisances, worshipped and glorified Lord Ramacandra.**

*siva kahe suna prabhu akhilera nalha tomara laiya nama amra kritartha*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Siva said Sri Ramacandra, O Lord. You are the master of entire universe. We simply satisfied by chanting your holy names.**

*niravadhi kasivasa bhavanira sane anande tomara name guna kari gane*

**Перевод:**

**I constantly reside in Kasi with my wife Parvati and happily glorify your transcendental names and qualities.**

*mriyamana jane ami diye tomara nama ramaei mahamantra taraka akhyana*

**Перевод:**

**I instruct the distressed people to chant your holy names. The name Rama is a name for deliverence.**

*tarak brahma ei rama nama hana kasipure mukti hetu tamara kirtana*

**Перевод:**

**The holy names of Rama is actually the Supreme deliverence. We engage in chanting Your holy names at Kasi in order to be liberated.**

It is stated in Adhyatma Ramayana as follows ( has to be translated):

*ataeva sabhara sevya sri govinda hana brahma rudra adi yata dekha deva gana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore Lord Govinda is worshipable by everyone including all the demigods such as Lord Brahma adn Lord Siva.**

*krsna bhakti rasa kadamba sadya nirupana sarvotkrsta krsna seva prathana prakarana*

**Перевод:**

**Thus I conclude the first chapter of Sri Krsna Bhakti Rasa Kadamba entitled “ Service to Krsna is the highest of all”.**

*sri caytanya nityananda prabhu panamiya advaita sundarananda mastake bandiya sri parnigopala pada kari abhilasa dina hina kahe ei nayananda dasa*

**Перевод:**

**I offer my humble obeisances unto the lotus feet of Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu, Sri Nityananda Prabhu, Sri Advaita Prabhu, Sri Sundarananda Thakur and Sri Parnigopal Thakur. I the most fallen Nayanananda dasa write this book under they guidence.**

## Chapter II

*jaya jaya rama krsna sakhagana satha sri caitanya nityananda mora prananath*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Sri Krsna and Balarama along with their cowherd friends. All glories to Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu and Sri Nityananda Prabhu who are my life and soul.**

*abhirama sundarananda gopala mahanta sri parnigopala pada smariya nitanta*

**Перевод:**

**I firmly remember the lotus feet of Abhirama Gopala, Sundarananda Gopal and Parni Gopal.**

*ataeva bhaja mana krsna sarvasraya tihon tripta haila jagata tripta haya*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore o dear mind, please worship the lotus feet of Krsna because He is the shelter of everyone. If He is satisfied then the whole world is satisfied.**

*brksa mule jala dile patra sakhara tripti patre jala dile nahe brksera jala prapte*

**Перевод:**

**If one waters at the root of a tree then it’s leaves and branches are automatically nourished. But if one pours water on the leaves however tree is not benedicted.**

*mukhe bhunjaile haya indriya tasana nasa karne anna dile nahe sarira posana*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly if feeds the stomach through the mouth then all the senses are nourished. But if one feeds the ear or nose however the body will not survive.**

*taiche krsna vine naya jagata pujana brksa mule jala dile patrera tasana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore one cannot please everyone by displeasing Krsna. As only watering the root of a tree nourishes its leaves.**

*sarva devatara mula haya bhagavana mulam hy visnor deva nama bhagavata gana*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme Lord is the origin of all the demigods. This is stated in SB that Lord Visnu is the root of all the demigods.**

SB 4.31.14:

*yathā taror mūla-niṣecanena tṛpyanti tat-skandha-bhujopaśākhāḥ prāṇopahārāc ca yathendriyāṇāṁ tathaiva sarvārhaṇam acyutejyā*

**Перевод:**

**As pouring water on the root of a tree energizes the trunk, branches, twigs and everything else, and as supplying food to the stomach enlivens the senses and limbs of the body, simply worshiping the Supreme Personality of Godhead through devotional service automatically satisfies the demigods, who are parts of that Supreme Personality.**

*krsna sarva devamaya sarva devesvara bhakta vatsala krsna bhakta priyankara*

**Перевод:**

**Krsna is the source and controller of all the demigods. He is very affectionate and benefactor towards His devotees.**

*krsna puja karile sarvadeva tusta haya araeva krsna hona sarvadevamaya*

**Перевод:**

**When one worships Krsna all the demigods are automatically satisfied. That is why Krsna is the Lord of all the demigods.**

It is stated in Skanda Purana as follows:

*arcite sarvadevese sankha cakra gadadhare arcite sarvadevah syur yatah sarva maya harih*

**Перевод:**

**Just by worshipping Visnu who holds a counch, a disk, a club in His hands, all the demigods are simultaniously worshipped. Because Lord Hari is the master of everyone.**

*hari bhakti hina janera saba akarana kiva veda bahu sastra tapasya karana*

**Перевод:**

**All the activities of a person who is devoid of devotion to Hari are useless even he studies the vedas, various scriptures or undergo austerities.**

*tapa japa yajna vidhi hari bhakti bine je sva karaye dharma jaya akarane*

**Перевод:**

**All penances and sacrifices performed by such a person without devotional sevice to Hari become futile.**

This is confirmed in Brihat Naradiya Purana as follows:

*kim veda kimu sastraiva kimba tirtha nisevanaih visnu bhakti bihinanam kim tapobhi kimadvaraih*

**Перевод:**

**If one is expert in the vedas and other literature or if he travels to the holy places or undergo austerities, everything goes in vain... if he does not engage in devotional service to Lord Hari.**

*je kare paramabhakti visnu aradhana tabe tara anya rapasyara nahi prayojana*

**Перевод:**

**One who worships Lord Visnu which is the highest form of devotional service does not require to perform any austerities.**

*hari sevaya haya sarva tapah phala ataeva anya tape dekhi je bi phala*

**Перевод:**

**The results of all austerities are simply achieved by service Lord Hari. Therefore other austerities are useless.**

*naradhaye yadi hari tara tapa vrtta klesa matra bhagi haya phala nahi tatha*

**Перевод:**

**If Lord Hari is not worshipped then all one’s austerities are useless. He simply takes trouble without any result.**

*hari aradhana bine kare punya karma punya phala tahe nahi kebala adharma*

**Перевод:**

**If one engages in pious activities without worshipping Hari he does not accumulate any piety rather incurs sin.**

*puna kahi slokardha artha vivarana antara bahye jara hari cintaniya hana*

**Перевод:**

**The purport of the above verse from Brihat Naradiya Purana is that one cannot become succesful if he is no fully Krsna conscious internally and externally.**

It is stated by Sri Akrura in the SB 10.40.9-10:

*sarva eva yajanti tvāṁ sarva-deva-mayeśvaram ye ’py anya-devatā-bhaktā yady apy anya-dhiyaḥ prabho*

**Перевод:**

**But all these people, my Lord, even those who have turned their attention away from You and are worshiping other deities, are actually worshiping You alone, O embodiment of all the demigods.**

*yathādri-prabhavā nadyaḥ parjanyāpūritāḥ prabho viśanti sarvataḥ sindhuṁ tadvat tvāṁ gatayo ’ntataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**As rivers born from the mountains and filled by the rain flow from all sides into the sea, so do all these paths in the end reach You, O master.**

*sarvatirtha sarva yajna haya phalodaya antarbahye jara hari kahila niseaya*

**Перевод:**

**He who is fully Krsna conscious internally and externally, automatically achieves the results of performing sacrifices and visiting holy places.**

*antare bahire jara hari sombandhahina tara tapa akarana sei bhagya hina*

**Перевод:**

**One who is devoid of any internal or external connection with Lord Hari, he is certainly unfortunate and he is under going of austerities are useless.**

As stated in Skanda Purana:

*ei kathara vivarana skanda purane padma puraneo kohila apane*

Apart from the above mentioned verse from Skanda Purana it is also described in Padma Purana:

*krsna bhakta jana hoya sarva dharma karta krsnera abhakta sarva adharmera bharta*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of Krsna have already performed all religious duties whereas the nondevotees are simply engages in irreligious activities.**

*dharma karma kare puna naradhaye hari narake basati tara punya karma kori*

**Перевод:**

**If one engages in religious activities but does not worship Lord Hari then inspite of his such engagements he goes to hell.**

*krsna hetu daive bhakta kare papa karma pap hetu nahi hoya sei hoya dharma*

**Перевод:**

**If by providence a devotee commits sin for the sake of Krsna, it is not to be called sin but pious activity.**

It is stated in Skanda Purana as follows (has to be translated).

Further more it is described in Padma Purana by the Lord Himself as follows: (has to be translated)

*ei vakya dridha dekha prabhura varana bhagavat gitaya kahe sunaha arjuna*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord Himself confirms this statement in BG while speaking to Arjuna.**

*atyanta achara hina nicha chandala jata ananya bhave je more seve abirata nicha haiya se hoa maharera sama sarva dharma vyavasaye hoya se jana*

**Перевод:**

**Even if a person is devoid of proper behavior fallen and born in a dog eaters family, but if he constantly serves me without deviation then at ... he becomes as good as a great soul. Such a person is supposed to have performed all religious duties.**

This is confirmed in Bhagavat Gita 9.30

*api cet su-durācāro bhajate mām ananya-bhāk sādhur eva sa mantavyaḥ samyag vyavasito hi saḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Even if one commits the most abominable action, if he is engaged in devotional service he is to be considered saintly because he is properly situated in his determination.**

*brahman ksatriya vaisya sudra ho apara kona varna hau matra je bhaje isvara visnu bhaktiyuta bhakta haye sovottama dharmi karmi jiva kaha keha nahe sama*

**Перевод:**

**If does not matter whether one is a bahman, a ksatriya, vaisya, a sudra or out caste, but if he simply worships the supreme Lord then he is the topmost. Persons with devotion to Lord Visnu are the highest and fruitive workers or religionists can not be equal to them.**

It is stated in Skanda Purana as follows

*brahman ksatriya vaisyah sudro va yadi betarah visnu bhakti samayukto jneyo sarvottamottamah*

**Перевод:**

**A person who is engaged in devotional service whether he is born in a brahmana, ksatriya, vaisya, sudra or in out caste family is the highest of all.**

*krsna padasraya bine anyera sarane nahi haya bhava trana kahila purane*

**Перевод:**

**If one takes shelter of others rather then Krsna, he cannot be delivered from the material ocean – states the puranas.**

*prashana purusu krsna bine e samsara dukha samudra haite nahi dekhi para*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna is the Supreme Personality of Godhead and the only enjoyer. Without taking shelter of His Lotus feet no one can cross beyond the miserable ocean of material existence.**

*sarva pranira mahat bhaya yamera yantrana krsna bine ghucaite nane anya jana*

**Перевод:**

**The greatest fear of all living entities is the punishment of Yamaraj. No one exept Krsna can counteract this punishment.**

It is described in SB 3.25.41 wherein Kapiladeva speaks as follows

*nānyatra mad bhagavataḥ pradhāna-puruṣeśvarāt ātmanaḥ sarva-bhūtānāṁ bhayaṁ tīvraṁ nivartate*

**Перевод:**

**The terrible fear of birth and death can never be forsaken by anyone who resorts to any shelter other than Myself, for I am the almighty Lord, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, the original source of all creation, and also the Supreme Soul of all souls.**

*bhakta vatsala krsna bhakta priya hana dhruva prati suniti vakya karaha sravana*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna is very dear and affectionate towards His devotees. Please hear the following statement of Suniti to her son Dhruva Maharaj.**

This is stated in SB 4.8.22

*tam eva vatsāśraya bhṛtya-vatsalaṁ mumukṣubhir mṛgya-padābja-paddhatim ananya-bhāve nija-dharma-bhāvite manasy avasthāpya bhajasva pūruṣam*

**Перевод:**

**My dear boy, you also should take shelter of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who is very kind to His devotees. Persons seeking liberation from the cycle of birth and death always take shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord in devotional service. Becoming purified by executing your allotted occupation, just situate the Supreme Personality of Godhead in your heart, and without deviating for a moment, engage always in His service.**

*bhakta priya bhakta vasya hoya bhagavan suka vakya bhagavate kara avadhana*

**Перевод:**

**Srila Sukadeva Gosvami has nicely described in SB that Lord Krsna is very dear to and under the control of His devotees.**

*udkhale bhandite nare yasoda sundari matara dekhiya srama sucintita hari*

**Перевод:**

**When beautiful mother Yasoda could not bind her son in the wooden mortar the Lord became worried by seeing His mothers hand labour.**

*kripa kori apane bandhana laia bhakta vasya bhagavan granthe vibarela*

**Перевод:**

**THe Lord personnly agreed to be bound by His devotee because He is under the control of His devotee’s love.**

*bhaktera adhina krsna bhakta vase cale ambaresa prasangete bhagavan bole*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna is subordinate to His devotees and according to their will. This fact the Lord personnly declares in connection with the incident of Maharaja Ambarish.**

As stated in SB 10.9.18

*sva-mātuḥ svinna-gātrāyā visrasta-kabara-srajaḥ dṛṣṭvā pariśramaṁ kṛṣṇaḥ kṛpayāsīt sva-bandhane*

**Перевод:**

**Because of mother Yaśodā’s hard labor, her whole body became covered with perspiration, and the flowers and comb were falling from her hair. When child Kṛṣṇa saw His mother thus fatigued, He became merciful to her and agreed to be bound.**

*durvasa trijagata bhrami sthana na paiya visnura saranloila vaikunthere jaiya*

**Перевод:**

**After wondering throughout the three worlds Dhruva Muni could not find any suitable place for shelter. Finally he went to Vaikuntha and took shelter of Lord Visnu.**

*ambarisa sthane prabhu kariyachi aparadha tumi tusta hao prabhu karaha prasada*

**Перевод:**

**I have commited an offense at the feet of Ambarisa Maharaj. Be pleased with me and bestow mercy upon me.**

*sei kalera suno katha kahena isvara ami bhaka parashina nahi svatantara*

**Перевод:**

**At that time the supreme Lord Visnu said to Durvasa Muni “ I am not independent, I am under the control of my devotee.**

*sadhura hrdaye baddha amara antara ei to kahila satya suno dvija bara*

**Перевод:**

**I am captured within the hearts of my devotees. O best of the brahmanas this is truth I am telling you.**

*durvasake ei katha kohe bhagavana aparadha ksamaite bhakta sthanke pathana*

**Перевод:**

**After speaking in this way the Lord instructed Durvasa to return to Ambarish and beg forgiveness.**

*krsna sama bhakta vasya ke ache dayala heno prabhu na bhajya gonaila kala*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore who else is so merciful and affectionate towards His devotees then Lord Krsna. I have simply wasted my time by not serving such a Lord.**

This is confirmed in SB 9.4.63-64

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca ahaṁ bhakta-parādhīno hy asvatantra iva dvija sādhubhir grasta-hṛdayo bhaktair bhakta-jana-priyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Personality of Godhead said to the brāhmaṇa: I am completely under the control of My devotees. Indeed, I am not at all independent. Because My devotees are completely devoid of material desires, I sit only within the cores of their hearts. What to speak of My devotee, even those who are devotees of My devotee are very dear to Me.**

*nāham ātmānam āśāse mad-bhaktaiḥ sādhubhir vinā śriyaṁ cātyantikīṁ brahman yeṣāṁ gatir ahaṁ parā*

**Перевод:**

**O best of the brāhmaṇas, without saintly persons for whom I am the only destination, I do not desire to enjoy My transcendental bliss and My supreme opulences.**

*bhakta ganera krsna bahi any anahi gati samsara samucha para ei ta yukti*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of Krsna have no other shelter them Lord Krsna His lotus feet are the only boat to cross the ocean of material existence.**

*tritape tapita tanu yata jika gana tahe yudaite sei yugala carana*

**Перевод:**

**The living entities are constantly burning with the three fold miseries. The lotus feet of the Lord are the only solace for them.**

*adhyatmika adhidaivika adibhautika ara ei tina tape saba tapita samsara*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone in this material world are afflicted by the three fold miseries namely miseries caused by one’s body, caused by the demigods and caused by other living entities.**

*krsna pada dvanda aravinda amrta barasne tapita janara dukha khandai apane*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna destroys the miserable conditions of the afflicted people by showering the nectar of His lotus feet.**

*krsnasaya bine tapa na haya khandana krsnoddhava samvada ekadasete varnana*

**Перевод:**

**Without taking shelter of the lotus feet of Krsna one’s three fold material miseries are not destroyed. This is confirmed in the conversations between Lord Krsna and Uddhava in the eleventh canto of SB**

In SB 11.19.9 stated as follows

*tāpa-trayeṇābhihatasya ghore santapyamānasya bhavādhvanīśa paśyāmi nānyac charaṇaṁ tavāṅghri-dvandvātapatrād amṛtābhivarṣāt*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Lord, for one who is being tormented on the terrible path of birth and death and is constantly overwhelmed by the threefold miseries, I do not see any possible shelter other than Your two lotus feet, which are just like a refreshing umbrella that pours down showers of delicious nectar.**

*ataeva puna kahi suna bandhu gana krsna bhaktera na haya kabha yama darasana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore o friends. Please hear again. The devotees of Krsna does not ever meet Yamaraja the superintendent of death.**

*krsna kahena mora bhakta bhakti anusare nirantaramora nama jiva smriti kare krsna krsna ei namalaite sunite ananda pulaka bhava haya jara cite sei haya bhaktottama nama grahana smarane ante tahara sthiti amara sadane*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna says if a person takes to the process of devotional service, or if someone constantly remember my holy names, or if one develops ecstatic symptoms while chanting and hearing my holy names then he is topmost devotee. At the end of his life he attains supreme abode as a result of his chanting and remembering my holy names.**

*nama phale sukhe tare sukhe tare samsara jaladhi jahe ache stri putradi kumbhira viradhi*

**Перевод:**

**Due to chanting the holy names of the Lord such a person happily crosses beyond the ocean of material existence which is full of aversed objects such as wife and children.**

*bhavasindhu tari bhakta krsna pada paya krsna bhakta yamalaya kabhu nahi jaya*

**Перевод:**

**After crossing the material existence the devotee reaches the lotus feet of Krsna. The devotees of Krsna never go to the abode of Yamaraja.**

It is described in Kashapa pancharatra as follows has to be translated.

*krsna bhata jane nahi yama adhikara ajamila upakhyana bhagavati pracara*

**Перевод:**

**Yamaraja has no authority on the devotees of Krsna. This is clearly displayed in the incident of Ajamila described in SB**

*mahapapi ajamila vipra kule janma nija dharam tyaga kori korila nicha karma*

**Перевод:**

**The most sinful Ajamila was born in a brahmin family but he gave up his prescribed religious duties and engaged in low class sinful activities.**

*madhya mamsa bhaksuna kaila byadha acarana svakriya kariya tyaga vesyate gamana*

**Перевод:**

**He ate meat, drink vine and led a life of a hu... He gave up his own duties and took pleasure in a prostitute.**

*vesya grbhe janmaila caya putra tara courya vrtti mitya kamukha vyavahara*

**Перевод:**

**He begot six sons in the womb of the prostitute, he developed propensities for stealing, for telling lies and lusty behavior.**

*kanistha putrere nama thuila narayana atusya tahe sneha vatsalya karana*

**Перевод:**

**He kept the name of his youngest son Narayana. He was very affectionate and loving to him.**

*kala krame jara grasta hoila atura yamaduta aila tare loite satvara*

**Перевод:**

**I due course of time Ajamila became afflicted with old age. At that time Yamadutas or the order carrier of Yamaraja came to take him away.**

*yamaduta aila caya ati bhayankara louha danda carma dadi kampita adhara*

**Перевод:**

**Fears looking six Yamadutas arrived near his bedside. Their lips were shaking in anger.**

*bhaya paiya ajamila murcha gata hona are putra putra rakha narayana*

**Перевод:**

**Due to great fear Ajamila became unconscious, he cry out O my son Narayana please save me.**

*nama bhase narayana balila jakhana mrya mana kale nama hoila smarana*

**Перевод:**

**His calling out for his son Narayana transformed into namabhase or chanting the Lords name in an offenseless stage. Thus he remembered the Lords name at the time of his death.**

*sarvapapa khandila tara namabhasa bale bhaya paiya yamaduta kampita sakale*

**Перевод:**

**All his sinful reactions were destroyed by the strength of namabhasa. The Yamadutas became frightened and began to shiver.**

*hena kale visnuduta aila carijana ajamilera ghucaila sakala bandana*

**Перевод:**

**At that time four Visnudutas arrived there and freed Ajamila from all his bondages.**

*yamaduta palaila yama vidya mana ajamilere kahila sakala akhyana*

**Перевод:**

**When the Yamadutas left Ajamila, Yamaraj personally came there and explained the whole incident to Ajamila.**

*namabhase mukta kaila sakala bandhana bhaktibhare nama balera ke kari varnana*

**Перевод:**

**Just by a glimpse of chanting the holy names of the Lord all your material bondeges were freed. So who can describe the glories of one who chants the holy names with devotion.**

*cita prasange yama kana dutagana visnubhakta jana nikate na jabe svapane*

**Перевод:**

**In this connection Yamaraja further instructed his followers do not go near a devotee of Lord Visnu even in your dream.**

*visnu bhaktajanra danda karta ami nahi niscaya kariya ei dutagane kahi*

**Перевод:**

**I have no authority to punish the devotees of Lord Visnu. He spoke these words with great confidence.**

This is stated in SB 6.2.7-8

*ayaṁ hi kṛta-nirveśo janma-koṭy-aṁhasām api yad vyājahāra vivaśo nāma svasty-ayanaṁ hareḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Ajāmila has already atoned for all his sinful actions. Indeed, he has atoned not only for sins performed in one life but for those performed in millions of lives, for in a helpless condition he chanted the holy name of Nārāyaṇa. Even though he did not chant purely, he chanted without offense, and therefore he is now pure and eligible for liberation.**

*etenaiva hy aghono ’sya kṛtaṁ syād agha-niṣkṛtam yadā nārāyaṇāyeti jagāda catur-akṣaram*

**Перевод:**

**The Viṣṇudūtas continued: Even previously, while eating and at other times, this Ajāmila would call his son, saying, “My dear Nārāyaṇa, please come here.” Although calling the name of his son, he nevertheless uttered the four syllables nā-rā-ya-ṇa. Simply by chanting the name of Nārāyaṇa in this way, he sufficiently atoned for the sinful reactions of millions of lives.**

It is further confirmed by the statement of Yamaraja in Visnu Purana 3.7.14 as follows ( has to be translated)

*vaisnvera dandyakarta ami kabhu nahi suna ore dutagana tomasabhe kahi*

**Перевод:**

**Hear o my followers I am never qualified to punish the vaisnavas**

*e katha suniya puna kahe duta gana ke dandya kare aniba yamera sadana*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these words from their master Yamaraja, the Yamadutas inquired “ who are actually punishable, whom should we bring to you for punishment.**

*yama kahe suna tahe kahi vivarana krsna sambandhahina jei sava jana krsna nama guna yasa jihvaya na sphure jara citta krsna nama dhyana na kare jahara mastaka krsne na kare pramana ekabara krsna murti nahi kare dhyana krsna karma hina jiva nudha naradhama tara sasti karta ami dandadhara yama*

**Перевод:**

**Yamaraj said bring those fods and lowest men who have no relationship with Krsna, whose tonques do not glorify the names, fame and qualities of Krsna, whose hearts do not meditate on holy names of Krsna, whose heads do not bow down to Krsna, who never meditate on the form of Krsna and who do not engage in activities to please Krsna, for punishment.**

*sei saba lokere yamapure krsna samvbandha hina je achaye samsare*

**Перевод:**

**Bring all those people to my abode, who are devoid of any connection with Krsna in the material existence.**

*krsna seva paricarya krsna bhakti bine kaliyuge gati nai krsna nama bine*

**Перевод:**

**Without engaging in devotional service to Krsna specially the chanting of the holy names, there is no other alternative for deliverance in Kaliyuga.**

*evakara diya vyasa kahe bara bara gati nahi gati nahi nama bine ara*

**Перевод:**

**By using the word eva Sri Vyasadeva repeatedly confirms that except the chanting of the holy names of the Lord there is no alternative, the is no alternative.**

*krsna nama bine gati nahi kali kale ei satya ei satya dharma sastre bale*

**Перевод:**

**Except the chanting of the holy names, there is no other way of deliverance in this age of Kali. This has repeatedly been established in the scriptures.**

It is stated in Brihat Naradiya Purana as follows

?????

*kali kale sara sara nama sankirtan sarvadhama labhe name ei nirupana*

**Перевод:**

**It has been ascertained in the sastras that congregational chanting of the holy names of the Lord is the essence of Kaliyuga. All religion follow the chanting of the holy names.**

This is stated in SB 11.5.36 as follows

*kaliṁ sabhājayanty āryā guṇa jñāḥ sāra-bhāginaḥ yatra saṅkīrtanenaiva sarva-svārtho ’bhilabhyate*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are actually advanced in knowledge are able to appreciate the essential value of this age of Kali. Such enlightened persons worship Kali-yuga because in this fallen age all perfection of life can easily be achieved by the performance of saṅkīrtana.**

*dosera samuha kali papera nicaya kintu eka kaliyuge mahaguna haya*

**Перевод:**

**Although the age of Kali is full of faults but there is a great quality in it.**

*krsna nama sarva sara kaliyuge dharma sri krsna kirtane haya siddha sarva karma*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting the holy names of Krsna is the essence of all. This is the religious principles prescribed for Kaliyuga. By chanting the holy names of Sri Krsna all one’s activities are successful.**

*krsna nama kirtane loka kritatrtha haiya parama pada paya sei mukta bandha haiya*

**Перевод:**

**By chanting the names of Krsna peoples lives are perfect. They become freed from material bondages and attain the supreme destination.**

SB 12.3.51 states

*kaler doṣa-nidhe rājann asti hy eko mahān guṇaḥ kīrtanād eva kṛṣṇasya mukta-saṅgaḥ paraṁ vrajet*

**Перевод:**

**My dear King, although Kali-yuga is an ocean of faults, there is still one good quality about this age: Simply by chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa mahā-mantra, one can become free from material bondage and be promoted to the transcendental kingdom.**

*yaga yoga yajna dharmerea apeksa nai kalye sarva siddhi haya lokera krsna nama laile*

**Перевод:**

**Other religioous duties such as sacrifice, meditation are not successful in Kali Yuga. Simply by chanting the holy names of Krsna people will achieve all perfection.**

*satya yuge dhana yage hoita kritartha treta yuge yajna dharme hoita diksita*

**Перевод:**

**People in the age of satya yuga achieved their goal of life through meditation and in treta yuga they achieve their goal of life by performing sacrifices.**

*swapara yuge krsna seva archana pujana sarva siddhi kaliyuge nama sankirtana*

**Перевод:**

**In Dwapara Yuga people attain their goal by worshipping deity form of Krshna in the temple. But in Kali Yuga all perfection can be achiebed simply by congregational chanting of the holy names.**

*dhyana yajna puja vidira tina yugera phala kaliyuge krsna name siddhi sakala*

**Перевод:**

**The prescribed methods for the three yugas are meditation, performance of sacrifices and temple worshipping. But in Kali Yuga all perfection are achieved simply by chanting of the holy names of Krsna.**

This is confirmed in Naradiya Purana as follows

???????

It is also mentioned in Vishnu Rahasya as follows

*abhyarcito harin bhaktya krite barsa sata trayam phalam upnoti avikalam kalau tat hari kirtane*

**Перевод:**

**The same result that one achives by worshipping Lord Hari with devotion for three hundred years, is achieved by chanting the names of Hari in this age of Kali.**

*satya yuge vidhi mate bhakti kari hari tina sata baras yadi aradhana kari sei phala kali yuge nama sankirtane sarva siddhi haya lokera namadi grahane*

**Перевод:**

**The result one achieves by properly worshipping lord Hari in Satya Yuga for three hundred years is easily achieved in Kali yuga simply by congregational chanting of the Lord’s holy names. Chanting of the holy names bring all auspiciousness.**

*naradiya purane suno yugadharma katana cari yugera phala kalau nama sankirtana*

**Перевод:**

**According to Naradiya Purana the yuga dharma of Kali Yuga is congregational chanting of the Lord’s holy names.**

*ataiva mane ami ppuna prabodhiye bhaga krsna cinta krsna kohila niscaya*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore I am again solacing my mind “ O dear mind, worship Krsna remember Krsna. I am telling you this with firm conviction.”**

*na kariha anya sanga anya alapana anya deva na bhajiha anyera are hana*

**Перевод:**

**Do not associate or converse with nondevotees. Do not worship the demigods or glorify others.**

*asatkatha asatcesta asatera sanga asat sastra asat vada asatkrida ranga e sava chadiya mana sadhusanga kari akapate kayamanesada bhaja hari*

**Перевод:**

**O dear mind give up worldly topics, ill endeavors, bad association study of nondevotional literature, worldly conversations and worldly sporting. And worship the lotus feet of Hari without duplicity with your body and mind.**

*kama krodha lobha moha karaha tyajana mada marsarya parityaga indriya damana*

**Перевод:**

**Please give up lust, anger, greed, illusion, pride, envy and control your senses.**

*dharma artha kama moksa chadiya vasana sadhu sanga kari kara govinda arcana*

**Перевод:**

**Abandon alldesires for religion, economic development, sense gratification, liberation and engage in worship of Govinda in the association of devotees.**

*sadhu sanga tulana nahe caturvarga phala svargadi pada jani tuchha sakala*

**Перевод:**

**The result of sadhu sanga or association with devotees cannot be compared with the four objects of life such as religion. Achieving heavenly planets is very insignificant.**

## Chapter III

### Text 1

*jaya re jaya re hare śrī kṛṣṇa caitanya dhanya dhanya avatāra*  
*dīna janāra bandhu kevala karuṇa sindhu dīna dekhi karaha uddhāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the supreme personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya. He is a friend of the poor and the most merciful incarnation. Please deliver this fallen soul.**

### Text 2

*parama ānanda jaya jaya nityānanda ṭhākura parama dayāla*  
*jaya jaya jayādvaita prema rasa yuta jaya jaya sundara gopāla*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the most blissful Śrī Nityānanda. He is the personification of compassion. All glories to Śrī Advaita who is full of love of God and all glories to Śrī Sundarānanda.**

### Text 3

*śrī caitanya anugata bhakti bhāva yuta jaya jaya vaiṣṇavā samāja*  
*sabākāra pade nati śata koṭi stuti siddhi kara ei kāja nija*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to all the devotees who are followers of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and engaged in pure devotional service. I offer my humble obeisances unto their lotus feet millions of times please make this work successful.**

### Text 4

*śrī kṛṣṇa bhajane nāhi kāla kāla niyama*  
*divā-niśi bhaja kṛṣṇa caḍi anya karma*

**Перевод:**

**There is no special rule regarding the time for worshipping Kṛṣṇa. One should abandon all varieties of activities and worship Kṛṣṇa day and night.**

### Text 5

*bālaka kale ārambhilā jīvana paryanta*  
*bhaja kṛṣṇa niravadhi kariyā ekānta*

**Перевод:**

**One should constantly worship Kṛṣṇa without deviation beginning from his childhood up to the end of his life.**

It is confirmed in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam (7.6.1)

*śrī-prahrāda uvāca kaumāra ācaret prājño dharmān bhāgavatān iha durlabhaṁ mānuṣaṁ janma tad apy adhruvam arthadam*

**Перевод:**

**Prahlāda Mahārāja said: One who is sufficiently intelligent should use the human form of body from the very beginning of life—in other words, from the tender age of childhood—to practice the activities of devotional service, giving up all other engagements. The human body is most rarely achieved, and although temporary like other bodies, it is meaningful because in human life one can perform devotional service. Even a slight amount of sincere devotional service can give one complete perfection.**

### Text 6

*bālya haite yena bhaje pāpa mūḍha mati*  
*vṛddha haile bhajiba kṛṣṇa ei āśa tathi*

**Перевод:**

**Only a sinful foolish person does not worship Kṛṣṇa from his childhood. He hopes that he will worship Kṛṣṇa when he gets old.**

### Text 7

*āji kāle karite tāra āyu bahi jaya*  
*sevā dharma nāhi haya māriyā*

**Перевод:**

**Today he thinks “I will worship Kṛṣṇa tomorrow” in this way he uselessly passes his life and cannot engage in the service of the Lord. Finally he dies and continues to suffer.**

### Text 8

*bālya kahe kṛṣṇa bhajiba yauvane*  
*nānā krīḍā kare śiśu bālaka gaṇa sane*

**Перевод:**

**Such a person says in his childhood I will worship the Lord when I am young and thus he engages in playing with other children.**

### Text 9

*bālya kāla ei rūpe vṛthā dekha jaya*  
*hitāhita nāhi jāne īśvara*

**Перевод:**

**Just see how a person uselessly spends his childhood in this way. By the influence of the illusory energy of the Lord he cannot understand what is beneficial for him and what is not beneficial for him.**

### Text 10

*yauvana vayase haya yuvatī vilāsa*  
*viṣaya vāsanā bāḍhe dhanete prayāsa*

**Перевод:**

**When he becomes a mature young man he enjoys the association of women. His desires for sense gratification and endeavor for accumulating wealth increase.**

### Text 11

*dhana upārjana ādi bāḍhe nānā raṅga*  
*grāmya sukhe magna haya viṣayīra saṅga*

**Перевод:**

**As a result of accumulating wealth his propensity for enjoyment increases. He then associates with persons attached to sense gratification and indulges in such activities.**

### Text 12

*deha garve takhana na jāne bhāla-manda*  
*īśvarera māyāte viṣaya haye bandha*

**Перевод:**

**At that time he cannot understand what is good and what is bad. Due to the illusory energy of the Lord he becomes entangled in material enjoyment.**

### Text 13

*yauvana daśāte kari dhana upārjana*  
*vṛddhāvasthā haile kṛṣṇa kariba sādhana*

**Перевод:**

**Such a person thinks – now I am young. Let me accumulate wealth. I will worship Kṛṣṇa when I am old.**

### Text 14

*ei rūpe yuvā jaya bārdhakya upasthite*  
*vṛddhāvasthā haile bhai baḍai durgati*

**Перевод:**

**In this way he reaches the old age. O brother, the old age is very miserable.**

### Text 15

*sarva kārye asamartha sadāi pīḍitā*  
*kṣudhā tṛṣṇā sahite nāre sadā pipāsila*

**Перевод:**

**He cannot perform any activities and is always diseased. He cannot tolerate hunger and thirst and is always hankering.**

### Text 16

*karṇa patha ruddha haiyā nāma nāhi śune*  
*cakṣu dṛṣṭi khāta haya kampa kṣaṇe kṣaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**He can no longer hear the names and so on His eyesight is diminished and hi shivers from time to time.**

### Text 17

*kāśa śvāsa jarā grasta kaṇṭha ghara ghara haya*  
*kapha vata śleṣma grasta satata āśaya*

**Перевод:**

**He becomes afflicted with cough bile and air. He dwindles, faces trouble in breathing and his throat chokes.**

### Text 18

*paridhīna haya takhana lobha haya sadā*  
*stri puruṣa bandha gaṇe kare amaryādā*

**Перевод:**

**Then he becomes defendant on others and is always greedy. His wife, children and relatives no longer respect him.**

### Text 19

*takhana kahaye vṛddha kāla mora gela*  
*tina kālera madhye kabhu hari sevā nāila*

**Перевод:**

**He laments “ my old age passed. I have never served Lord Hari either in my childhood, youth hood and in old age".**

### Text 20

*vṛddha haile keha jāni loka lāje kāya*  
*kona mūḍhera vṛddha haile cintā nāhi haya*

**Перевод:**

**When one is old he laments out of shame. Yet there are other foolish people who even in their old age do not worry or lament.**

### Text 21

*asat saṅgera phala kṛṣṇa karma hīnā*  
*asat vyāpāra kari majaila?? dīna*

**Перевод:**

**The result of bad association is the absence of devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. A person wastes his valuable time engaging in sinful activities.**

### Text 22

*tina kṛta ei rūpe vṛthā tāra jaya*  
*nija karma bhuñje loka īśvara māyayā*

**Перевод:**

**In this way a person uselessly spend his whole life. By the influence of the illusory energy of the Lord everyone enjoys the result of their own karma.**

### Text 23

*tāhe vṛddha keha haiche?? keha bālye mare*  
*yuvā kāle mṛtyu vā dekhaha kaṇa nāre*

**Перевод:**

**Even then someone grows old and someone dies in his childhood and there are yet others who die during their youth age.**

### Text 24

*etheka?? dekhiyā mudrā nā bujhaye jarā*  
*īśvarera mayā rajju tārite pare kara*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore those who do not understand these facts are simply foolish. Actually who can escape the influence of the Lords illusory energy?**

It is described in Viṣṇu Purāṇa (1.17.72-73)

###########(has to be translated)

### Text 25

*pāiyā durlabha deha yadi vaneha?? sukha*  
*bhaja kṛṣṇa sadā tabe chādi viṣayān mukha*

**Перевод:**

**If ?even? after received this rarely attained human form of life a person desires real happiness than he should give up inclination towards material enjoyment and always worship Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 26-27

*na na yoni bhrami jīva bahu puṇya phale*  
*nara deha pāya yadi ei bhūmi tale pāpa puṇya kaha saba nara dehe jāni śubha suba?? karma-kāṇḍa ei karma bhūmi*

**Перевод:**

**After wondering through various spices of life and as a result of heaps of pious activities the living entity obtains a human form of life in this earth. The pious and impious activities are applicable only to the human beings. Thus human body is the field of both pious and impious activities.**

### Text 28

*pāiyā durlabha deha hele hārāila*  
*kaneh?? mūlye cintāmaṇi āpani becila??*

**Перевод:**

**Having received this rare human life if one looses by utter negligence then it is to be understood that he sold touch stone in exchange of glasses.**

### Text 29

*amūlya ratana deha pāiyā yatane*  
*mitya?? viṣaya pāila becilam?? āpane*

**Перевод:**

**I have received this valuable jewel like human body, but I have sold myself in exchange of false temporary enjoyment.**

### Text 30

*bhāva bhoga viṣaya lāgi hena deha gela*  
*pāiyā durlabha deha hare na bhajila*

**Перевод:**

**My life is wasted because of material enjoyment. Morever?? inspite of receiving this rare human body I did not worship Hari**

It is stated in Shanti Sataka as follows

janmedham vandhyatam hitam (has to be translated)

### Text 31

*hena janma vandhya?? haila nāhita saphula??*  
*sakala īśvara mayā moha mahimā kevala*

**Перевод:**

**Such a human form of life did not yeild any result. This is simply due to the bewilderment of the Lords illusory energy.**

### Text 32

*tabe keha janya śūnya kena moha kara*  
*mahā rūpa vipada jale baddha kalevara*

**Перевод:**

**Why then do you knowingly create illusion for yourself and become entangled in the dangerous networks of Mayā.**

### Text 33

*agni śikhā dekhiyā pataṅga gaṇa dhāya*  
*bhakṣya sāmagrī bole pudhi?? pade tayā*

**Перевод:**

**As soon as the insects see the flame of fire they rush in thinking it to be food.**

### Text 34

*bhakṣya- bhakṣya nāhi jñāna tāra acetana*  
*bhālo manda na jāniyā haila nidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**Since their consciousness is not developed they cannot discriminates between eatables and uneatables. Thus without understanding what is good and what is bad they are destroyed.**

### Text 35

*ajñāne dekhaha mīna varasi?? bhaksaye??*  
*tāhāra yatana tāra āge na jā naye.*

**Перевод:**

**Just see how a fish swallows a fishing hook. They do not know about the pain in a duance???**

### Text 36

*na jāniyā mṛtyu haya tāra acetana*  
*āmā sabhāra dekha āche viśiṣṭa cetana*

**Перевод:**

**They die out of ignorance due to their undeveloped consciousness. But human beings have developed consciousness.**

### Text 37

*jāniyā āmāra tabu ajñāna sakale*  
*baddha haichi?? dekha saṁsāra vipada jale*

**Перевод:**

**Yet knowingly we have become ignorant and we have been bound in the dangerous net of saṁsāra.**

It is described in the scripture as follows

ajanan dahatahartim…..(has to be translated)

### Text 38

*viṣaya vista mati na haiyo?? āra*  
*viṣayī janāra kabu na dekhi uddhāra*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore do not become absorbed in material enjoyment, for I do not see any hope of deliverance for the materialists.**

## Chapter III (also)

All glories to Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa Balarāma

*jaya re jaya re hare śrī kṛṣṇa caitanya dhanya dhanya avatāra dīna janāra bandhu kevala karuṇa sindhu dīna dekhi karaha uddhāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the supreme personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya. He is a friend of the poor and the most merciful incarnation. Please deliver this fallen soul. (3.1)**

*parama ānanda jaya jaya nityānanda ṭhākura parama dayāla jaya jaya jayādvaita prema rasa yuta jaya jaya sundara gopāla*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the most blissful Śrī Nityānanda. He is the personification of compassion. All glories to Śrī Advaita who is full of love of God and all glories to Śrī Sundarānanda.(3.2)**

*śrī caitanya anugata bhakti bhāva yuta jaya jaya vaiṣṇavā samāja sabākāra pade nati śata koṭi stuti siddhi kara ei kāja nija*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to all the devotees who are followers of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and engaged in pure devotional service. I offer my humble obeisances unto their lotus feet millions of times please make this work successful. (3.3)**

*śrī kṛṣṇa bhajane nāhi kāla kāla niyama divā-niśi bhaja kṛṣṇa caḍi anya karma*

**Перевод:**

**There is no special rule regarding the time for worshipping Kṛṣṇa. One should abandon all varieties of activities and worship Kṛṣṇa day and night.(3.4)**

*bālaka kale ārambhilā jīvana paryanta bhaja kṛṣṇa niravadhi kariyā ekānta*

**Перевод:**

**One should constantly worship Kṛṣṇa without deviation beginning from his childhood up to the end of his life.(3.5)**

It is confirmed in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam (3.7.6.1)

*śrī-prahrāda uvāca kaumāra ācaret prājño dharmān bhāgavatān iha durlabhaṁ mānuṣaṁ janma tad apy adhruvam arthadam*

**Перевод:**

**Prahlāda Mahārāja said: One who is sufficiently intelligent should use the human form of body from the very beginning of life—in other words, from the tender age of childhood—to practice the activities of devotional service, giving up all other engagements. The human body is most rarely achieved, and although temporary like other bodies, it is meaningful because in human life one can perform devotional service. Even a slight amount of sincere devotional service can give one complete perfection.**

*bālya haite yena bhaje pāpa mūḍha mati vṛddha haile bhajiba kṛṣṇa ei āśa tathi*

**Перевод:**

**Перевод:**

*viṣaya kahake kahi kara avadhana grāmya sukha grāmya karma grāmya gītā gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Please hear attentively about the definition of material sense objects.(3.40)**

*vahyendriya samyama bhāva ajitendriya nija abhilāṣa yate sei ta viṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**Morever material sense objects are those items which are desired by a person whose external senses are uncontrolled.**

*svadeha sukha tarparya jeva karma haya kṛṣṇa sambandha hīnā tāre viṣaya kahaya*

**Перевод:**

**Those activities which performed for the satisfaction of one’s own body and are unrelated to Kṛṣṇa are known as viṣaya.(3.41)**

*viṣaya asakta dosa karaha smaraṇa bhagavāna arjune kaṇa gitaya varṇana*

**Перевод:**

**Please remembyr Lord Kṛṣṇa’s instructions to Arjuna in Bhagavad Gītā regarding the faults of being attached to material enjiyment.(3.42)**

*viṣaya bhavite purusera viṣaya saṅga haya saṅga haile tāhe asakti badeya*

**Перевод:**

**When a person contemplets on the object of the senses he developes attachment for them.(3.43)**

*kamasakta haile punaḥ kārye haya krodha krodhe haya mahā jahe ksina haya bodha*

**Перевод:**

**when one is too mych attached to lust he again develops anger. From anger comes illusion. From illusion one’s intelligence is diminished.(3.44)**

*mohe haya smṛti naṣṭa śāstra jñāna yata kartavya akartavya jñāna tāhe haya hata*

**Перевод:**

**Due to illusion only one’s memeory including all scriptural knowledge is lost. As a result pne’s power of discrimination between good and bad is defeated.(3.45)**

*smṛti bhrasta haile haya buddhi binasana buddhi lopa haile haya tāhāte marana*

**Перевод:**

**Loss of memory results in lose of intelligence. When one’s intelligence I lost he again falls down.(3.46)**

As it is confirmed in in Bhagavd Gītā (2.62-63)

*dhyāyato viṣayān puṁsaḥ / saṅgas teṣūpajāyate saṅgāt sañjāyate kāmaḥ / kāmāt krodho ’bhijāyate*

**Перевод:**

**While contemplating the objects of the senses, a person develops attachment for them, and from such attachment lust develops, and from lust anger arises.**

*krodhād bhavati sammohaḥ / sammohāt smṛti-vibhramaḥ smṛti-bhraṁśād buddhi-nāśo / buddhi-nāśāt praṇaśyati*

**Перевод:**

**From anger, complete delusion arises, and from delusion bewilderment of memory. When memory is bewildered, intelligence is lost, and when intelligence is lost one falls down again into the material pool.**

*viṣayite kṛṣṇabesa kabhu nāhi haya purāṇa pramana tāhe sunaha niscaya*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa consciousness cannot enter into the hearts of material enjoyers. Please hear the evidences from puranas in this regard.(3.47)**

*visayi janāra citte viṣaya avesa tāra dehe duragata sadā krsnavesa*

**Перевод:**

**The hearts of the material enjoyers are full of material desires.Kṛṣṇa consciousness situated far away from such hearts.(3.48)**

*tāhāte samanya eka vastu rakhiya yadi jaya sei vastu anya dese ukatibe jaya paseim dese rakha dravya ukata pūrva dese yanha na rakhila puna tanha pave kise.*

**Перевод:**

**If one keeps an item in one particular place how can he expects it to get in some other place. As one keeps an object on the western side how can he receive it in the eastern side.(3.49-50)**

*viṣayī janāra mata viṣaya vāsanā tāra dehe durgata sadā kṛṣṇa prema*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa prema stays far away from those who have desire for material enjoyment.(3.51)**

*kṛṣṇavesa thake jeva kṛṣṇa premi haya visayita citte sadā viṣaya dharma haya*

**Перевод:**

**A person who is always absorbed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness will certainly achive love of Kṛṣṇa whereas the heart of a material enjoyer is filled with material desires.(3.52)**

*ataeva visayite nāhi kṛṣṇavesa ei ta kahila dosa viṣaya avesa*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore there is certainly not any tinge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness aong the material enjoyers. This is the deficiancy of material enjoyments.(3.53)**

*kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇe' haya kṛṣṇavesa sadā kṛṣṇa vinu tāra citte na rahe ekada*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of Kṛṣṇa are always absorbed in thought of Kṛṣṇa. They so not forget Kṛṣṇa even for a moment.(3.54)**

*ataeva bhagavate kahe bhagavāna viṣaya bhavite jāna viṣayake pāna*

**Перевод:**

**That is why the supreme Lord states in Śrīmad bhāgavatam that if a person simply thinks of material enjoyment he will attain material enjoyment.(3.55)**

*amake citaye je vā se amake pāya āmā chādi bhakta citta kanhe nāhi jaya*

**Перевод:**

**But one who thinks of me surely attains me. The mind of a devotee does not deviate at all from me.(3.56)**

*bhakta janera citta amite sadā lia ataeva viṣayī loka sadā amaya hīnā*

**Перевод:**

**The mind of a devotee is always absorbed in me whereas the mind of a sense enjoyer is always devoid of me.(3.57)**

It is described in Viṣṇu Purāṇa “viṣaya cittanam….” has to be translated

It is stated in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (7.5.30)

*śrī-prahrāda uvāca matir na kṛṣṇe parataḥ svato vā mitho ’bhipadyeta gṛha-vratānām adānta-gobhir viśatāṁ tamisraṁ punaḥ punaś carvita-carvaṇānām*

**Перевод:**

**Prahlāda Mahārāja replied: Because of their uncontrolled senses, persons too addicted to materialistic life make progress toward hellish conditions and repeatedly chew that which has already been chewed. Their inclinations toward Kṛṣṇa are never aroused, either by the instructions of others, by their own efforts, or by a combination of both.**

*kṛṣṇa karma bahirmukha sadā soucya haya kṛṣṇa karma hīnā janera vṛthā ayuksaya*

**Перевод:**

**Persons who are aversed to the service of Kṛṣṇa are always impure amd they uselessly waste their human form of life.(3.58)**

*surya udaya asta divasa rajani eirupe dīna masa vatsaradi gani dīna masa varsa krame āyu pūrṇa haya mṛtyu haya yamapure praktana bhunjaya*

**Перевод:**

**The sun rises and sets and thus the days and nights are passed in this way days, months, and years pass by and one’s duration of life comes to an end. After death a person goes to the abode of Yamarāja to enjoy the results of his previous karma.(3.59-60)**

*sei kṣaṇa nihi jaya vṛthā āyu vāda jeva kṣaṇe kṛṣṇa kathā sādhura samvada*

**Перевод:**

**Even a moment spent in discussing the topics of Kṛṣṇa with the devotees never goes in vain.(3.61)**

*kṛṣṇa vijaye jeva kāla vyaya kare sad vyaya āyu sei kahila vicare*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who spends time for the sake of Kṛṣṇa, know it for certain that it is the proper utilisation of one’s duration of life.(3.62)**

**Перевод:**

**A self controlled person can reside anywhere and every where. the devotees of Kṛṣṇa are satisfied wherever they reside.(3.63)**

*gṛha sakta jāna jei kare gṛha karma grhe thake kare sadā gṛha cara dharma*

**Перевод:**

**An attached householder however lives at home and engages in hosehold activities and always absorbed in household comfort only.(3.64)**

*se janāra nahe kabhu kṛṣṇa pade rati pare sikhaile na haya kṛṣṇa mati*

**Перевод:**

**Such a person can never develop attachment for the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa of his own. He cannot fir his mind on Kṛṣṇa unless trained by others.(3.65)**

*parasara śikṣā śravaṇa saṅga gune nāhi haya kṛṣṇe mati gṛha sakta jāne*

**Перевод:**

**Unless an attacked householder hears, learns and associates with devotees of Kṛṣṇa he cannot consentrate his mind on Kṛṣṇa.(3.66)**

*ajitendriya viṣayī viṣaya asvadana purah punaḥ kare tāra carbita carvana*

**Перевод:**

**The sense gratification of the uncontrolled material enjoyers is like chewing the chewed.(3.67)**

*gṛha sakta viṣayīra kabhu nahe gati śrī kṛṣṇa sevaya jarā nāhi dekhi mati*

**Перевод:**

**An attached householder who is fond of material enjoyments and has no interest for the service of Kṛṣṇa never reach the goal of life. There were many householder devotees such as Ambarīṣa Maharaja who though lived of home yet were aloof from material bondage.(3.68)**

It is stated in Santi sataka as follows “vane api daśa prabhavanti…” Has to be translated.

*tāhā dekha bhagavate kahe bhagavāna sādhu jāna grhe thāki dukha nāhi pāna*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme Lord has said in Śrīmad Bhāgavatam that the saintlu persons never meet any distress even while living at home (3.69)**

*grhe thāki kṛṣṇa karma kari harṣa māne prahara prahardha kṛṣṇa kathā alapane*

**Перевод:**

**They remain at home and happily engage in activities related to Kṛṣṇa. They spend 2/3 hours a day discussing the topics of Kṛṣṇa.(3.70)**

*tāra frha vandhana nimitta nāhi haya dīna ratri cari danda krsnake sevaya*

**Перевод:**

**Their homes do not become the xause of bodage as they serve Lord Kṛṣṇa day and night.(3.71)**

It is described in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (4.30.19) as follows

*gṛheṣv āviśatāṁ cāpi / puṁsāṁ kuśala-karmaṇām mad-vārtā-yāta-yāmānāṁ / na bandhāya gṛhā matāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are engaged in auspicious activities in devotional service certainly understand that the ultimate enjoyer or beneficiary of all activities is the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Thus when one acts, he offers the results to the Supreme Personality of Godhead and passes life always engaged in the topics of the Lord. Even though such a person may be participating in family life, he is not affected by the results of his actions.(72)**

*jitendriya jāna vāsa karu jahan tanhan kṛtārtha śrī kṛṣṇa bhakta vāsa kare janhan ?? visaya vāsanā laiyā yadi vana jaya vanete thakiya tāra visaye mana dhāya*

*ragi jāna yadi kare ranete vasati tathapi sakala dosa sangete vasati*

*grhe thāki panca indriya karaye damana nivrtta rāgera haya geha tapovana*

**One who control his five senses while living at home then his home turns into a hermitage.**

*grhi bhakta ambarīṣa ādi mahasaya vandana nimitta tāra gṛha nāhi haya ?? iha nāhi jāne mudrā visyera bhole kona rūpe dinna jauka ei mātra bole*

**Being bewildered bu material enjoyment a foolish person does not know this. He thinks “somehow or other the gas to pass his days.”**

*kintu dīna masa varsa sītā grsma yata cakra prāya sei sava phire avirata*

**Перевод:**

**But days, month, years, winter and summer come and go. In this way the wheel of time moves constantly.**

*kāla nityā rūpa hona tāra nāhi ksaya manusyera āyu mātra haraye niscaya*

**Перевод:**

**The time factor is eternal and inexhoustible. It certainly takes away a man’s duration of life.**

This is confirmed in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (2.3.17)

*āyur harati vai puṁsām udyann astaṁ ca yann asau tasyarte yat-kṣaṇo nīta uttama-śloka-vārtayā*

**Перевод:**

**Both by rising and by setting, the sun decreases the duration of life of everyone, except one who utilizes the time by discussing topics of the all-good Personality of Godhead.**

*dīna ratri prahara danda kṣaṇa ādi kari kṛṣṇa kara hīnā kāla viphala haya tari*

**Перевод:**

**A person who does not wngage in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa simply wastes his time in the form of days, nights, hours and month.**

*sei dīna deena haya daridra tulya mani kṛṣṇa karma līlā guṇa jave nāhi śuni*

**Перевод:**

**I consider that day as most inauspecious and miserable when I do not hear about the trancendental qualities and pastimes o Kṛṣṇa or engage in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa.**

*sei kṣaṇa ksina haya jala vimba prāya kṛṣṇa sambandha vine jeva kṣaṇa jaya*

**Перевод:**

**That moment is uselessly wasted just like a bubble when one remains without Kṛṣṇa consciousness.**

*asta prahara madhye na smare jabe hari prahara prahara tulya jāniha tahari*

**Перевод:**

**A person who daes not remember Kṛṣṇa within twenty four hours know it for certain that these hours are like beating for him.**

*kṛṣṇa karma kṛṣṇa varta jabe nāhi haya sei sava jāniha bhai vṛthā āyu ksaya*

**Перевод:**

**O brother, the time that is spent without descussing the topics of Kṛṣṇa and engaging in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa is certainly wasting the human form of life.**

It is described in Rudra Yamala as follows “dinadeenam ksanam…” has to be translated.

*kṛṣṇa kathā vimukha janera āyu vṛthā vṛkṣa gaṇa vahu kāla vanci rahe yathā*

**Перевод:**

**The duration of life for persons who ate aversed to hearing the topics of Kṛṣṇa is useless just like the trees who live long lives.**

*śva-viḍ-varāhoṣṭra-kharaiḥ saṁstutaḥ puruṣaḥ paśuḥ na yat-karṇa-pathopeto jātu nāma gadāgrajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

*śva-viḍ-varāhoṣṭra-kharaiḥ saṁstutaḥ puruṣaḥ paśuḥ na yat-karṇa-pathopeto jātu nāma gadāgrajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Men who are like dogs, hogs, camels and asses praise those men who never listen to the transcendental pastimes of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the deliverer from evils.**

*sei lokera vṛthā janma naradhama sei purāṇa puruṣa kṛṣṇa naradhila jei*

**Перевод:**

**The life of a person who has not worshipped Kṛṣṇa the primeval Lord is useless such a person is lowest among mankind.**

*bhagavata purāṇa jei na kaila śravaṇa brāhmaṇa vaiṣṇava mukhe na kaila havana sei loka naradhama vṛthā janma tāra ei ta kahila kathā puranera sāra*

**Перевод:**

**One who has not heard Śrīmad bhāgavatam purāṇa and did not arrange to perform fire sacrifice by qualified Btahmanas and vaisnavas is certainly is a fallen soul. His life is useless – This is essence of the puranas.**

It is described in the cripture as follows “yairna srutam bhāgavatam…” (has to be translated)

*kṛṣṇa kathā śravaṇādi rahita jeva jāna vyartha indriya tāra karaha śravaṇa*

*sarvendriya thakita sei indriya vihina jei indriya nāhi haya kṛṣṇa sambandha adhina*

**Перевод:**

**The frog makes crocking sound from his hole as if to invite snake to came and eat him.**

*tena mate puruṣa nānā grāmya katha gaṇe ātma āyu vṛthā neya naṣṭa kare yame*

**Перевод:**

**The hands of a person that are not engaged in the service of Kṛṣṇa are useless and as good as blind.**

**Перевод:**

**The eyes of such a person are compared to the eyes on the peackok feather; They are useless because they do not see the form of the Lord.**

*kṛṣṇa kṣetra sādhu tīrtha śrī guru darśana jeva jāna nāhi jāna thakite caraṇa tāhāra caraṇa dui sacala vrksopama indriya thakite haya anindriya sama*

**Перевод:**

**Перевод:**
