In Śrīmad bhāgavatam (2.3.20-23) where sage Saunaka speaks to Suta Goswami as follows:

*bile batorukrama-vikramān ye na śṛṇvataḥ karṇa-puṭe narasya jihvāsatī dārdurikeva sūta na copagāyaty urugāya-gāthāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who has not listened to the messages about the prowess and marvelous acts of the Personality of Godhead and has not sung or chanted loudly the worthy songs about the Lord is to be considered to possess earholes like the holes of snakes and a tongue like the tongue of a frog.**

*bhāraḥ paraṁ paṭṭa-kirīṭa-juṣṭam apy uttamāṅgaṁ na namen mukundam śāvau karau no kurute saparyāṁ harer lasat-kāñcana-kaṅkaṇau vā*

**Перевод:**

**The upper portion of the body, though crowned with a silk turban, is only a heavy burden if not bowed down before the Personality of Godhead who can award mukti \[freedom\]. And the hands, though decorated with glittering bangles, are like those of a dead man if not engaged in the service of the Personality of Godhead Hari.**

*barhāyite te nayane narāṇāṁ liṅgāni viṣṇor na nirīkṣato ye pādau nṛṇāṁ tau druma-janma-bhājau kṣetrāṇi nānuvrajato harer yau*

**Перевод:**

**The eyes which do not look at the symbolic representations of the Personality of Godhead Viṣṇu \[His forms, name, quality, etc.\] are like those printed on the plumes of the peacock, and the legs which do not move to the holy places \[where the Lord is remembered\] are considered to be like tree trunks.**

*īvañ chavo bhāgavatāṅghri-reṇuṁ na jātu martyo ’bhilabheta yas tu śrī-viṣṇu-padyā manujas tulasyāḥ śvasañ chavo yas tu na veda gandham*

**Перевод:**

**The person who has not at any time received the dust of the feet of the Lord’s pure devotee upon his head is certainly a dead body. And the person who has never exerienced the aroma of the tulasī leaves from the lotus feet of the Lord is also a dead body, although breathing.**

*ityadi prakare mana sikhaila tāre bhajiya govinda pada kṛtārtha kara more*

**Перевод:**

**O my mind, I have thus explained you everything in detail. Now please worship the lotus feet of Govinda and satisfy me.**

*bhakti kari bhaja hari sakala chadiya kāya mana vākya niṣṭhā sudṛḍha kariyā*

**Перевод:**

**Abandon everything and worship Hari with love and devotion serve Him by your body, mind and speech with resolute determination.**

*adhyātmika jñāna yoga sankhya jñāna yata karma yoga kriyā yoge haiyā virata*

**Перевод:**

**Refrain from mental speculation discussing sankhya philosophy, fruitive activities ad worldly activities.**

*bhakti bhāve bhakta vasya haya bhagavāna uddhave kahena kṛṣṇa bhagavate pramana*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme Lord becomes controlled by His devotees love and devotion. This is confirmed by Kṛṣṇa to Uddhava in Śrīmad bhāgavatam.**

*kṛṣṇa kahena śuna uddhava priya mora bhakti bhāve vasibhuta āmi hoi tora*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa says my dear Uddhava, I am fully controled by your love and devotion.**

*yogera sadhane āmi yata vāsa noi sankhya yoga jñāna tapasya ādi kai e saba sadhane vāsa korite se ta nāre bhakti bhāve bhakta gaṇa vāsa kore more*

**Перевод:**

**I am not controlled by the proccess of mystic yoga, sankhya yoga, giving charity and performance of austerities. Rather my devotees simply control me through love and devotion.**

This is described in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (11.14.20) as follows

*na sādhayati māṁ yogo na sāṅkhyaṁ dharma uddhava na svādhyāyas tapas tyāgo yathā bhaktir mamorjitā*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Uddhava, the unalloyed devotional service rendered to Me by My devotees brings Me under their control. I cannot be thus controlled by those engaged in mystic yoga, Sāṅkhya philosophy, pious work, Vedic study, austerity or renunciation.**

*ei kathā kahena bahama saksate bhagavane dainya kori apanake prabhura caraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahmā has clearly said with humility to the supreme Lord about this.**

*mukti hetu bhakti chādi jñāna upasye tāra siddhi nāhi hoi klesa bhagi haye*

**Перевод:**

**One who rejects devotional service and cultivates impersonal knowledge to achieve liberation, can never attain perfection. But simply suffers distress.**

*sarva kalyanera hetu bhaktira sādhana tāhā chādi mukti hetu jñāna nista hana dhanya tyāga kori puna parama yatane tusa agra bhane tandula karaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**A person who becomes attached to impersonal knowledge to get liberation while giving up devotional service which is the cause of all auspiciousness is simply beating the husks in order to get rice.**

*dhanya vinā tandula prapti agaraya nāhi hana ata eva klesa bhagi srama akarana*

**Перевод:**

**One can not get rice by beating the husk. Therefore it will simply result inuseless hard labor.**

*bhakti vinā kṛṣṇa priya kona karma naya sankhya jñāna ādi kori jeva sastre kāya*

**Перевод:**

**No other activities including sankhya and jñāna are as dear to Kṛṣṇa as devotional service.**

This is stated in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (10.14.4)

*śreyaḥ-sṛtiṁ bhaktim udasya te vibho kliśyanti ye kevala-bodha-labdhaye teṣām asau kleśala eva śiṣyate nānyad yathā sthūla-tuṣāvaghātinām*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Lord, devotional service unto You is the best path for self-realization. If someone gives up that path and engages in the cultivation of speculative knowledge, he will simply undergo a troublesome process and will not achieve his desired result. As a person who beats an empty husk of wheat cannot get grain, one who simply speculates cannot achieve self-realization. His only gain is trouble.**

As well as in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (1.5.12)

*naiṣkarmyam apy acyuta-bhāva-varjitaṁ na śobhate jñānam alaṁ nirañjanam kutaḥ punaḥ śaśvad abhadram īśvare na cārpitaṁ karma yad apy akāraṇam*

**Перевод:**

**Knowledge of self-realization, even though free from all material affinity, does not look well if devoid of a conception of the Infallible \[God\]. What, then, is the use of fruitive activities, which are naturally painful from the very beginning and transient by nature, if they are not utilized for the devotional service of the Lord?**

*kṛṣṇa priya bhakti vine anya karma nānā dvija dharma daiva kara ādi acharana dhana vyaya tapah śruti teja paurusadi kṛṣṇa priya nāhi hoija yoga bala buddhi*

**Перевод:**

**Prescribed brahminical duties, worshiping the demigods, giving charity, undergoing austerities, studying vedas, physical strengs, mystic perfection, great influence and intelligence can not make kṛṣṇa happy. He is only pleased by devotional service.**

It is described in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (6.3.32)

*śṛṇvatāṁ gṛṇatāṁ vīryāṇy uddāmāni harer muhuḥ yathā sujātayā bhaktyā śuddhyen nātmā vratādibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who constantly hears and chants the holy name of the Lord and hears and chants about His activities can very easily attain the platform of pure devotional service, which can cleanse the dirt from one’s heart. One cannot achieve such purification merely by observing vows and performing Vedic ritualistic ceremonies.**

*jñāna tapa yajña sauca vrata ācaraṇa bhakti bene ithe kṛṣṇa trpta nāhi hana*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Kṛṣṇa is not satisfied through mental speculations, austerity, performance of sacrifices, cleanliness, and observing vows. But he is satisfied through devotional service.**

*śrī kṛṣṇa prinana hetu bhakti ārādhana bhakti bene tapasyādi saba virambana*

**Перевод:**

**Cultivation of devotional service is meant for pleasing Kṛṣṇa. A part from devotional service all other austerities are simply disturbance.**

It is stated in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (7.7.51-52)

*nālaṁ dvijatvaṁ devatvam ṛṣitvaṁ vāsurātmajāḥ prīṇanāya mukundasya na vṛttaṁ na bahu-jñatā na dānaṁ na tapo nejyā na śaucaṁ na vratāni ca prīyate ’malayā bhaktyā harir anyad viḍambanam*

**Перевод:**

**My dear friends, O sons of the demons, you cannot please the Supreme Personality of Godhead by becoming perfect brāhmaṇas, demigods or great saints or by becoming perfectly good in etiquette or vast learning. None of these qualifications can awaken the pleasure of the Lord. Nor by charity, austerity, sacrifice, cleanliness or vows can one satisfy the Lord. The Lord is pleased only if one has unflinching, unalloyed devotion to Him. Without sincere devotional service, everything is simply a show.**

*bhakti bhāva sama keha nahe kṛṣṇa priya jñāna tapah puṇya vrata dāna dharmadaya*

**Перевод:**

**There is nothing more dear to Kṛṣṇa than devotional service. Speculative knowledge, austerity, pious activities, vows and charity can not please Him.**

This is stated by Supreme Lord to Arjuna in Padma Purāṇa

*na ca bhakti samam jnanam na ca bhakti samam tapah na ca bhakti samam dhyānaṁ na ca bhakti samam vrata nāhi bhakti samam janam nāsti bhakti sama gatiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Impersonal knowledge, performance of austerities, performing pious activities, results of fruitive activities, meditation, observing vows, charity are not equal to the pure devotional service. They can not take one to the supreme destination.**

*dharma artha kama mokṣa puruṣārtha sādhana bhakti kāche ei saba trina tulya hana*

**Перевод:**

**The four objectives of life such as religiousness, economic development, sense gratification and liberation a like straws in front of devotional service.**

*kṛṣṇa kathamrta sindhu magna bhakts gaṇa trina tulya catur varga phala tāra kaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees are merged I the nectarian acean of Kṛṣṇa Kathā. They consider the result of four objectives of life as insignificant straws.**

In his Bhavartha Dipika camentary on Śrīmad bhāgavatam (10.87.41) Sridhara Swami quotes the following verse. This verse composed by himself in glorification of Śrī Nṛsiṁhadeva. “ ta kathamrta pathodhou…”has to be translated.

*bhakti sana keha naya caturvarga phala bhakti vine bhakta ganera anya nāhi bala*

**Перевод:**

**The result of the four objectives of life such as religiousness, economic development etc. can not be compared to devotional service to the Lord. The devotional service os the only strength of the devotees.**

*sakala puruṣārtha phala mukti pada hana hena mukti na vacay kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**The ultimate result of all objectives is liberation the devotees of Kṛṣṇa do not desire such liberation.**

*kṛṣṇa kahena yadi mukti diye bhakta gaṇe' bhakti vine mukti tāra na kare grahane*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Kṛṣṇa says when I ofer liveration to my devotees they do not accept it. Rather they want devotion to me.**

It is stated in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (3.29.13)

*sālokya-sārṣṭi-sāmīpya-sārūpyaikatvam apy uta dīyamānaṁ na gṛhṇanti vinā mat-sevanaṁ janāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A pure devotee does not accept any kind of liberation—sālokya, sārṣṭi, sāmīpya, sārūpya or ekatva—even though they are offered by the Supreme Personality of Godhead.**

*sevā vine mukti pada bhakta na vancaye brahmā vaivarta granthe vivariya kahe*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā Vaivarta Purāṇa elaborately describes that the devotees of the Lord do not aspire for liberation.**

*bhakti mukti bheda tāhe karaha śravaṇa mukti haya caturvidha tāhāte varṇana*

**Перевод:**

**The difference between devotional service and liberation is described therein. According to that description the liberation is of four kinds.**

*viṣṇu loka vasati āra tatsvrupa dhāraṇa samipa vasi āra brahme lina hana ei mata caturvidha muktira lakṣaṇa janma mṛtyu rahita haya muta jei jāna*

**Перевод:**

**To ive on the same planer as the Lord, to possess same opulence as the Lord, to live as an associate of the Lord and to merge into the existence of the Lord are the four kinds of liberation. A liberated person is fee from birth and death.**

*jarā vyadhi nāhi tathe dukha śoka roga kona klesa nāhi tāhe parananda bhoga*

**Перевод:**

**A liberated doul does not undergo any distress, lamentation, old age and disease. He only enjoys spiritual bliss.**

*hena mukti sukha vañca na kare bhakta gaṇa sevya sevaka bhāva yathe nāhi āna*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees of the Lord do not desire such happiness of liberation on the platform of liberation however there is no conception of served and servant.**

*mukta haile sevā sukha na pāya tāhāte sena sukha bhakti phala satata bhakate*

**Перевод:**

**On the platform of liberation thereis no happiness due to service. The devotees constantly enjoy the happiness of service which is the fruit of devotional service.**

*bhakti mukti ei bheda purāṇe lakṣaṇa bhakta gaṇa sevananda karaye prarthana*

**Перевод:**

**These are the different symptoms of devotional service and liberation. The devotees always pray for the happiness of devotional service.**

It is stated in Brahmā Vaivarta Purāṇa “ salokya sarsti sarupya…”

Elsewhere in Brahmā Vaivarta Purāṇa it says “bhaktir bhagavataḥ sevā…”

*kaivalya mukti bhakta na kare vāsanā kṛṣṇa dileha nāhi nena dasya bhāva vinā*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees never desire the liberation of merging into the existence of the Lord. Even if Kṛṣṇa offers such liberation, the devotees do not accept it, rather they pray for His servitorship.**

Lord Kṛṣṇa spoke to Uddhava in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (11.20.34)

*na kiñcit sādhavo dhīrā bhaktā hy ekāntino mama vāñchanty api mayā dattaṁ kaivalyam apunar-bhavam*

**Перевод:**

**Because My devotees possess saintly behavior and deep intelligence, they completely dedicate themselves to Me and do not desire anything besides Me. Indeed, even if I offer them liberation from birth and death, they do not accept it.**

It is also described in Nārāyaṇa Vyudha stava as follows “ na kincit sadhavo…” has to be translated.

*nirvana kaivalya mukti bhaktera tuccha hana sevā niṣṭhā salokyadi dusta ati nānā*

**Перевод:**

**Merging into Brahman is never accepteble to the devotees. But the other forms of liberation are not detrimental.**

It is stated in the scriptures as follows “atra tyajya tayaibakta…” has to be transated.

*ei to kahila bheda bhakti mukti dui bhakti phale kona gati tāhā śuna koi*

**Перевод:**

**Thus I have described the difference between devotional service and liberation. Now please hear about the goal one achies as a result of devotional service.**

*bhaktagana bhakti krame pāya kṛṣṇa gati sevā niṣṭhā samipaya rūpe bhagavati gati*

**Перевод:**

**By cultivation of devotional service the devotees attain the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. They become associates of the Lord.and engage in His eternal service.**

*ity ādi vidhae kari māne samvadhana bhakti bhāve kara sevā sei nanda nandana*

**Перевод:**

**In this way I have pacified my mind and preached him to serve the son of Nanda Maharaja with love and devotion.**

*bhakti haye tāhe jāni dvividha prakara sakama niskama bhakti ei to vicāra*

**Перевод:**

**There are two types devotional service namely devotional service with material desires and devotional service without material desires.**

*drdha kori bhakti bhāve bhaje bhagavāna ubhaya bhaktete bhakta vañca rūpe pan*

**Перевод:**

**Those who worship the Lord with determination whether they have material desires or no material desires receive their respective desired result.**

*jarā je vāsanā tāra taiche siddha haya dharma artha kama oksa je jāna vancaya*

**Перевод:**

**According to one’s desire ane achive perfection. If people desire religiousness, economic development, sense gratification and liberation they can achieve so by their endeavours.**

*kami bhaktera kama pūrṇa bhakti hoite hana niskama bhaktera phal govinda sevana*

**Перевод:**

**All material desires of a devotee sho possess material desires are fulfilled by the execution of devotional service. The result of unmotivated devotional service is the service of Govinda.**

This is confirmed in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (2.3.10)

*akāmaḥ sarva-kāmo vā mokṣa-kāma udāra-dhīḥ tīvreṇa bhakti-yogena yajeta puruṣaṁ param*

**Перевод:**

**A person who has broader intelligence, whether he be full of all material desire, without any material desire, or desiring liberation, must by all means worship the supreme whole, the Personality of Godhead.**

*yajnadi vidhane je vā bhaje bhagavān svargadi abhilāṣa kori hoiya sakama veda vidhi ācārya puṇya karma kori karaye vibhoga sei āmāra ragari*

**Перевод:**

**If a person desires to enjoy the heavenly pleasures and worhips the Lord with performance of sacrifices and accumulate piety through vedic ritualistic ceremonies, certainly enjoys happiness in the heaven which is inhabited by immortal beings.**

*svargadi kaorahe bhoga puṇya yavat rāya puṇya ksaye puna asi saṁsāre janma haya*

**Перевод:**

**On the strength of pious activity one enjoys happiness in the heavenly planet. But when his pious activities are worn out he return to the material world.**

*śubhāśubha karma phale janma mṛtyu haye parambara gatayata nija karma saye*

**Перевод:**

**According to one’s karma one takes birth in a higher or lower species. It is according to karma that one wonders through out the universe.**

*śrī kṛṣṇa bhajete nahe yamera sasana śubhāśubha karma phale karaye bhramana*

**Перевод:**

**If one worships Śrī Kṛṣṇa he is not subjected to be punished by Yamarāja. One wonders simply as a result of his pious and impious activities.**

It is stated in Bhagavad Gītā (9.20-21)

*te taṁ bhuktvā svarga-lokaṁ viśālaṁ kṣīṇe puṇye martya-lokaṁ viśanti evaṁ trayī-dharmam anuprapannā gatāgataṁ kāma-kāmā labhante*

**Перевод:**

**When they have thus enjoyed vast heavenly sense pleasure and the results of their pious activities are exhausted, they return to this mortal planet again. Thus those who seek sense enjoyment by adhering to the principles of the three Vedas achieve only repeated birth and death.**

*ananyāś cintayanto māṁ ye janāḥ paryupāsate teṣāṁ nityābhiyuktānāṁ yoga-kṣemaṁ vahāmy aham*

**Перевод:**

**But those who always worship Me with exclusive devotion, meditating on My transcendental form—to them I carry what they lack, and I preserve what they have.**

*sakama bhajane haya gata yata jāne janma mṛtyu garba vāsa sukha sukha śuni*

**Перевод:**

**By worshiping the Lord with material desires one continue to wonder throughout the universes in various species of life. It is also heard that such a person undergoes repeated birth and death and the miseries of living in the womb.**

*abhilāṣa tyāga kari prīte bhaja hari tāra janma nāhi haya saṁsāra bhitari*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore one should give up all material desires and worship hari with love and devotion. Then such a person will no longer take birth in this world.**

*nimita rahita bhakti haya sarvottama siddhadika pada tāra keha nahe sama*

**Перевод:**

**The devotional service which is devoid of any tige of material deseres is the topmast. Nothing including mystic perfections are equal to it.**

*niskama bhaktera phale haya kṛṣṇa prapti jivopadhi naṣṭa kari haya kṛṣṇa gati*

**Перевод:**

**By executing pure devotional service a living entities material designation are destroyed and he achieves the Kṛṣṇa the goal of life.**

*bhagavati mukti haya niskama sadhane sevā sukha pāya sei śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**By practicing pure devotional service one becomes an eternal associate of the Lord. He obtains happiness by serving the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

*bhukta anna jirna ena kore jatharanali sei rase kore pusta indriya sakale*

**Перевод:**

**Just as the fire in the stomach digestes the food that one eats and the substance of this food nourishes all the senses.**

*taice bhakti jivopadhi kori vinasane bhagavati gati dena sevā parayane*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly the devotional service destroys material designation of a living entity and awards him the service and position of an associate of the Lord.**

*niskaa bhaktite haya kṛṣṇa anande sthiti janma mṛtyu garbhavasa nāhi gata gati*

**Перевод:**

**When ane engages in pure devotional service he floats in the ocean of bliss. He does not undergo the cycle of birth and death and he is not forced to live in the womb.**

It is stated in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (3.25.32)

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca devānāṁ guṇa-liṅgānām ānuśravika-karmaṇām sattva evaika-manaso vṛttiḥ svābhāvikī tu yā animittā bhāgavatī bhaktiḥ siddher garīyasī*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Kapila said: The senses are symbolic representations of the demigods, and their natural inclination is to work under the direction of the Vedic injunctions. As the senses are representatives of the demigods, so the mind is the representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. The mind’s natural duty is to serve. When that service spirit is engaged in devotional service to the Personality of Godhead, without any motive, that is far better even than salvation.**

It is also stated in śāstras “jarayadi sosayati….” has to be translated.

And in Kasi Kāṇḍa “na cyavanti he tad bhakto…” has to be translated.

*a brahmā bhuvanera haya janma mṛtyu gati kṛṣṇa pada pajle puna nahiko avrtti*

**Перевод:**

**From the highest planet brahmāloka down to the lowest, all the places are full of births and deaths. But if one obtains the supreme planet of Kṛṣṇa does not take birth again.**

It is stated in the Bhagavad Gītā (8.16) as follows

*ā-brahma-bhuvanāl lokāḥ punar āvartino ’rjuna mām upetya tu kaunteya punar janma na vidyate*

**Перевод:**

**From the highest planet in the material world down to the lowest, all are places of misery wherein repeated birth and death take place. But one who attains to My abode, O son of Kuntī, never takes birth again.**

*kona bhakta bhaje hari viṣaya lagiya vise jena yatna kore amṛta cadiya*

**Перевод:**

**When a devotee worships Hari for the sake of material enjoyment then he is rejecting nectar and endeavoring for poison.**

*kṛṣṇa ānanda sukha amṛta na jāne se jāna viṣaya vāsanā vise karaye yatana*

**Перевод:**

**One who does not know about the nectar of bliss derived from the service of Kṛṣṇa, simply endeavors for the poisonous material enjoyment.**

*dayalu svabhāva kṛṣṇa karuṇa hrdaya sei sevakere prabhu sa karuṇa haya*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Kṛṣṇa is naturally merciul and kind hearted. He becomes compassionate to such servants.**

*viṣaya na diyā dena nija pada dvanda nirmala hrdaya kote parama ānanda*

**Перевод:**

**Instead of giving material enjoyments the Lord awards the service of His lotus feet. In this way the hearts of His servants become purified and joyful.**

*kṛṣṇa krpa haile ghuce saṁsāra vāsanā tāhāte siddhanta śuna prakrite upama*

**Перевод:**

**By the mercy of Kṛṣṇa one’s material desire are destroyed. Now please hear the conclusion through the following example.**

*andha bālaka yena kariyā rodhana ātma icchaya kore mrttika bhakṣaṇa tāhā dekhi tāra pitā mrttika phelaiya sarkara sandes deya tāra mukhe laiyā*

**Перевод:**

**Just as a blind boy cries and eats clay out of his ow sill. Seeing this his father takes away the clay and feeds him sugar candy and milk sweets.**

*sarkarara svada pāiyā mrttika tyāga kote pitā sthāne sei dravya cahe bare bare*

**Перевод:**

**After tasting sugar candy the boy rejects the clay and he repeatedly asks for the same from his father.**

*taice bhaktera pitā haya bhagavān nija pada padma tāre dite yatna pāna*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly the supreme Lord is the father of Hi devotees. Therefore He carefully awards them the service of His lotus feet.**

As stated in the scripture

sakama api bhajatama (has to be translated)

*sarva kama na chādi seve bhagavān kṛṣṇa daśa bali jarā māne abhimana kona na mage bara mukti ādi kari narayana vyudha stave dekhana vicari*

**Перевод:**

**A person who proudly considers himself a servant of Kṛṣṇa, gives up all material desires and worships the Lord. He does not pray for any benedictions such as liberation. One should refer the following verse from Nārāyaṇa Vyuha stava.**

As stated there

*na dharmam kamam artham vā kṛṣṇa dasya pada bime rahe kichu vara sarva śāstre ei kathā kahilena dara*

**Перевод:**

**There is nothing greater than the servitorship of Kṛṣṇa. All the scriptures establishes this fact.**

*ye haya kṛṣṇera dasa kāya mana prane sei haya pujaniya ei tribhuvane*

**Перевод:**

**One who becomes a servant of Kṛṣṇa with one’s body, mind and life, is alone worshipable in the three worlds.**

*acara anacaradi smṛti ukta yata nāhi kare anya pūjā bidhi abhimata*

**Перевод:**

**Various prescriptions and prohibitions as well as worship of the demigods, that are described in the smṛtis are not followed by the unalloyed devotees of Kṛṣṇa.**

*deva ganera homa rsi tarpana bhute balidana pitri sraddhadi kāraṇa smṛti ukta nityā kriyā panca mahā yajña deva yajña pitri yajña āra bhauta yajña brahmā yajña narayajna ādi kriyā yata grihastera prati ei bidhi śāstra mata*

**Перевод:**

**Performing fire sacrifice to please the demigods, offering oblations to the sages, animal sacrifices, offering oblations to the forefathers, as well as the five sacrifices to please the demigods, forefathers, ghosts, Brahmā and other living entities mentioned on the smṛtis are all prescribed for the attached householders.**

*kṛṣṇa dasa haya jīva kṛṣṇa parayana sei yadi ei sava na kare arcana devarsi pitri ganera nāhi haya rini pratyavaya kṛṣṇa tāra ghucana āpani*

**Перевод:**

**If a servant of Kṛṣṇa who is attached to the service of Kṛṣṇa does not perform any of these activities, then the sin that is incured by not doing so is totally destroyed personally by Kṛṣṇa and such a person does not remain indebted to the demigods, sages, and forefathers.**

It is stated in Śrīmad bhāgavatam 11.5.41.

*devarṣi-bhūtāpta-nṛṇāṁ pitṝṇāṁ na kiṅkaro nāyam ṛṇī ca rājan sarvātmanā yaḥ śaraṇaṁ śaraṇyaṁ gato mukundaṁ parihṛtya kartam*

**Перевод:**

**O King, one who has given up all material duties and has taken full shelter of the lotus feet of Mukunda, who offers shelter to all, is not indebted to the demigods, great sages, ordinary living beings, relatives, friends, mankind or even one’s forefathers who have passed away. Since all such classes of living entities are part and parcel of the Supreme Lord, one who has surrendered to the Lord’s service has no need to serve such persons separately.**

*kṛṣṇa bhakta kene na puje anya deva gaṇa gitaya pramana śuna prabhura vacana*

**Перевод:**

**Why do the devotees of Kṛṣṇa not worship the demigods? Please hear the words of the Lord which is evident in Bhagavad Gītā**

*deva yajaka loka devata pujiya devaloke vasati kare tatpara haiyā*

**Перевод:**

**By worshipping the demigods the demigod worshipers reside happily in the kingdom of the demigods.**

*pitrloka yājī haya pitrloka gati bhuta gaṇa yajiya haya bhuta loka prapti*

**Перевод:**

**Those who worship the forefathers reach the planets of the forefathers and those who worship the ghosts live with the ghosts.**

*kṛṣṇa kahe jīva more bhaje drdha māne svasvata vasati kare sei mora sthāne*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa says – who ever worships me with determination, eternally lives with me.**

*mora bhakta āmā bahi kahu nāhi jaya arjune kahila kṛṣṇa bhagavat gitaya*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa confirmed this to Arjuna that my devotees do not go anywhere but come to me.**

As found on Bhagavat Gītā 9.25

*yānti deva-vratā devān / pitṝn yānti pitṛ-vratāḥ bhūtāni yānti bhūtejyā / yānti mad-yājino ’pi mām*

**Перевод:**

**Those who worship the demigods will take birth among the demigods; those who worship the ancestors go to the ancestors; those who worship ghosts and spirits will take birth among such beings; and those who worship Me will live with Me.**

*śraddhā kari anya deva karaye yajana sehata sādhana haya śrī kṛṣṇa arcana*

**Перевод:**

**when a person worships the demigods with faith, it is also performed as a worship of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

*ei kathā prabhu kahe gītā bhagavate avidhi bhajana sei nahe vidhi mate*

**Перевод:**

**It is stated by the Lord in both Bhagavad Gītā and Śrīmad Bhāgavatam that such a worship is done without proper understanding.**

*avidhi sabdera vyākhyā svamira likhana mukti bine anya phala devatantare hana*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīdhara Swami explains the word avidhi as follows that the demigods are capable of awarding other fruits except the liberation.**

*tri varga phaladata haya deva gaṇe' mukti pada nāhi haya kṛṣṇa sevā vine.*

**Перевод:**

**the demigods can award the three objectives of life namely religiousness, economic development and sense gratification, but without the service of Kṛṣṇa no oe can attain liberation.**

*saksat sevate āra devatantara sevane vaisamya tāhāre kahi kara avadhane*

**Перевод:**

**Let me explain the difference between the direct service to the Lord and the service to the demigods. Please listen attentively.**

It is described in B.g. (9.23.)

*ye ’py anya-devatā-bhaktā / yajante śraddhayānvitāḥ te ’pi mām eva kaunteya / yajanty avidhi-pūrvakam*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are devotees of other gods and who worship them with faith actually worship only Me, O son of Kuntī, but they do so in a wrong way.**

*saksat bhajane haya kṛṣṇa loka gati devata yajiya haya devaloka prapti*

**Перевод:**

**By directly worshiping the Lord one attains the abode of Kṛṣṇa whereas by worshiping the demigods one reaches the planets of the demigods.**

*sei sevā devalokera haya vināśana ataeva brahmādi devera achaye patana*

**Перевод:**

**The planets of the demigods are in due course of time. Therefore the demigods headed by Brahmā are subjected to death.**

*ataeva vinasi phala dekhi deva gaṇe' tave ta sabhāra sevā kibā prayojane*

**Перевод:**

**So, it is a fact that the venedictions awarded by the demigods are temporary. Hence what is need of worshiping them?**

*kṛṣṇa saksat sevā avinasi phala paramānanda nityā sukha nityānanda sthala*

**Перевод:**

**But the benedictions received by directly serving Kṛṣṇa is eternal, full of bliss and source of ecstasy.**

This is confirmed in B.G. 7.23.

*antavat tu phalaṁ teṣāṁ tad bhavaty alpa-medhasām devān deva-yajo yānti mad-bhaktā yānti mām api*

**Перевод:**

**Men of small intelligence worship the demigods, and their fruits are limited and temporary. Those who worship the demigods go to the planets of the demigods, but My devotees ultimately reach My supreme planet.**

In his commentary o this verse Sridhara Swamipada has stated as follows

(yadyapi sarva api) has to be translated.

*kṛṣṇera dasera ei kahila prastuti sarva tyāga kari karo govinda bhakati*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the glories of the servant of Kṛṣṇa. Therefore give up everything and engage in devotional service to Govinda.**

*tavat karma kare nāhi kare tyāga yadavadhi kṛṣṇa name nahe anurāga*

**Перевод:**

**One should cotinue with one's activities until one develops attachment for chanting the holy names of Kṛṣṇa.**

*kṛṣṇa kathā sravanadau jabe haya mati sarva karma tyāga haya kahila jukati*

**Перевод:**

**As soon as one develops faith in gearing the topics of Kṛṣṇa he should abandon all varieties of activities. This is the conclusion.**

It is stated in Śrīmad bhāgavatam 11.20. 9 where in Kṛṣṇa speakes to Uddhava

*tāvat karmāṇi kurvīta na nirvidyeta yāvatā mat-kathā-śravaṇādau vā śraddhā yāvan na jāyate*

**Перевод:**

**As long as one is not satiated by fruitive activity and has not awakened his taste for devotional service by śravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ viṣṇoḥ \[SB 7.5.23\] one has to act according to the regulative principles of the Vedic injunctions.**

*vidhi avidhe kari śāstra mata yata sevanistha jāna jīva śrī kṛṣṇa bhakata sakala karaye tyāga dharma dharma vidhi kāya māne kṛṣṇa sevā yāra nira vādī sei ta uttama bhakra kahe bhagavān ekadase bhagavate tāhāra pramana*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee of Kṛṣṇa who is fixed in devotional service gives up all rules and regulations mentioned in the scriptures, gives up all religious and irreligious activities and constantly worships Kṛṣṇa with his body and mind is a first class devotee.**

This is confirmed in Śrīmad bhāgavatam (11.11.32.) by the Lord himself.

*ājñāyaivaṁ guṇān doṣān mayādiṣṭān api svakān dharmān santyajya yaḥ sarvān māṁ bhajeta sa tu sattamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He perfectly understands that the ordinary religious duties prescribed by Me in various Vedic scriptures possess favorable qualities that purify the performer, and he knows that neglect of such duties constitutes a discrepancy in one’s life. Having taken complete shelter at My lotus feet, however, a saintly person ultimately renounces such ordinary religious duties and worships Me alone. He is thus considered to be the best among all living entities.**

*dharma tyāga karile kaha vikarma jāni haya viakrmete haya jāni papadi sañcaya*

**Перевод:**

**It is a fact that by giving pu religious duties one oncurs sin and by engaging I hostile activities one accumulates sinful ractions.**

*heno cintā na kariha kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇa kāya māne jīva kare śrī kṛṣṇa cintana bhaktera hrdaye kṛṣṇa pravesa kariyā viakrma tāhāra khaṇḍe sadaya haiyā*

**Перевод:**

**Do not worry, O devotees of Kṛṣṇa. One who thinks of Kṛṣṇa with ones body and mind Kṛṣṇa enters into the heart of such a devotee and mercifully destoyes all his sinfull reactions.**

It is described in Śrīmad bhāgavatam 11.5.42.

*sva-pāda-mūlam bhajataḥ priyasya tyaktānya-bhāvasya hariḥ pareśaḥ vikarma yac cotpatitaṁ kathañcid dhunoti sarvaṁ hṛdi sanniviṣṭaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who has thus given up all other engagements and has taken full shelter at the lotus feet of Hari, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, is very dear to the Lord. Indeed, if such a surrendered soul accidentally commits some sinful activity, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who is seated within everyone’s heart, immediately takes away the reaction to such sin.**

*bhakti aṅga akarahe haya pratyavay ei kathā bhakti śāstre korelena sthaye*

**Перевод:**

**It is established in the devotional scripture that by failing to cultivating limbs of devotional service one becomes sinfull.**

*karmanga na karile bhakta dusta naya bhakti phale bhaktera sakala sulabha haya*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee is not at fault if he does not perform limbs of fruitive activities because a devotee acheives everything as a result of devotional service.**

It is stated in Śrīmad bhāgavatam 1.2.63.

?????

*śrī govinda pada dvandam praṇamya sirasa guruṁ vaisnavan bhagavat prasthan natva ca likhitam mayā*

**Перевод:**

**I offer my respectfull obeisances of the lotus feet of Śrī Govinda, my spiritual master and to the vaisnavas who are very dear to the Lord. It is their mercy only that I am writting this book.**

*śrī caitanya nityānanda advaita ācārya abhirāma sundarānanda mahānta gaṇa arya*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Śrī Caitanya Śrī Nityānanda and Śrī Advaita Ācārya. All glories to Abhirāma Thakur, Sundarananada Thakur and all the devoties of Kṛṣṇa.**

*śrī parnigopala prabhu gopāla caraṇa katara dekhiyā diha caraṇe sarana*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Śrī Parnigopala Prabhu and all glories to Gopāla Caraṇa Prabhu. I am very distressed please give me shelter at your lotus feet.**

*kṛṣṇa bhakti rasa kadamba śravaṇe ullasa katare varnila śrī nayanananda daśa*

**Перевод:**

**By hearing Kṛṣṇa Bhakti Rasa Kadamba one will become blissfull. Thus Śrī Nayanananda dasa describes this book.**

Sri Krsna Bhakti Rasa Kadamba.

## Chapter IV

## Summary

All glories to Lord Krsna.

### Text 1

*kaiśoram jaladā prabham śaśimukhaṁ śyāmaṁ jagat maṅgalam*  
*śrī vaṁśīvadanaṁ surāri dalanaṁ go gopāla balair bhṛtāṁ*  
*vandyam nārada-siddha-kinnara gaṇaiḥ stutyām mahendrādibhir*  
*vṛndāraṇya vihāreṇaṁ\* bhajamāno govardhana dhārīnaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto the supreme Lord Shyamsundara, whose bodily complexion is like the new cloud, whose face resembles the moon, who is the benefactor of the entire universe, who plays on His flute, who destroys the demons, who is surrounded by the cows and the cowherd boys. O my mind please adore the lord, who is worshiped by Narada, Siddhas, Kinnaras and many great personalities, who eternally enjoys His pastimes in Vrindavan, and who lifted Govardhana Hill.**

### TEXT 2

*jay kṛṣṇa śrī kṛṣṇa caitanya jaya jaya jaya rāma nityānanda bhakti rasamaya*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Lord Sri Krsna and all glories to Lord Sri Krsna Caytanya. All glories to Lord Balarama and all glories to Sri Nityānanda, who is full of devotional mellows.**

### Text 3

*sādana sahite jaya śrī sundarānanda*  
*śrīdāmā sudāmā jaya sakhāgaṇavṛnda*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Sri Sundarānanda along with his associates. All glories to the cowherd boys headed by Śrīdāmā and Sudama**

### Text 4

*sakalera śara bhakti-bhakti sarvottama*  
*bhagavate bhakti parama dharma kaṇa*  
*akitava parama dahrma kahe bhagavate*

**Перевод:**

**Devotional service is the essence and highest of all. It has been established in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam that devotional service is the non duplicious supreme occupational duties of all living entities.**

This is confirmed in SB 1.1.2

*dharmaḥ projjhita-kaitavo ’tra paramo nirmatsarāṇāṁ satāṁ vedyaṁ vāstavam atra vastu śivadaṁ tāpa-trayonmūlanam śrīmad-bhāgavate mahā-muni-kṛte kiṁ vā parair īśvaraḥ sadyo hṛdy avarudhyate ’tra kṛtibhiḥ śuśrūṣubhis tat-kṣaṇāt*

**Перевод:**

**”Completely rejecting all religious activities which are materially motivated, this Bhāgavata Purāṇa propounds the highest truth, which is understandable by those devotees who are fully pure in heart. The highest truth is reality distinguished from illusion for the welfare of all. Such truth uproots the threefold miseries. This beautiful Bhāgavatam, compiled by the great sage Vyāsadeva \[in his maturity\], is sufficient in itself for God realization. What is the need of any other scripture? As soon as one attentively and submissively hears the message of Bhāgavatam, by this culture of knowledge the Supreme Lord is established within his heart.”**

In His commentary on this verse Śrīdhara Swami gives his commentary, atra mokṣa-vāñchan paryantaṁ nirastam. The desire for liberation is also rejected. Simply devotional service to the Lord.Else where in Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam (1.2.26. and 6.3.22.) states.

*etāvān eva loke ’smin puṁsāṁ dharmaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ bhakti-yogo bhagavati tan-nāma-grahaṇādibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**“Devotional service, beginning with the chanting of the holy name of the Lord, is the ultimate religious principle for the living entity in human society.”**

*mumukṣavo ghora-rūpān hitvā bhūta-patīn atha nārāyaṇa-kalāḥ śāntā bhajanti hy anasūyavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**”Those who are serious about liberation are certainly nonenvious, and they respect all. Yet they reject the horrible and ghastly forms of the demigods and worship only the all-blissful forms of Lord Viṣṇu and His plenary portions.”**

### Text 5

*jñāna sāṅkhya-yoga karma sanyasa ādi yata*  
*karma ādi cātur varṇa dharma āche yata*  
*sarva dharma śara ei bhakti yoga hañā*  
*parama dharma bhagavati kailā nirūpaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Various religious duties such as cultivation of knowledge, performance of fruitive activities, acceptence of sanyasa, following the regulative principles and so on are described in the scriptures. But Śrīmad Bhāgavatam has ascertained that devotional service is the assence of all religious duties and the supreme occupational duties for all living entities.**

### Text 6

*tāhe bhakti yoga jani trividha lakṣaṇa*  
*sādhana bhakti bhava bhakti āra bhakti prema*

**Перевод:**

**There are three categories of devational service namely Sadhana bhakti, bhava bhakti and prema bhakti.**

### Text 7

*bhakti rasāmṛta sindhu grantera varṇana*  
*tāhāte paripāti goswāmī vacana*

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Rupa Goswami has eleboratily described the characteristics of devotional service in his book Śrī Bhakti Rasāmṛta Sindhu.**

### Text 8

*sei sindhu laharī\* paraśite kaṇa*  
*sādhu kṛpā āvaleśe\* hoyache vāśana*

**Перевод:**

**By the mercy of the devotees I have developed a desire to touch a particle of that nectarian ocean.**

### Text 9

*śata koṭi praṇati sahasra nivedana*  
*bhāṣā varṇanera doṣā na kāra grahaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**I offer my unlimited obeisances at the lotus feet of the devotees, so they may not take my faults incured while writing this book.**

### Text 10

*śrī rūpera caraṇa smaraṇa kori māne*  
*bhava bhakti asi lekhā tri vidhā lakṣaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**I meditate on the lotus feet of Śrī Rupa Goswami, as I begin to describe the symptoms of the three categories of devotional service.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.1.) it is stated

*sa bhaktih sadhanam bhavah prema ca iti tridhoditā*

pg 4

**Перевод:**

**The devotional service has been divided into three categories namely Sādhana bhakti, Bhava bhakti and Prema bhakti.**

### Text 11

*bhakti haya tāhe sādhana sādhyā rupa nāma*  
*bhaṅgī krame gosāi kahena abhidhāna\**

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Rupa Goswāmī has described in the devotional service as sādhana or the process to achieve goal of life and sādhyā or the goal of life.**

### Text 12

*indriya preraṇa krame dehe sādhe thāke*  
*sādhyā bhāna sādhana bhakti boli kahe ??take??*

**Перевод:**

**The devotional service that one executes with the help of his senses to attain the goal of life is called Sādhana bhakti.**

### Text 13

*sādhana bhakti kṛtī sādhyā kahila lakṣaṇa*  
*dehendriya sadhyare\* se kṛtī sādhana*

**Перевод:**

**By engaging in sādhana bhakti through ones body and senses one can ultimately achieve the desired goal.**

### Text 14

*kṛtī sādhyā boli sabe bhaktite kahila*  
*takhana bhaktera chitte sandehā upajila*

**Перевод:**

**When sādhana bhakti which are practiced through ones body and senses was counted among the categories of pure devotional service, doubts arose in the mind of the devotees.**

### Text 15

*indriya sādhana janya yadi bhakti hañā*  
*bhaktite karaniya\* dehe bhāvera udgama*

**Перевод:**

**If devotional service is meant to be performed by ones body and senses than how is it that devotional service awakens love within ones body?**

### Text 16

*bhava sādhyā prema vastu seha kṛtī hoya*  
*prema nityā siddha ithe sandehā upajāya*

**Перевод:**

**If the ecstatic love of God, which is attainable through ecstatic emotion is achieved by executing devotional service through ones body and senses than one will certainly develop a love that the ecstatic love of God is eternally perfect.**

### Text 17

*tāhātesiddhānta śuno prabhura vacana*  
*nityā siddhasya bhavasya sandhena\* bhajana*

**Перевод:**

**Please here Śrī Rupa Goswāmī conclusion in this regard. The ecstatic emotion or bhava is eternally perfect.**

### Text 18

*bhava sādhyā nahe nityā siddha bhava haya*  
*anubhāva dvāre jñāna vikriyādi mayā*

**Перевод:**

**Bhava is not obtained through physical activities rather bhava is eternally perfect state. One has to revive this state through knowledge and attachment.**

### Text 19

*bhāvera lakṣaṇa cihna aśru ādi karī*  
*tāhāra ākhyāna haya śāstre bhakti balī*

**Перевод:**

**The shedding of tears and so on are the symptoms of bhava. The state of bhava is described in the scriptures as devotional service.**

### Text 20

*sei bhava hṛdaye prakaṭa rupa haya*  
*sādhana bhakti balī takhana nāma haya*

**Перевод:**

**As one practicing sādhana bhakti gradually the bhava manifest within ones heart.**

pg 6

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.2.) as follows:

*kṛtī sādhyā bhavet sādhyā bhava sa sādhana bhidā nityā siddhasya bhavasya prākaṭyaṁ hṛdi sādhyātha*

**Перевод:**

**“When devotional service is practiced through ones body and senses it is called sādhana bhakti. This sādhana bhakti gradually develops ones bhava. The state of bhava is an asset of an eternally perfect devotee.”**

### Text 21

*bhakti kare kahi tāha karaha śravaṇa*  
*ye prakāre kṛṣṇe mati haya niṣevaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Please here with attention the definition of devotional service, by which ones mind becomes fixed in the service of Kṛṣṇa.**

It is stated in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (3.25.32.)

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca devānāṁ guṇa-liṅgānām ānuśravika-karmaṇām sattva evaika-manaso vṛttiḥ svābhāvikī tu yā animittā bhāgavatī bhaktiḥ siddher garīyasī*

**Перевод:**

**“Lord Kapila said: The senses are symbolic representations of the demigods, and their natural inclination is to work under the direction of the Vedic injunctions. As the senses are representatives of the demigods, so the mind is the representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. The mind’s natural duty is to serve. When that service spirit is engaged in devotional service to the Personality of Godhead, without any motive, that is far better even than salvation.”**

### Text 22

*kṛṣṇe mati ??praveśahona?? aneka prakāre*  
*kāma dveṣa bhayādi āchaye vicāre*

**Перевод:**

**Ones mind becomes attracted to Kṛṣṇa in various ways such as due to lusty desires, envy, fear and so on.**

### Text 23

*tabe vairānubandhena āveśa bhakti haita*  
*anukūla rahite nahila bhakti yata*
