**Перевод:**

**Although this symptoms are also called devotional service , but they are unable to reach the category of favorable devotional service.**

### Text 24

*anukulye kano kriya kṛṣṇoddese haya*  
*samanyata bhakti bole tāhā kahe kaya*

**Перевод:**

**If any activity is performed favorably for the pleasure of kṛṣṇa than it is called devotional service.**

### Text 25

*bhaṅgī krame risigana ei katha kano*  
*tasmat kenabi ubayena ityadi vacana*

???????Translation is missed

This is explained in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (7.1.31.)

*gopyaḥ kāmād bhayāt kaṁso dveṣāc caidyādayo nṛpāḥ sambandhād vṛṣṇayaḥ snehād yūyaṁ bhaktyā vayaṁ vibho*

**Перевод:**

**“My dear King Yudhiṣṭhira, the gopīs by their lusty desires, Kaṁsa by his fear, Śiśupāla and other kings by envy, the Yadus by their familial relationship with Kṛṣṇa, you Pāṇḍavas by your great affection for Kṛṣṇa, and we, the general devotees, by our devotional service, have obtained the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.”**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.3.) states

*sā bhaktiḥ saptama skandhe bhaṅgyā devarṣiṇoditā*

**Перевод:**

**In the Seventh Canto of the Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam Nārada Muni has systematically described this process of sādhana-bhakti or devotional service in practice. (folio)**

The topics of this sādhana bhakti is described by the great sage Nārada in a disguised manner in the seventh canto of Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam. (bhumipati)

### Text 26

*kṛṣṇe nirmala mati jñāna karmādi rahita*  
*jñāna śabde brahma jñāna tāhe anābhṛta*

**Перевод:**

**One should fix his purity of mind in kṛṣṇa without the tinge of fruitive activities and mental speculations. The word jñāna refers to the knowledge of impersonal brahman.**

### Text 27

*bhāgānya sandana jñāna bhakti abhimata*  
*sei jñāna sādhane mati rākhibe satata*

**Перевод:**

**Searching for knowledge about the worshipable lord is favourable to devotional service. One should constantly endeavor for such cultivation of knowledge.**

### Text 28

*karma sabhe smṛti uktā nityā kriyā karma*  
*tāhe anābhṛta naya uttama bhakti marma*

**Перевод:**

**Fruitive activities are refered to those, which are mentioned in the smrti literature as eternal duties. But pure devotional service is uncovered by such activities.**

### Text 29

*kṛṣṇa paricaryā karma na ??kaiva tejana??*  
*kṛṣṇa karma haye jāni bhakti aṅga nirūpaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One should never give up the activities that satisfies the Kṛṣṇa, becouse the activities performed for the sake of Kṛṣṇa are ascertained as limps of devotional service.**

### Text 30

*karmādira ādi pade vairāgya sāṅkhya yoga*  
*ihātha anāvṛta bhaktira prayoga*

**Перевод:**

**Renunciation and metaphisical knowledge cannot cover pure devotional service.**

### Text 31

*ānukūlye prīti snehe seve bhagavāna*  
*sarvendriye kṛṣṇa sevā parama bhakti nāma*

**Перевод:**

**When one serves Kṛṣṇa favourably with love and affection through ones senses, then it is called pure devotional service.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu the following verse from Nārada Pancaratra is quoted :

*sarvopādhi-vinirmuktaṁ tat-paratvena nirmalam hṛṣīkena hṛṣīkeśa-sevanaṁ bhaktir ucyate*

**Перевод:**

**“Devotional service to the Supreme Lord means engagement of all the senses in His service. In such service there are two important features: First, one must be purified of all designations, and second, the senses should be engaged only in the service of the Supreme Lord, the master of the senses. That is pure devotional service.”**

pg 9

The Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.1.11) defines pure devotion thus:

*anyābhilāṣitā-śūnyaṁ jñāna-karmādy-anāvṛtam ānukūlyena kṛṣṇānu- śīlanaṁ bhaktir uttamā*

**Перевод:**

**“One should render transcendental loving service to the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa favorably and without desire for material profit or gain through fruitive activities or philosophical speculation. That is called pure devotional service.”**

### Text 32

*sarvendriya jarā kṛṣṇa karme niyojita*  
*anya śubha śubha karme saṅgatā rahita*  
*kṛṣṇa sambandhi kriyā tāre bhakti kāya*  
*sāmānya bhakti haile haya mahā bhaktyudaya*

**Перевод:**

**One whose senses is engaged in the service of kṛṣṇa and are devoid of attachment for pious and impious activities it is called devotional service becouse this activities are related to Kṛṣṇa. From ordinary devotional service one gradually develops pure devotional service.**

In the Nārada-pañcarātra (1.2.13) it is stated-

*surarṣe vihitā śāstre harim uddiśya yā kriyā saiva bhaktir iti proktā tayā bhaktiḥ parā bhavet iti*

**Перевод:**

**“ Any activities sanctioned in the revealed scriptures and aiming at the satisfaction of the Supreme Personality of Godhead are accepted by saintly teachers as the regulative principles of devotional service. If one regularly executes such service unto the Supreme Personality of Godhead under the direction of a bona fide spiritual master, then gradually he rises to the platform of serving in pure love of God.”**

### Text 33

*parama bhaktira sutra karaha śravaṇa*  
*sarvopādhi rahita haiyā kṛṣṇa niṣṭhā manā*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear characteristics of pure devotional service. One should give up all material designations and fix his mind on the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 34

*sarvopādhi rahita anyābhilāṣa śūnya*  
*nāhi abhilāṣa mukti svargādika janya*

**Перевод:**

**One should give up material designation and desire for material enjoyments. He should not endeavor for liberation attaining heavenly planets.**

### Text 35

*vākyendriye kṛṣṇa nāma guṇādi varṇana*  
*śravaṇete hare nāma guṇādi śravaṇa*  
*pg 10*

**Перевод:**

**Such a person constantly chants the holy names and describes the transcendental qualities of Kṛṣṇa with his speech and the senses. He hears the holy names and qualities of Kṛṣṇa through out his ears.**

### Text 36

*haste paricaryā sevāra āyojana*  
*manete karaye sadā śrī mūrti bhāvanā*

**Перевод:**

**With hands he makes arrangements for the service of Kṛṣṇa and within his mind he constantly remembers the deity form of the Lord.**

### Text 37

*mastakera kārya pada padma yuge nāti*  
*dehe āliṅgana bhakta janera saṅgāti*

**Перевод:**

**His head is engaged in bowing down to the Lord and he engages his body in embracing the devotees.**

### Text 38

*cakṣura sāphalya kṛṣṇa mūrti ālokane*  
*tathā bhakta guru sādhu tīrtha sandarśane*

**Перевод:**

**The perfection of the eyes is to see the deity form of Kṛṣṇa, to see ones spiritual master and the Vaiṣṇavas, and to visit the holy places.**

### Text 39

*sarvendriya jarā kṛṣṇa karme niyojila*  
*tara dehe parama bhakti baliyā kahila*

**Перевод:**

**Know it for the certain that one, who has engaged all his senses in the service of Kṛṣṇa, is engaged in pure devotional service.**

It is mentioned in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (10.10.38.)

*vāṇī guṇānukathane śravaṇau kathāyāṁ hastau ca karmasu manas tava pādayor naḥ smṛtyāṁ śiras tava nivāsa-jagat-praṇāme dṛṣṭiḥ satāṁ darśane ’stu bhavat-tanūnām*

**Перевод:**

**Henceforward, may all our words describe Your pastimes, may our ears engage in aural reception of Your glories, may our hands, legs and other senses engage in actions pleasing to You, and may our minds always think of Your lotus feet. May our heads offer our obeisances to everything within this world, because all things are also Your different forms, and may our eyes see the forms of Vaiṣṇavas, who are nondifferent from You.**

### Text 40

*seita sādhana bhakti haye dui nāma*  
*vidhi bhakti rāga bhakti kāra avadhāna*

**Перевод:**

**Please here carefully that the cultivation of devotional service is two kinds namely vaidhi bhakti or devotional service performed according to rules and regulations and raga bhakti or devotional service rendered through attachment.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.5.) as follows

*“vaidhi raganuga ceti sa dvidha sādhana vidhā “*

**Перевод:**

**“The sādhana bhakti is of two types namely vaidhi bhakti and raganuga bhakti.”**

The definition of vaidhi bhakti is as follows:

### Text 41

*rāga hīnā bhajana je vā śastreṇa śaraṇe*  
*prīti śūnya kṛṣṇa bhaje devatāra jñāne*

**Перевод:**

**The devotional service that is devoid of attachment and love for kṛṣṇa and rendered under the rules and regulations of the scriptures while considering kṛṣṇa an ordinary demigod is called vaidhi bhakti.**

### Text 42

*kṛṣṇa na bhajile haya naraka gamana*  
*bhajile abhīṣṭa pūrṇa nityā sukha hañā*

**Перевод:**

**If one does not worship Kṛṣṇa then he certainly goes to hell and if one worships Kṛṣṇa then ones desires are fulfilled and such a person attends eternal happiness.**

### Text 43

*papa puṇyā bhaye bhāge śāstrera ādeśe*  
*paramātmā bhagavāna aśeṣa viśeṣe*  
*anukūla sneha hīnā bhajana viṣṇu jñāne pg 12*  
*sei vaidhi bhakti haya pūjyā pūjakera krame*

**Перевод:**

**When being afraid of pious and impious activities and under the instructions of the scripture one worships the supersoul or the Lord it is called vaidhi bhakti : although this worship is performed favourably yet it is devoid of love for the Lord.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu  *(1.2.6)*

*yatra rāgān avāptatvāt pravṛttir upajāyate śāsanenaiva śāstrasya sā vaidhī bhaktir ucyate*

**Перевод:**

**When there is no attachment or no spontaneous loving service to the Lord, and one is engaged in the service of the Lord simply out of obedience to the order of the spiritual master or in pursuance of the scriptures, such obligatory service is called vaidhi-bhakti.**

### Text 44

*rāga hīnā bhaktira sāśana śāstre kaṇa*  
*padma purāṇādi granthe munira vacana*

**Перевод:**

**the sages have described in the scriptures such as Padma Purana about the rules and regulations for performing devotional service that is devoid of spontaneous attachment.**

### Text 45

*abhaya vañchibe je apāna kalyāṇa*  
*iha kāla parakāle sukhera vidhāna*  
*yama bhava-roga kṣaya śrī kṛṣṇa sādhane pg13*  
*ataeva bhaja hari sadā sāvadhāne*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who wants fearlessness, own benefit, happiness in this life and next, freedom from Yamarajas punishment and cure of his material disease should always carefully worship the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 46

*śravaṇa kīrtana kāra smaraṇa manana*  
*govinda bhajane sarva āpadā khaṇḍana*

**Перевод:**

**One should hear, chantand remember the glories of Govinda, for worshiping Govinda vanquishes all ones dangers.**

It is described in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.7-8

*tasmād bhārata sarvātmā bhagavān īśvaro hariḥ śrotavyaḥ kīrtitavyaś ca smartavyaś cecchatābhayam*

**Перевод:**

**O descendant of King Bharata, one who desires to be free from all miseries must hear about, glorify and also remember the Personality of Godhead, who is the Supersoul, the controller and the savior from all miseries.**

*smartavyaḥ satataṁ viṣṇur vismartavyo na jātucit sarve vidhi-niṣedhāḥ syur etayor eva kiṅkarāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Kṛṣṇa is the origin of Lord Viṣṇu. He should always be remembered and never forgotten at any time. All the rules and prohibitions mentioned in the śāstras should be the servants of these two principles.’**

### Text 47

*ityādi śūnya śāstre vaidhi bhaye bhaje*  
*vaidhi bhakti lakṣaṇa kahila vijña mājhe*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this injunctions of the scripture when one worships the Lord out of fear of the rules and regulations, it is called vaidhi bhakti by the learned people.**

### Text 48

*sarva varṇāśrama prati aiche vākya kaya*  
*govinda sādhana vinā \* naraka niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**This injunctions are applied to all the varnas and asramas. Infact any one, who does not worship Govinda, certainly goes to hell.**

pg 14

It is described in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.11)

*ya eṣāṁ puruṣaṁ sākṣād- ātma-prabhavam īśvaram na bhajanty avajānanti sthānād bhraṣṭāḥ patanty adhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**If one simply maintains an official position in the four varṇas and āśramas but does not worship the Supreme Lord Viṣṇu, he falls down from his puffed-up position into a hellish condition.**

### Text 49

*vidhi mārge vaidika tāntrika pūjārīte*  
*bhajile kṛtārtha jīva ubhaya kālete*

**Перевод:**

**If a living entity follows the rules and regulations of the scriptures while engaging in worshiping the Lord then he becomes successful both in this life and next life.**

### Text 50

*tāhāra pramāṇa śuno śrī yutera\* sāśana*  
*guṇā krame bhaktira hoya cayati\* lakṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīpād Rupa Goswāmī has described six symptoms of devotional service according to their qualities. They are as follows:**

### Text 51

*kleśaghnī śubhadā ei vaidhi bhakti phala*  
*mokṣa laghutākṛt sudurlabhā bhava bhakti phala*

**Перевод:**

**The results of vaidhi bhakti are: 1) it destroys all material distresses, 2) it bestows all auspiciousness. The results of bhava bhakti are: 1) it makes the liberation look insignificant, 2) it is very rare.**

### Text 52

*sāndrānanda viśeṣātma śrī kṛṣṇa karśani*  
*prema bhakti phala ei śāstra mate śuni*

**Перевод:**

**pg 15**  
**The results of Prema bhakti are 1)It is full of intense bliss and 2) It attract Kṛṣṇa.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.1.17.)

*kleśaghnī śubhadā mokṣa laghutākṛt sudurlabhā sāndrānanda viśeṣātma śrī kṛṣṇa karśani ca sa*

**Перевод:**

**There are six characteristics of pure devotional service. It destroyes ones material miseries, it awards ones auspiciousness, it makes liberation insignificant, it is very rare, it is full of transcendental bliss and it attracts Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 53

*kleśāstu trividha pāpāḥ tad bījaṁ\* avidyā*  
*prārabdham ca aprārabdhaṁ aihikaṁ pāpam*

**Перевод:**

**Material distresses are of three kinds namely sinful activities, the seed of sinful activities and nescience. The sinful activities are of two types namely: 1) those sinful activities which are situated in the subtle body of a living entities as prejudice and which have not been fructified, and 2) those sinful activities which have been fructified and a living entity has accepted a material body in order to enjoy its results.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.1.20.)

*yathāgniḥ su-samṛddhārciḥ karoty edhāṁsi bhasmasāt tathā mad-viṣayā bhaktir uddhavaināṁsi kṛtsnaśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Uddhava, just as a blazing fire turns firewood into ashes, similarly, devotion unto Me completely burns to ashes sins committed by My devotees.**

### Text 54

*prārabdham pūrva\* kālikaṁ purā aparādhena*  
*caṇḍāla ādi kule janma tadāpi kṛṣṇa nāma*  
*grahaṇa ādi na pūyeta yajña karmādhikāri samo bhavati*

**Перевод:**

**Due to ones sinful activities committed in the previous life one takes birth in the family of a dog eater. But if he takes to the chanting the holy name of Kṛṣṇa than he can become purified of all sinful reactions and becomes eligible to perform sacrifices.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu  **(**1.1.21. and 1.1.23)

*yan-nāmadheya-śravaṇānukīrtanād yat-prahvaṇād yat-smaraṇād api kvacit śvādo ’pi sadyaḥ savanāya kalpate kutaḥ punas te bhagavan nu darśanāt*

**Перевод:**

**To say nothing of the spiritual advancement of persons who see the Supreme Person face to face, even a person born in a family of dog-eaters immediately becomes eligible to perform Vedic sacrifices if he once utters the holy name of the Supreme Personality of Godhead or chants about Him, hears about His pastimes, offers Him obeisances or even remembers Him.**

*aprārabdha phalaṁ pāpam kūṭaṁ bījaṁ phalaumukhaṁ\* krameṇa eva pralayeta\* viṣṇu bhakti ratātmanām*

**Перевод:**

**The four kinds of sinful activities such as unfructified, seed, seeding and fructified sinful activities of the person, who are attached to the devotional service of Visnu are gradually destroyed.**

### Text 55

*??becjam?? ajñāna rūpaṁ citta-sthaṁ pāpam tapasyādina*  
*punāti kuntī cittaṁ na śuddha yāti kṛṣṇāghri\* sevayā vinā*

**Перевод:**

**By undergoing austerities and so on ones sinful activities are destroyed. But unless one serves the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa his heart does not become purified of sinful desires.**

This is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.1.24.)

*tais tāny aghāni pūyante tapo-dāna-vratādibhiḥ nādharmajaṁ tad-dhṛdayaṁ tad apīśāṅghri-sevayā*

**Перевод:**

**Although one may neutralize the reactions of sinful life through austerity, charity, vows and other such methods, these pious activities cannot uproot the material desires in one’s heart. However, if one serves the lotus feet of the Personality of Godhead, he is immediately freed from all such contaminations.**

### Text 56

*avidyā karmāśaya rupa vandanaṁ tat kṛṣṇa*  
*bhaktya udgranthanam\* bhavati yathā tathā*  
*yatayaḥ jitendriyā gaṇa na iti*

**Перевод:**

**The knots of fruitive activities which is born from nescience and which is firmly situated in the heart are easily destroyed by the devotees of Kṛṣṇa whereas the self-controlled renunciates who are not devotees of Kṛṣṇa are unable to destroy them.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.1.25)

*yat-pāda-paṅkaja-palāśa-vilāsa-bhaktyā karmāśayaṁ grathitam udgrathayanti santaḥ tadvan na rikta-matayo yatayo ’pi ruddha-sroto-gaṇās tam araṇaṁ bhaja vāsudevam*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees, who are always engaged in the service of the toes of the lotus feet of the Lord, can very easily overcome hard-knotted desires for fruitive activities. Because this is very difficult, the nondevotees—the jñānīs and yogīs—although trying to stop the waves of sense gratification, cannot do so. Therefore you are advised to engage in the devotional service of Kṛṣṇa, the son of Vasudeva.**

### Text 57

*sa tridhā bhavati sarva jagat prīṇatvaṁ\**  
*sarva sadguṇa pradatvaṁ sukhadatvaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**Devotional service is all auspicious. And it is divided into three categories: it please everyone, it fill one with good qualities and it gives happiness.**

It is confirmed in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu

(1.1.28)

*yenārcito haris tena tarpitāni jaganty api rajyanti jantavas tatra jaṅgamāḥ sthāvarā api*

**Перевод:**

**A person who is engaged in devotional service in full Kṛṣṇa consciousness is to be understood to be doing the best service to the whole world and to be pleasing everyone in the world. In addition to human society, he is pleasing even the trees and animals, because they also become attracted by such activities.**

(1.1.29)

*yasyāsti bhaktir bhagavaty akiñcanā sarvair guṇais tatra samāsate surāḥ harāv abhaktasya kuto mahad-guṇā manorathenāsati dhāvato bahiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**All the demigods and their exalted qualities, such as religion, knowledge and renunciation, become manifest in the body of one who has developed unalloyed devotion for the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Vāsudeva. On the other hand, a person devoid of devotional service and engaged in material activities has no good qualities. Even if he is adept at the practice of mystic yoga or the honest endeavor of maintaining his family and relatives, he must be driven by his own mental speculations and must engage in the service of the Lord's external energy. How can there be any good qualities in such a man?**

*sukha-pradatvaṁ-*

**Перевод:**

**Devotional service as giver of happiness -**

(1.1.30)

*sukhaṁ vaiṣayikaṁ brāhmam aiśvaraṁ ceti tat tridhā*

**Перевод:**

**Happiness from material enjoyment, happiness from identification with Brahman and happiness derived from Kṛṣṇa consciousness, are the three categories of happiness.**

(1.1.31)

*siddhayaḥ paramāścaryā bhuktir muktiś ca śāśvatī nityaṁ ca paramānandaṁ bhaved govinda-bhaktitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A person who has surrendered himself at the lotus feet of Govinda and who has thus developed pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness can be very easily awarded all the perfections desired by the impersonalists; and beyond this, he can enjoy the happiness achieved by the pure devotees.**

Thus end of description of *”klesaghni”* and *“subhada”,* which are the results of sādhana bhakti.

*mokṣa-laghutā-kṛt- pg 19*

**Перевод:**

**Devotional service minimizes the importance of liberation-**

(1.1.33)

*manāg eva prarūḍhāyāṁ hṛdaye bhagavad-ratau puruṣārthās tu catvāras tṛṇāyante samantataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When even a little attachment to Kṛṣṇa arises in a person's heart, then religiosity, economic development, sense gratification and liberation become as insignificant as straw.**

(1.1.34)

*hari-bhakti-mahā-devyāḥ sarvā mukhyādi siddhayaḥ bhuktayaś cādbhutās tasyāś ceṭikāvad anuvratāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The wonderful pleasures derived from the main mystic perfections are afraid of Hari-bhakti-mahā-devī, pure devotional service to Lord Hari, and they become her faithful attendants.**

***sudurlabhā-***

**Перевод:**

**Devotional service is rarely achieved-**

(1.1.36)

*jñānataḥ su-labhā muktir bhuktir yajñādi-puṇyataḥ seyaṁ sādhana-sāhasrair hāri-bhaktiḥ su-durlabhā*

**Перевод:**

**By cultivating philosophical knowledge one can understand his spiritual position and thus be liberated, and by performing sacrifices and pious activities one can achieve sense gratification in a higher planetary system, but the devotional service of the Lord is so rare that even by executing hundreds and thousands of such sacrifices one cannot obtain it.**

(1.1.37) pg 20

*rājan patir gurur alaṁ bhavatāṁ yadūnāṁ daivaṁ priyaḥ kula-patiḥ kva ca kiṅkaro vaḥ astv evam aṅga bhagavān bhajatāṁ mukundo muktiṁ dadāti karhicit sma na bhakti-yogam*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmī continued: My dear King, the Supreme Person, Mukunda, is actually the maintainer of all the members of the Pāṇḍava and Yadu dynasties. He is your spiritual master, worshipable Deity, friend, and the director of your activities. To say nothing of this, He sometimes serves your family as a messenger or servant. This means He worked just as ordinary servants do. Those engaged in getting the Lord's favor attain liberation from the Lord very easily, but He does not very easily give the opportunity to render direct service unto Him.**

### Text 58

*ei ta kahila mātrā bhava bhakti phale*  
*mokṣa laghutākṛt sudurlabhā dui bale*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the two results of bhava bhakti namely** ***moksalaghutakrit*** **and** ***sudurlabhā*** **are explained**

Now the results of Prema bhakti are being described as follows

**Перевод:**

**It is bestows transcendental bliss Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.1.39)**

*tvat-sākṣāt-karaṇāhlāda-viśuddhābdhi-sthitasya me sukhāni goṣpadāyante brāhmāṇy api jagad-guro*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Lord, O master of the universe, since I have directly seen You, my transcendental bliss has taken the shape of a great ocean. Being situated in that ocean, I now realize all other so-called happiness to be like the water contained in the hoofprint of a calf.**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-karṣiṇī-*

**Перевод:**

**Attracting Kṛṣṇa-**

(1.1.41)

*kṛtvā hariṁ prema-bhājaṁ priya-varga-samanvitam bhaktir vaśīkarotīti śrī-kṛṣṇa-karṣiṇī matā*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti, devotional service, is known as śrī-kṛṣṇa-karṣiṇī, which means that she fully controlls Kṛṣṇa in so many ways, through the influence of love.**

*yathāikādeśe (SB 11.14.20)-*

As stated in the Eleventh Canto of the Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam-

(1.1.42)

*na sādhayati māṁ yogo na sāṅkhyaṁ dharma uddhava na svādhyāyas tapas tyāgo yathā bhaktir mamorjitā*

**Перевод:**

**\[The Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, said:\] 'My dear Uddhava, neither through aṣṭāṅga-yoga \[the mystic yoga system to control the senses\], nor through impersonal monism or an analytical study of the Absolute Truth, nor through study of the Vedas, nor through practice of austerities, nor through charity, nor through acceptance of sannyāsa can one satisfy Me as much as one can by developing unalloyed devotional service unto Me.**

### Text 59

*ei to kahila bhāi bhaktira cayā guṇā*  
*gosāira sūtra tāhe kāraha śravaṇa*  
*pg 21*

**Перевод:**

**O brothers You have thus heard the six characteristics of devotional service described by Śrīla Rupa Goswāmī .**

### Text 60

*sādhana bhaktite dui guṇā age kahe*  
*kleśaghnī śubhadā ei dui hoye*

**Перевод:**

**The two characteristics of sādhana bhakti are already described as kleśaghnī and śubhadā**

### Text 61

*bhava bhakti cārī guṇā pūrva dui loiyā*  
*nija guṇā saha cārī anugata hoya*

**Перевод:**

**The bhava bhakti consist of four characteristics. Two from the above mentioned sādhana bhakti and other two are mokṣa laghutākṛt and sudurlabhā.**

### Text 62

*prema bhaktire sadā guṇā nija guṇā saha*  
*pūrva pūrva anugata sadā guṇā hoya*

**Перевод:**

**The prema bhakti consist of six characteristics two from sādhana bhakti, two from bhava bhakti and other two are sāndrānanda** ***viśeṣātmā*** **and** ***śrī kṛṣṇa karśani*****.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.1.44)

*agrato vakṣyamānāyās tridhā bhakter anukramāt dviśaḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ padair etan māhātmyaṁ parikīrtitam*

**Перевод:**

**At the beginning of this book, the greatness of devotional service, bhakti, has been glorified by describing these six uncommon characteristics listed above. Bhakti can be divided into three categories, with each successive category having two more qualities than the previous one.(folio)**

We will gradually describe this topics of sādhana bhakti, bhava bhakti and prema bhakti. In each category there are two extraordinary characteristics were mentioned. Since the previously mentioned characteristics are included in the latter categories therefore sādhana bhakti has two characteristics bhava bhakti has four characteristics and prema bhakti has six characteristics (bhumipatis ) \*

### Text 63

*ebe kahi sei bhakti sādhe kona jarā*  
*bhakti śāstre kahe tara adhikārī niyama*

**Перевод:**

**Now I will describe the persons who practice such devotional service. The devotional scriptures describes the qualification of such person .**

### Text 64

*saṁsāra bhramite keha nija puṇyā phale*  
*kona rūpe kṛṣṇa bhakta sādhu saṅga mile*

**Перевод:**

**While wondering throughout the universe a fortunate living entity somehow or other gets the association of Kṛṣṇa\`s devotees.**

### Text 65

*sei saṅga hoya tara sad-gati kāraṇa*  
*śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇāravinde rati āse hañā*

**Перевод:**

**Such association of the devotees becomes a criteria for his deliverance. Soon such a person develops attachment for the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

It is described in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (10.51.53.)

*bhavāpavargo bhramato yadā bhavej janasya tarhy acyuta sat-samāgamaḥ sat-saṅgamo yarhi tadaiva sad-gatau parāvareśe tvayi jāyate matiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When the material life of a wandering soul has ceased, O Acyuta, he may attain the association of Your devotees. And when he associates with them, there awakens in him devotion unto You, who are the goal of the devotees and the Lord of all causes and their effects.**

### Text 66

*kono jana ati bhāgye śrī kṛṣṇa sādhane*  
*śraddhā janmāye yāra sevā hetu māne*  
*pg 23*

**Перевод:**

**Out of great fortune one engages in the worship of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and faith for His service.**

### Text 67

*gṛhe thākiyā viṣaye ati śakta\* naya*  
*atiśaya vairāgyao tāhe nāhi hoya*

**Перевод:**

**Although he remains at home he neither becomes attached to it nor becomes indifferent to it.**

### Text 68

*sei hoya adhikārī bhaktira sādhane*  
*ekadeśa uddhavere\* kahena\* apāne*

**Перевод:**

**Such a person is qualified for cultivating devotional service. This is described by Lord Kṛṣṇa to Uddhava in eleventh canto of Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu it is stated

(1.2.14.)

*yaḥ kenāpy ati-bhāgyena jāta-śraddho 'sya sevane nāti-sakto na vairāgya-bhāg asyām adhikāry asau*

**Перевод:**

**One who has become purified by association with great devotees engaged in the service of the Lord, who is not overly attached to material enjoyment nor overly renounced or falsely renounced, such a person is a qualified candidate for devotional service. (folio)**

One who has developed faith in the worship of Kṛṣṇa as a result of his piety accumulated from association of devotees as well as who is neither attached nor detached, such a person is qualified to be engaged in devotional service.\* (bhumipati)

(1.2.15)

*yadṛcchayā mat-kathādau jāta-śraddhas tu yaḥ pumān na nirviṇṇo nātisakto bhakti-yogo 'sya siddhidaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Somehow or other, if one is attracted to talks about Me and has faith in the instructions I have set forth in Bhagavad-gītā, and if one is actually detached from material things and material existence, his dormant love for Me will be awakened by devotional service.**

### Text 69

*sei bhakta hoya trividha lakṣaṇa*  
*uttama madhyama āra kaniṣṭha kathana*

**Перевод:**

**Such a devotee are divided into three categories namely uttama adhikari, madhyama adhikari and kanistha adhikari.**

**THE UTTAMA ADHIKARI—**

### Text 70

*śāstra yuktire nipuṇā\* dṛḍha ??nisehayasoda??*  
*śāstre viśvāsa ati kahila sarvathā*

**Перевод:**

**One who has firm conviction in the scriptures and very expert in ascertaining its conclusions as well as who has immense faith in the scriptures, he is an Uttama adhikārī.**

### Text 71

*dṛḍha-niścaya śabde kahi vastu vicārādi*  
*puruṣārtha sādhana vicāre dṛḍha yadi*  
*prauḍhā śraddhā bhakti śāstre viśvāsa-yāti yāra pg 25*  
*uttama boliyā hoya ākhyāna tāhāra*

**Перевод:**

**The word dṛḍha-niścaya indicates that such a person is firmly convinced about the absolute truth and in the ascertainment of the supreme goal of life. An uttama adhikārī has matured faith in the devotional scriptures.**

It is described in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu

(1.2.17)

*śāstre yuktau ca nipuṇaḥ sarvathā dṛḍha-niścayaḥ prauḍha-śraddho 'dhikārī yaḥ sa bhaktāv uttamo mataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who is expert in logic and understanding of revealed scriptures, and who always has firm conviction and deep faith that is not blind, is to be considered a topmost devotee in devotional service.\* (folio)**

MADHYAMA ADHIKĀRĪ –

### Text 72

*śāstre taratama buddhi naheta nipuṇā*  
*bhakti śāstre kahe sei madhyama lakṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One who is not expert in devotional scriptures and thus discriminates, but possesses strong faith he is known as madhyama adhikārī.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu

(1.2.18.)

*yaḥ śāstrādiṣv anipuṇaḥ śraddhāvān sa tu madhyamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He who does not know scriptural argument very well but who has firm faith is called an intermediate or second-class devotee madhyama adhikārī.**

KANIṢṬHA ADHIKĀRĪ –

### Text 73

*śāstrādite\* ye vā nipuṇā ati naya*  
*sādhane kamala\* śraddhā kaniṣṭha are koya*

**Перевод:**

**One who is not at all expert in the scriptures and whose faith is very tender is called kaniṣṭha adhikārī.**

### Text 74

*vicārādi krame antarera bheda hañā*  
*komala śraddhā boli taha śāstra kare kaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One whose faith is eligible for being diverted by opposing scriptural reasoning he is called kaniṣṭha adhikārī by the compilars of the scripture.**

### Text 75

*śrī bhāgavata mate śuno bhaktira lakṣaṇa*  
*uttama madhyama āra kaniṣṭha varṇana*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear the characteristics of the devotional service executed by uttama, madhyama, and kaniṣṭha adhikārī as described in Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam.**

**UTTAMA ADHIKĀRĪ**

### Text 76-78

*sarva bhūte samā bhava nija parā samā*  
*jive ātmāte yāra bhāgavata bhāvanā samā śāstra mitra yāra manā apamāna nindā stuti sukha dukha nāhi bhinnā jñāna sthira mati bhakti manā haye bhaktottama gītā bhāgavate kahe prabhu sanātana*

pg 27

**Перевод:**

**The symptoms of a uttama adhikārī is that he is equal to all living entities, he sees the Supreme Lord situated in everyones heart, he sees everything in relation with the Lord, he is equal to the friends or enemies and honour and dishonour, he does not discriminate between blasphemy or glorification and his mind is fixed in devotional service. the Supreme Lord has described these characteristics of uttama adhikārī in Bhagavad-Gita and in Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam.**

In Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam (11.2.45) it is stated

*śrī-havir uvāca sarva-bhūteṣu yaḥ paśyed bhagavad-bhāvam ātmanaḥ bhūtāni bhagavaty ātmany*

**Перевод:**

**eṣa bhāgavatottamaḥ “Śrī Havir said: The most advanced devotee sees within everything the soul of all souls, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Consequently he sees everything in relation to the Supreme Lord and understands that everything that exists is eternally situated within the Lord.”**

In Bhagavad - Gītā (12.18-19)

*samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca tathā mānāpamānayoḥ śītoṣṇa-sukha-duḥkheṣu samaḥ saṅga-vivarjitaḥ tulya-nindā-stutir maunī santuṣṭo yena kenacit aniketaḥ sthira-matirbhaktimān me priyo naraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who is equal to friends and enemies, who is equipoised in honor and dishonor, heat and cold, happiness and distress, fame and infamy, who is always free from contaminating association, always silent and satisfied with anything, who doesn’t care for any residence, who is fixed in knowledge and who is engaged in devotional service—such a person is very dear to Me.**

### Text 79-81

*ekadeśa kahe hari uddhavera\* prati*  
*govinde satata yāra rahe niṣṭhā mati śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇa padma sadā yāra ?dhyāna? deva-gaṇā ye vā pada dhyāna nāhi pāna sei pade sadā mati haya bhakta jāne nimesārddha citta yāra na jaya anye sthāne*

**Перевод:**

**In the eleventh canto of Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam Lord Kṛṣṇa explains to Uddhava the qualities of an uttama adhikārī.**

**A person who has firm faith at the lotus feet of me, who constantly meditates on my lotus feet, which are rare even for demigods, and whose heart does not deviate from me even for a moment, he is an uttama adhikārī.**

It is described in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (11.2.53)

*tri-bhuvana-vibhava-hetave ’py akuṇṭha-smṛtir ajitātma-surādibhir vimṛgyāt na calati bhagavat-padāravindāl lava-nimiṣārdham api yaḥ sa vaiṣṇavāgryaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead are sought even by the greatest of demigods, such as Brahmā and Śiva, who have all accepted the Supreme Personality of Godhead as their life and soul. A pure devotee of the Lord can never forget those lotus feet in any circumstance. He will not give up his shelter at the lotus feet of the Lord for a single moment—indeed, not for half a moment—even in exchange for the benediction of ruling and enjoying the opulence of the entire universe. Such a devotee of the Lord is to be considered the best of the Vaiṣṇavas.**

### Text 82

*uttamera\* gṛhe nāhi vasati vāśana*  
*śyāmasundara sadā karaha bhāvanā*

**Перевод:**

**An uttama adhikārī has no interest in living at home and he constantly remembers Lord Syamasundara.**

### Text 83

*krodha hena viraktata indriya damana*  
*kṣamā citta jive dayā parā dukha sahana*

**Перевод:**

**He is devoid of anger detach to material enjoyment, self controlled, forgiving, compassionate to all living entities and cannot tolerate others distress.**

### Text 84

*sarva-jana priya sadā viṣayā lobha hīnā*  
*dāna yuktā bhaya śāka rahita pravīṇa*

**Перевод:**

**He is an expert, dear to everyone and always devoid of greed for material enjoyment. He is charitable devoid of fear and lamentation.**

### Text 85

*bhakti yuktā janera e dāsa lakṣaṇa*  
*purāṇantarera śuno kahiye vacana*

**Перевод:**

**There are the ten characteristics of a devotee explained in the puranas.**

*akrodha vairāgya jitendriyātvam kṣamā dayā sarva-jana priyatvaṁ ṇīrlobha dānaṁ bhaya śoka hīnam bhaktasya chinnam\* daśalakṣanam tat*

**Перевод:**

**A pure devotee possesses these ten qualities. He is devoid of anger, detach from material enjoyment, self controlled, forgiving merciful, dear to all living entities, he is devoid of greed, fear and lamentation and he is charitable.(??? from where???)**

###################

MADHYAMA ADHIKĀRĪ---

### Text 86-87

*īśvare karaye prema bhakti ācaraṇa*  
*vaisnave karaye maitra bhava prakāśān*  
*anugata kimbā mūrkha kṛpā kore ati*  
*dveṣike\* upekṣā kare madhyama ?kheyati?*

**Перевод:**

**A madhyama adhikārī is he who engages in loving devotional service to the Lord, makes friendship with the vaisnava, bestows mercy upon the subordinates or the fool and ignores the offenders.**

It is stated in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam 11.2.46

*īsvare tad-adhīneṣu bāliśeṣu dviṣatsu ca prema-maitrī-kṛpopekṣā yaḥ karoti sa madhyamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**An intermediate or second-class devotee, called madhyama-adhikārī, offers his love to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, is a sincere friend to all the devotees of the Lord, shows mercy to ignorant people who are innocent and disregards those who are envious of the Supreme Personality of Godhead.**

KANIṢṬHA ADHIKĀRĪ—

### Text 88-89

*śrī kṛṣṇa sevana kare hoya tatpara*  
*tvadīya vaiṣṇava adhike nā pūje aparā*  
*prākṛta bhaktera ei kahila lakṣaṇa*  
*śrīla bhāgavata mate trividha varṇana*

**Перевод:**

**One who eagerly worships kṛṣṇa, but does not worship the devotees of the Lord is called a mundane devotee or a kaniṣṭha adhikārī.**

There are three categories of a devotees described in Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam (11.2.47)

*arcāyām eva haraye pūjāṁ yaḥ śraddhayehate na tad-bhakteṣu cānyeṣu sa bhaktaḥ prākṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee who faithfully engages in the worship of the Deity in the temple but does not behave properly toward other devotees or people in general is called a prākṛta-bhakta, a materialistic devotee, and is considered to be in the lowest position.**

### Text 90

*ei ta kahila tina bhaktera lakṣaṇa*  
*saguṇa nirguṇa bhakti karaha śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the symptoms of three kinds of devotees. Now please hear about the descriptions of devotional service mixed with three modes of material nature.**

### Text 91

*sattva rajaḥ tamaḥ ei prākṛta jātā haya*  
*ei ??tinyukta?? bhakti saguṇa bhakti kāya*

**Перевод:**

**There are three modes of material nature namely goodness, passion and ignorance. Devotional service that is mixed with these modes of nature is called devotional service mixed with the modes of material nature.**

### Text 92

*nirguṇa harira bhakti śakale uttama*  
*guṇāguṇā bheda tāhe karaha śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**The pure devotional service to the Lord Hari is without any tinge of the three modes of material nature. Now hear the categories of mixed devotional service.**

### Text 93

*sāttvikā śraddhā śuka janya kārma janya rajasā*  
*adharma janya ye śraddhā se haya tāmasa*

**Перевод:**

**Devotional service mixed with the mode of goodness bestows happiness, mixed with the mode of passion bestows worldly activities and mixed with the mode of ignorance bestows sinful activities.**

### Text 94

*kṛṣṇāśraya yei śraddhā guṇā tīta hañā*  
*ataeva nirguṇa balī tāhā kare kaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**The devotional service which is beyond the three modes of material nature and id executed for the pleasure of Kṛṣṇa is called nirguna bhakti or devotional service without any tingle if material modes.**

Kṛṣṇa explains this to Uddhava in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam 11.25.27 and 11.25.29

*sāttviky ādhyātmikī śraddhā karma-śraddhā tu rājasī tāmasy adharme yā śraddhā mat-sevāyāṁ tu nirguṇā*

**Перевод:**

**Faith directed toward spiritual life is in the mode of goodness, faith rooted in fruitive work is in the mode of passion, faith residing in irreligious activities is in the mode of ignorance, but faith in My devotional service is purely transcendental.**

*sāttvikaṁ sukhaṁ ātmotthaṁ viṣayotthaṁ tu rājasam tāmasaṁ moha-dainyotthaṁ nirguṇaṁ mad-apāśrayam*

**Перевод:**

**Happiness derived from the self is in the mode of goodness, happiness based on sense gratification is in the mode of passion, and happiness based on delusion and degradation is in the mode of ignorance. But that happiness found within Me is transcendental.**

### Text 95

*tina guṇe trijagat kariche mohana*  
*guṇā tīta hoiyā sevā govinda caraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**The entire three worlds are bewildered by the three modes of material nature. Therefore transcend these three modes and serve the lotus feet of Govinda.**

### Text 96

*kṛṣṇa kahena vimalā bhaktite āma seve*  
*triguṇa laṅghiyā se amara bhava labhe*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Kṛṣṇa says, one who engages in my pure devotional service transcends the three modes of material nature and attain my supreme nature.**

In Bhagavad - Gītā (14.26) it is stated

*māṁ ca yo ’vyabhicāreṇa bhakti-yogena sevate sa guṇān samatītyaitān brahma-bhūyāya kalpate*

**Перевод:**

**One who engages in full devotional service, unfailing in all circumstances, at once transcends the modes of material nature and thus comes to the level of Brahman.**

####################

### Text 97

*nirguṇa bhagavan kṛṣṇa prakṛtira parā*  
*tiṅho sakalera draṣṭā sarva parātpara*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Kṛṣṇa is fully transcendental, without any tingle of material modes. He is situated beyond the material creation, seer of everything and the supreme personality of Godhead.**

### Text 98

*tāṅre yebā bhaje seha hoite nirguṇa*  
*bhāgavate śukadevā kahila vacana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore anyone who worships kṛṣṇa is also becomes transcendental. This is confirmed by Śukadeva Goswāmī in Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam.**

10.88.5

*harir hi nirguṇaḥ sākṣāt puruṣaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ sa sarva-dṛg upadraṣṭā taṁ bhajan nirguṇo bhavet*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Hari, however, has no connection with the material modes. He is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, the all-seeing eternal witness, who is transcendental to material nature. One who worships Him becomes similarly free from the material modes.**

### Text 99

*cātur-vidhā bhāve pravartaka gītayā varṇila*  
*ārtā jijñāsu arthārthī jñānī ye kahila*

**Перевод:**

**It is described in Bhagavad - Gītā four types of people surrender unto Kṛṣṇa. They who are distressed, desire for material gain and learned.**

In Bhagavad - Gītā (7.16) it is stated :

*catur-vidhā bhajante māṁ janāḥ sukṛtino ’rjuna ārto jijñāsur arthārthī jñānī ca bharatarṣabha*

**Перевод:**

**O best among the Bhāratas, four kinds of pious men begin to render devotional service unto Me—the distressed, the desired of wealth, the inquisitive, and he who is searching for knowledge of the Absolute.**

Also it is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.21)

*sa kṣīṇa-tat-tad-bhāvaḥ syāc chuddha-bhakty-adhikāravān yathebhaḥ śaunakādiś ca dhruvaḥ sa ca catuḥ-sanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When the materialistic motivations of a person have been destroyed, he becomes eligible to perform pure devotional service of the highest order. Examples of these four types of persons, \[in the same order as listed in the Gītā\], are-the elephant Gajendra, who was seeking relief from distress; Śaunaka Ṛṣi and the sages of Naimiṣāraṇya, who were inquisitive; Mahārāja Dhruva, who was searching after wealth; and the four Kumāras, who were wise men, seeking for knowledge.**

### Text 100-101

*gajendrā bhajile kṛṣṇe graha ārtā hoiyā*  
*jijñāsu śaunakādi mahimā śūnya*  
*artha kāṅkṣī hoiyā dhruvā bhaje bhagavan*  
*jñānī bhakta sanakādi śakale pradhāna*

**Перевод:**

**Being afflicted by distress Gajendra the king of elephant worshiped Kṛṣṇa. Hearing the glories of the Lord the inquisitive sages headed by Saynaka worshiped Him. Being distress by material gain Mahārāja Dhruvā worshiped the Lord. And the learned devotees headed by Saynaka engaged in the Lord\`s service.**

### Text 102

*ei matā cātur-vidhā pravarta kahila*  
*yāre bhakta kṛpā hoila se bhakti paila*

**Перевод:**

**Thus the four types of people who surrender to the Lord are explained. In fact anyone, who is favored by the mercy of a devotee certainly attains devotional service.**

### Text 103

*kṛṣṇa bhakti hoya bāhu tapasyādi pale*  
*koṭi koṭi madhye keha bāhu bhāgyā phale*

**Перевод:**

**As a result of great fortune and on the strength of great austerities one out of millions of people attains devotional service to kṛṣṇa!**

### Text 104

*svadharmete śata śata janma ata dhiyā*  
*kona jañā mukta haya jñāna yoga pāiyā*

**Перевод:**

**After worshiping the Lord for hundred lifetimes while remaining in his occupational duties one becomes learned and thus gets freedom from material bondage.**

### Text 105-106

*prākṛta śarīra thāki mukta abhimānī*  
*kimbā siddha-gaṇa yata sālokyādi gāmī*  
*mukta siddha koṭi koṭi madhye kona jana*  
*sudurlabhā hari bhakta bhakti yuktā hañā*

**Перевод:**

**Among those who proudly consider themselves as liberated while remaining in the material body or those perfect beings who attained the spiritual abode or among millions of liberated souls, a devotee of Hari who engages in devotional service is very rare.**

In Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (6.14.5) it is stated

*muktānām api siddhānāṁ nārāyaṇa-parāyaṇaḥ sudurlabhaḥ praśāntātmā koṭiṣv api mahā-mune*

**Перевод:**

**O great sage, among many millions who are liberated and perfect in knowledge of liberation, one may be a devotee of Lord Nārāyaṇa, or Kṛṣṇa. Such devotees, who are fully peaceful, are extremely rare.**

### Text 107

*kṛṣṇa kāṇena mora bhakta ekāntika manā*  
*dhīra sādhu jana saba āma parāyaṇā*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Kṛṣṇa says “My devotees engage their minds in thinking of Me without deviation. They are sober, saintly and attached to Me.**

### Text 108

*yadi amī kaivalyādi dite cāhi mukti*  
*nāhi laya mukti bāra binā dāsya bhakti*

**Перевод:**

**Even if I want to award liberation to my devotees they do not accept it. They, only desire to engage in My service eternally.**

Lord Kṛṣṇa explains to Uddhava in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (11.20.34)

*na kiñcit sādhavo dhīrā bhaktā hy ekāntino mama vāñchanty api mayā dattaṁ kaivalyam apunar-bhavam*

**Перевод:**

**Because My devotees possess saintly behavior and deep intelligence, they completely dedicate themselves to Me and do not desire anything besides Me. Indeed, even if I offer them liberation from birth and death, they do not accept it.**

Also in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (11.14.14)

*na pārameṣṭhyaṁ na mahendra-dhiṣṇyaṁ na sārvabhaumaṁ na rasādhipatyam na yoga-siddhīr apunar-bhavaṁ vā mayy arpitātmecchati mad vinānyat*

**Перевод:**

**One who has fixed his consciousness on Me desires neither the position or abode of Lord Brahmā or Lord Indra, nor an empire on the earth, nor sovereignty in the lower planetary systems, nor the eightfold perfection of yoga, nor liberation from birth and death. Such a person desires Me alone.**

### Text 109

*ataeva dhruvā yukti karaha śravaṇa*  
*āpanāke dekha dhruvā koriyāche nindana*

**Перевод:**

**Please consider the statements of Dhruvā Maharaja and He has condemned himself.**

### Text 110

*ucca\* pada sarvotkṛṣṭa paila kṛṣṇa sthāne*  
*sei pada tuccha kare bhakti bhāve bine*

**Перевод:**

**Although he attained the topmost position from the Lord, but he considered this position as insignificant becouse it is devoid of devotional service.**

### Text 111-112

*kṛṣṇa pada padma sevayā ye ānanda hoya*  
*bhakta saṅge kṛṣṇa kothāya sevā sukha dayā*  
*tārā kāche brahma sukha tuccha kori māne*  
*sevā sukha nāhi tathā ?tei? heya jāni*

**Перевод:**

**The happiness desired from merging into brahman is insignificant in comparison to the happiness one achieves by serving the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa and discussing the topics of Kṛṣṇa in the association of the devotees. It is inferior because there is no happiness of serving the Supreme Lord.**

### Text 113

*ataeva svargādi pada ati tuccha hoya*  
*tāhā diyā ohe prabhu bhūlāile\* niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! You have bewildered me by awarding the most insignificant position of heavenly Kingdom.**

In Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam 4.9.10 it is stated

*yā nirvṛtis tanu-bhṛtāṁ tava pāda-padma-dhyānād bhavaj-jana-kathā-śravaṇena vā syāt sā brahmaṇi sva-mahimany api nātha mā bhūt kiṁ tv antakāsi-lulitāt patatāṁ vimānāt*

**Перевод:**

**My Lord, the transcendental bliss derived from meditating upon Your lotus feet or hearing about Your glories from pure devotees is so unlimited that it is far beyond the stage of brahmānanda, wherein one thinks himself merged in the impersonal Brahman as one with the Supreme. Since brahmānanda is also defeated by the transcendental bliss derived from devotional service, then what to speak of the temporary blissfulness of elevating oneself to the heavenly planets, which is ended by the separating sword of time? Although one may be elevated to the heavenly planets, he falls down in due course of time.**

### Text 114

*muktistu\* pañca vidhā yathā sālokyaṁ*  
*sastin\* sāmīpyam sārūpyam sāyujyaṁ ca*  
*śrī kṛṣṇena dīyamānāpi pañca-vidha muktiḥ*  
*sevābhiratā bhakta nā ghṛnanti cātur-vidhā*  
*tu bhaktya nāti virūḍha ??*

**Перевод:**

**There are five kinds of liberation namely living in the same planet with the Lord, possessing the same opulence of the Lord, living as associate with the Lord, possessing the same feature as that of the Lord, and merging into existence of the Lord. Although Lord Kṛṣṇa awards this five kinds of liberation to His devotees, but they do not accept them. They are simply satisfied by His service. Among the five kinds of liberation the first four are not very hostile to the devotees.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.55) it is stated

*atra tyajiyā ?tayivokta? muktiḥ pañca vidhāpi cet sālokyādi tathā api atra bhaktya nāti viruddhate*

**Перевод:**

**Although devotees reject the five kinds of liberation still the first four kinds of liberation are not unfavorable for devotional service.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.56 it is described

*sukhāiśvaryottarā seyaṁ prema sevottarety\* api sālokyādir dvidha tatra nadyā sevā jusam\* matā*

**Перевод:**

**The first four kind of liberation are further divided into two categories namely liberation based on loving service. In the first category the desire for happiness and opulence is prominent and in the second category the desire to the loving service to the Lord is prominent. The devotees consider the first category to be hostile to devotional service.**

### Text 115

*kim ca prema mādhurya ākāṅkṣīnah\**  
*ekāntino bhakta pañca vidhaṁ api muktaṁ*  
*nā aṅgī kurvanti iti parāmarśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The unalloyed devotees who desire the sweet love of God do not accept any of the five kinds of liberation. This is the purport.**

*śrī kṛṣṇe ekānta bhakti yāra upajaye*

*śrī nandanandana mādhurya yāra āsvādana*  
*vicalite nāre keha tāhā kāra manā*

*ṣaḍaiśvarya paripūrṇa lakṣmīkānta haye*  
*braja bhaktagaṇere seha sukha nāhi bhaye*

**Although Lord Nārāyaṇa the husband of the goddess of fortune is full with six opulences, yet he cannot attract devotees of Braja.**

*govinda harila yāra niravadhi manā*

### Text 120

*anya sukha tara kāche ati tuccha prāya*  
*trivargādi phala yata seha kona dayā*

**All other happiness including the three objectives of life such as religiosity, economic development and sense gratification appear insignificant to them.**

### Text 121

*inho\* kṛṣṇa tiṅho paravyoma nārāyaṇa*  
*tathāpi prema rasa utkarṣa brajendranandana*

*tatrāpi ekāntinām śreṣṭha govinda hrītā mānasaḥ yeṣāṁ śrīśa prasādopi\* mano hartum nā ?saknuyat?*

**Перевод:**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.59 it is stated

*siddhāntah tu avhedāpi śrīśa kṛṣṇa svarūpayoḥ arāsena utkṛṣyate kṛṣṇa rūpaṁ eṣa rasa sthitiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**“Although there is no difference between Lord Kṛṣṇa and Lord Nārāyaṇa in truth and conclusion yet due to topmost ecstatic love the form of Kṛṣṇa remains superior. Because the transcendental mellows of ecstatic love places Kṛṣṇa on the highest platform.”**

*kṛṣṇa bhakti pathe keha pravarta hoiyā*

*anaṇusthānāto\* doṣo bhakti aṅgānām prajāyate nā karmaṇām akaraṇad eṣa bhakti adhikāriṇām*

**“If the pure devotees do not execute the limbs of devotional service they become faulty. But if they do not perform pious fruitive activities they do not incur any sin.**

### Text 127

*svakīya svakīya adhikāre niṣṭhā hoile*  
*sei hoya sadguṇa sarva śāstre bole*

### Text 128

*sva sva dharma viparyaye hoye doṣā jāni*  
*niṣe āya kahila\* ei bhakti śāstre muni*

**Перевод:**

**It is described in the devotional scriptures if one give up his own occupational duties he certainly becomes sinful.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.65 it is stated

*sve sve ’dhikāre yā niṣṭhā sa guṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ viparyayas tu doṣaḥ syād ubhayor eṣa niścayaḥ*

### Text 129

*viṣṇu bhaktānāṁ kadācit daivāt*  
*vikarma bhavet tathāpi prāyaścittaṁ*  
*tu nā ucitām iti vaiṣṇava*  
*śastrāṇam rahasyaṁ.*

**Перевод:**

**Even if the devotees of the Lord Viṣṇu accidentally commit sinful activities still there is no need for them to undergo atonement. This is the ??history?? of vaiṣṇava literatures.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.71 it is stated

(Ś- B 11.5.42)

*sva-pāda-mūlam bhajataḥ priyasya tyaktānya-bhāvasya hariḥ pareśaḥ vikarma yac cotpatitaṁ kathañcid dhunoti sarvaṁ hṛdi sanniviṣṭaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who has thus given up all other engagements and has taken full shelter at the lotus feet of Hari, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, is very dear to the Lord. Indeed, if such a surrendered soul accidentally commits some sinful activity, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who is seated within everyone’s heart, immediately takes away the reaction to such sin.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.66 it is stated

(Ś– B1.5.17 )

*tyaktvā sva-dharmaṁ caraṇāmbujaṁ harer bhajann apakvo ’tha patet tato yadi yatra kva vābhadram abhūd amuṣya kiṁ ko vārtha āpto ’bhajatāṁ sva-dharmataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who has forsaken his material occupations to engage in the devotional service of the Lord may sometimes fall down while in an immature stage, yet there is no danger of his being unsuccessful. On the other hand, a nondevotee, though fully engaged in occupational duties, does not gain anything.**

In Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (5.18.22) it is stated

*mat-prāptaye ’jeśa-surāsurādayas tapyanta ugraṁ tapa aindriye dhiyaḥ ṛte bhavat-pāda-parāyaṇān na māṁ vindanty ahaṁ tvad-dhṛdayā yato ’jita*

**Перевод:**

**O supreme unconquerable Lord, when they become absorbed in thoughts of material enjoyment, Lord Brahmā and Lord Śiva, as well as other demigods and demons, undergo severe penances and austerities to receive my benedictions. But I do not favor anyone, however great he may be; unless he is always engaged in the service of Your lotus feet. Because I always keep You within my heart, I cannot favor anyone but a devotee.**

Śrī Kṛṣṇa bhakti rasa kadamba.

## Chapter V

### Text 1

*śrīdāmā adyā ca suhṛdo rādhikā yasya vallabhā*  
*??prasur?? yaśodā janakaḥ śrī nando braja vallabhāḥ*  
*saṅkarṣaṇa agraja yasya yasya vṛndāvanam purī*  
*murāri vandanaṁ yasya tasya vande padāmbujam*

**Перевод:**

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who is very dear to Śrīdāmā, who is a beloved of Rādhikā, who is the affectionate son of Śrī Nanda and Yaśodā, who is dear to all residents of Braja, whose elder brother is Saṅkarṣaṇa (Balarāma), whose abode is Vṛndāvana and who plays flute.**

### Text 2

*jaya śacī tanayā parama avatāra*  
*veda purāṇa nigama śruti smṛti sāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the son of Śacī, who is most magnanimous incarnation. He is the goal of Vedas, Purāṇas, Nigamas, Śrutis and Smṛtis.**

### Text 3

*ānanda-kāṇḍa nityānanda-rāya*  
*kali bhāva tāraṇa kāraṇa yāhāra kṛpāya*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the most blissful Lord Nityānanda by whose mercy one can cross the ocean of Kali-yuga.**

### Text 4

*jaya jaya pārṣada sakhā-gaṇa vṛnda*  
*jaya jaya śrī abhirāma sundarānanda*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to all the associates and friends of Lord. All glories to Śrī Abhirāma and Śrī Sundarānanda Prabhu.**

pg 2

### Text 5

*?? avaśyaka?? haya bhaktera bhakti aṅga sādi*  
*akāraṇe pratya vai teñi nityā kaṇā*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees are duty to execute the limbs of devotional service, failing which they incur sin. Therefore it is described as eternal.**

### Text 6

*karile abhīṣṭa siddhi na karile kṣati*  
*ei kathā kahilena sarva varṇa prati*

**Перевод:**

**By executing devotional service one achieves the desired goal and by neglecting it one incurs sin. This principle is applicable to all the four varṇas.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu it is stated

(1.2.9)

*ity asau syād vidhir nityaḥ sarva-varṇāśramādiṣu nityatve 'py asya nirṇītam ekādaśy-ādivat phalam*

**Перевод:**

**This prescription is applicable to the entire range of varṇāśrama followers alike-brahmacārīs, gṛhasthas, vānaprasthas, sannyāsīs, brāhmaṇas, kṣatriyas, vaiśyas and śūdras. It has been positively concluded that by steadily following this instruction one may attain results similar to those obtained by observing the ekādaśī-vrata or other such vows.**

### Text 7

*sādhana bhaktira aṅga āche bahu mata*  
*hari bhakti vilāsa ādi granthe su-vidita*

**Перевод:**

**There are various limbs of sādhana bhakti or regulative devotional service. They are elaborately described in Hari Bhakti Vilāsa.**

pg 3

### Text 8

*śrī rūpa gosvāmī tāhā varṇilena punar*  
*rasāmṛta sindhu granthe cātuḥ ṣaṣṭi krama*

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Rūpa Gosvāmī Prabhu has described them in his book Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu and divide them into sixty-four limbs of devotional service.**

The primary limbs of devotional service are described in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.74-77

*guru-pādāśraya tasmat kṛṣṇadīkṣādi śikṣaṇaṁ viśrambheṇa guroḥ sevā sādhuvartmānuvartanam sad-dharma-pṛcchā bhogādi tyāgaḥ kṛṣṇasya hetave nivāso dvārakādau ca gaṅgāderapi sannidhau vyāvahāreṣu sarveṣu yāvadarthānuvartitā harivāsara sammāno dhātryaśvatthādi gauravaṁ eṣāmatra daśaṅgānāṁ bhavet prārambharupatā*

**Перевод:**

**The primary ten limbs of devotional service :**

- **Taking shelter of the lotus feet of a spiritual master.**
- **To be initiated in Kṛṣṇa mantras by the spiritual master and receiving instructions about ones relationship with the Lord, the goal of life, and the process to achieve the goal.**
- **Serving spiritual master with firm faith.**
- **Following in the footsteps of the devotees.**
- **Inquiring about the religious principles approved and practiced by devotees.**
- **Giving up the enjoyments for the pleasure of Kṛṣṇa**
- **Residing in the Ganges**
- **Accepting wealth only as much as necessary to maintain once body and soul together.**
- **Properly following vow of Ekādaśī**
- **And to protect the prestige of trees such as aśvattha or banyan.**

**g 4**

### Text 9

*ei to kahila aṅga ?prārabdha? rūpa daśe*  
*vyatireke kahi śuna punar daśa śeṣe*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the first ten limbs of devotional service. Now please hear the next ten limbs.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.78-83 it is stated

*saṅga tyāgo vidūreṇa bhagavadvimukhair janaiḥ śiṣyādyananuvandhitvaṁ mahārambhādyanudyamaḥ vahu grantha kalābhyāsa vyākhyā vāda vivarjanam vyāvahāre ‘pyakārpaṇyaṁ śākādyavaśavartitā anya devān avajñā ca bhūtān udvegadāyitā sevā nāmāparādhanāṁ udbhavāvakāritā kṛṣṇa tad bhakta vidveṣa vinindādyasahiṣṇutā vyatireka tayāmīṣāṁ daśānāṁ syādanuṣṭhitīh*

pg 5

*asyās tatra praveśayā dvāratve ‘pyaṅga viṅśateh trayāṁ pradhānam evoktaṁ gurupādāśrayādikam*

**Перевод:**

**One should execute the next ten limbs of devotional service by refraining from such activities.**

- **One should give up the association of persons, who are averse to the Lord.**
- **One should not initiate unqualified candidate either by force, temptation or money.**
- **One should not endeavor for huge enterprises that are unfavorable for devotional service.**
- **One should not read or teach too many books.**
- **One should become strait forward in ordinary dealings.**
- **One should be equilibrium in loss or gain.**
- **One should not disrespect demigods.**
- **One should not cause pain to any living entity.**
- **One should not act in such a way that it will create offenses against the holy name or devotional service.**
- **One should not blaspheme or tolerate blasphemy of Kṛṣṇa and His devotees.**

**Although these twenty limbs are like a gateway for entering into the kingdom of devotional service. First three limbs beginning with “taking shelter of the lotus feet of the spiritual master” are the principle ones.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.84-95 states rest of the limbs of devotional service.

*dhṛtir vaiṣṇava cihnānāṁ harernāmakṣarasya ca nirmālyādeś ca tasyāgre tāṇḍavaṁ daṇḍavannatih abhyutthānamanuvrajyā gatiḥ sthāne parikramah arcanaṁ paricaryā ca gītam śaṅkitānāṁ japah vijñaptih stava pāṭhaś ca svādo naivedya pādyayoḥ dhūpamālyādisaurabhyaṁ śrī mūrteḥ spṛṣṭirīkṣaṇam ārātrikotsavadiś ca śravaṇaṁ tat kṛpekṣaṇam smṛtir dhyānaṁ tathā dāsyaṁ sakhyam ātma-nivedanam nija priyopaharaṇaṁ tad-arthe ’khila-ceṣṭitam sarvathā śaraṇāpattis tadīyānāñ ca sevanaṁ tadīyās tulasī śāstra mathurā vaiṣṇavādayaḥ yathā vaibhava sāmagrī sad goṣṭhībhir mahotsavah ūrjādaro viśeṣeṇa yatra janmādinādiṣu śraddhā viśeṣatah prītih śrī mūrteraṅghrisevane śrīmad-bhāgavatārthānām āsvādo rasikaiḥ saha sajatīyāśye snigdhe sādhau saṅgah svato vare nāma-saṅkīrtanaṁ śrī mathurā-maṇḍale sthitiḥ aṅgānāṁ pañcakasyāsya pūrvaṁ-vilikhitasya canikhila-śraiṣṭhyavodhāya punar apyatra kīrtanam iti kāya hṛṣīkāntah kāraṇānāṁ upāsanāh catuhṣaṣṭih pṛthak sāṅghātika bhedāt kramādināh*

**Перевод:**

- **One should mark his body with tilak and decorate his body with various signs such as conch and disk**
- **One should mark his body with the holy names of Hari**
- **One should accept flower garland which is offered to Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should dance before the Lord.**
- **One should offer obeisances to the Lord**
- **One should stand up whenever he sees arrival of the deity form of the Lord**
- **One should follow the Lords procession**
- **One should visit the places of pilgrimage**
- **One should circumambulate the deity, the adobe of the Lord and the Tulasī**
- **One should worship the Lord**
- **One should bath, decorate and dress the deity of the Lord.**
- **One should sing the glories of the Lord**
- **One should congregationally chant the holy names, forms, qualities and pastimes of the Lord**
- **One should chant the Lords names softly**
- **One should submit his mind and speech for the service of the Lord**
- **One should offer prayers to the Lord**
- **One should relish the remnants of the Lord**
- **One should drink the water that has washed the lotus feet of the Lord.**
- **One should smell the incense and flowers offered to the Lord**
- **A qualified person should touch the deity of the Lord.**
- **One should see the deity form of the Lord**
- **One should see aratik of the Lord and participate in festivals**
- **One should here the names, forms, qualities and pastimes of Śrī Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should await mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should remember the names, forms, qualities and pastimes of Śrī Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should properly meditate on the names, forms, qualities and pastimes of Śrī Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should serve the Lord as a menial servant**
- **One should establish friendship with the Lord**
- **One should surrender everything to the Lord**
- **One should offer the most favorable items to the Lord**
- **One should fully endeavor to please Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should fully surrender to Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should serve Tulasī the beloved of Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should study the scriptures such as ŚRĪMAD - BHĀGAVATAM**
- **One should reside at Mathurā**
- **One should serve the devotees of Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should serve the Lord according to ones capacity and accompanied by his associates**
- **One should specially follow the vow of kartika vrata**
- **One should observe and celebrate appearance day of the Lord**
- **One should maintain faith and love for serving the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa**
- **One should relish actual purport of Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam in association of like minded devotees**
- **One should associate with a pure devotee who is superior than himself**
- **One should congregationally chant the holy names of Śrī Hari**
- **One should reside Mathurā**

**Although last limbs is like have already been mentioned previously (10.13.34,36,45) yet in order to stress their importance the author has again repeated them.**

**In this way the sixty four limbs of devotional service which are to be practiced either individually or collectively through ones body, mind and senses has thus gradually been described**

- **Taking shelter of the lotus feet of the spiritual master**

### Text 10

*śrī guru pādāśraya āge karibe yatane*  
*śabda brahma param brahma niṣṇāta śānta sthāne*

**Перевод:**

**One should first of all take shelter of the spiritual master with topmost care. Such a sober spiritual master must be well acquainted with the transcendental sound vibration.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.97

*asmād guruṁ prapadyeta jijñāsuḥ śreya uttamam śābde pare ca niṣṇātaṁ brahmaṇy upaśamāśrayam*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore any person who seriously desires real happiness must seek a bona fide spiritual master and take shelter of him by initiation. The qualification of the bona fide guru is that he has realized the conclusions of the scriptures by deliberation and is able to convince others of these conclusions. Such great personalities, who have taken shelter of the Supreme Godhead, leaving aside all material considerations, should be understood to be bona fide spiritual masters.**

2\) Being initiated and instructed by spiritual master

### Text 11

*kṛṣṇa dīkṣā kari punaḥ bhajana lakṣaṇa*  
*bhagavat dharma śikṣā avaśya kāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One should take initiation from bona fide spiritual master and begin worship the Lord. One must also learn the teachings regarding ones constitutional duties.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.98

*tatra bhāgavatān dharmān śikṣedagurvātmadaivatah amāyayānuvṛtyā yaistuṣyedātmātmado hariḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Accepting the bona fide spiritual master as one’s life and soul and worshipable deity, the disciple should learn from him the process of pure devotional service. The Supreme Personality of Godhead, Hari, the soul of all souls, is inclined to give Himself to His pure devotees. Therefore, the disciple should learn from the spiritual master to serve the Lord without duplicity and in such a faithful and favorable way that the Supreme Lord, being satisfied, will offer Himself to the faithful disciple.**

3\) Serving the spiritual master with firm faith

### Text 12

*viśvāsa koriyā gurur karibe sevana*  
*guru sarvadā eva mayā kṛṣṇa tulya hañā*

**Перевод:**

**One should faithfully serve the spiritual master, for the spiritual master is worshipable by the demigods and he is as good as Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 13

*manuṣya buddhi na kariha na kariha anya jñāna*  
*ācārya rūpa āmi hoi kahe bhagavān*

**Перевод:**

**One should not consider the spiritual master as an ordinary human being. The supreme Lord said, “ I appear in this world as an ācārya.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.99

*ācāryaṁ māṁ vijāniyān nāvanmanyeta karhicit na martya-buddhyāsūyeta sarva-deva-mayo guruḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One should know the ācārya as Myself and never disrespect him in any way. One should not envy him, thinking him an ordinary man, for he is the representative of all the demigods.**

4\) Following in the foot steps of the devotees

### Text 14

*pūrva mahānta saba ye dharma ācarila*  
*sādhura vartma bali tāhāre kahila*

**Перевод:**

**The religious principles that the previous saintly persons have practiced are called the path of the saintly persons.**

### Text 15

*se pathe calilā āra āpadā na hañā*  
*ataeva sādhu patha kāra anveṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**By following the path of the saintly persons one does not fall into danger. Therefore one should endeavor to follow the path of the saintly persons.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.100

*sa mṛgyah śreyasām hetuḥ panthāh santāpa varjitah anvāptaśramam pūrve yena santha pratasthire*

**Перевод:**

**The path by which the previous great personalities have easily traversed and have achieved perfection is the mast beneficial and smooth. One should search for such path (Skanda Purāṇa)**

### Text 16

*śruti smṛti purāṇādi pañcarātra vidhi*  
*sādhu patha dekhāite saba prasiddhi*

**Перевод:**

**The rules and regulations which are described in the Śrutis, Smṛtis, purāṇas and pañcarātras are meant for supporting the path of saintly persons.**

### Text 17

*tāhāte ye bhakti bhava kaila nirūpaṇa*  
*tad anusāre ācarile āpadā khaṇḍana*

**Перевод:**

**If one practiced devotional service, which is, ascertain in these scriptures, all his distresses will be destroyed.**

### Text 18

*iha chādi ātyantika kare ācaraṇa*  
*bhakti nahe sei haya utpāta kāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**If one rejects these authorized processes of devotional service and practiced something else then it is not devotional service. It is simply a disturbance in the society.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.101 it is stated

*śruti-smṛti-purāṇādi-pañcarātra-vidhiṁ vinā aikāntikī harer bhaktir utpātāyaiva kalpate*

**Перевод:**

**“Devotional service of the Lord that ignores the authorized Vedic literatures like the Upaniṣads, Purāṇas and Nārada-pañcarātra is simply an unnecessary disturbance in society.”**

pg 12

### Text 19

*śāstra rahita bhakti kare ācaraṇa*  
*vidhi prāpta nahe sei utpāta janya hañā*

**Перевод:**

**If one engages in devotional service that is not supported or based on the scriptures, then it is simply a disturbance.**

**5 ) Inquiring about the regulative principles approved and practiced by devotees**

### Text 20

*sad-dharma jijñāsate yāra niṣṭhā mati*  
*acirāt tāhāra sarva siddhi śīghra gati*

**Перевод:**

**One who is interested in inquiring about the religious principles will soon achieve all perfection.**

**6) Giving up enjoyment for pleasure of Kṛṣṇa**

### Text 21

*kṛṣṇoddeśe bhogya priya karaye te jāne??*  
*āpane na khāya deya kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇe*  
*kṛṣṇaloke vasati haya atula sampada*  
*nāhi haya sei janera kakhana āpadā*

**Перевод:**

**A person who gives up all enjoyment for the pleasure of Kṛṣṇa and offers his most favorable object to the Lord and there after distributes it to the devotees, certainly goes to the abode of Kṛṣṇa and never face any distress.**

**pg 13**

**7) Residing in a holy place such as Dvārakā.**

### Text 22

*dvārāvatī pure vāsa sarva su-lakṣaṇa*  
*cātur-bhuja tulya haya sei sa bhajana*

**Перевод:**

**Residing in a holy place such as a Dvārakā is all auspicious for such residents are compared to the four handed associates of Nārāyaṇa.**

### Text 23

*vatsara ṣaṇ-māsā kimbā māsārdha pakṣa jāni*  
*dvārāvatī pure vāsa bahu bhāgya māne*

**Перевод:**

**Only out of good fortune one is able to live in the holy places such as Dvārakā even for a year, six month, one month or fifteen days.**

### Text 24

*ādi pade puruṣottama kṣetra gaṅgā vāsa*  
*ei saba jāni ha bhakti aṅga prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**The usage of the word “ādi” I this context refer to residing at purī, Vṛndāvana, Navadvīpa and or the banks of Ganges. Know it for the certain that these are all limbs of devotional service.**

**8) Accepting wealth only as much as necessary to maintain once body and soul together.**

### Text 25

*svakīya nirvāha hetu karibe grahaṇa*  
*yāhāte sei haya jāni ātmīya bharaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One should accept only as much wealth as it requires to maintain him and his family members.**

### Text 26

*nyūnam adhikam bhikṣā na kariha sañcaya*  
*paramārthe ‘cyuta hañā saṅgrahe niṣe haya*

pg 14

**Перевод:**

**Do not accumulate wealth more then you require, otherwise you will certainly fall from your path of spiritual life.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu 1.2.108

*yāvatā syāt sva-nirvāhaḥ svī-kāryāt tāvad artha-vit ādhikye nyūnatāyāñca cyavate paramārthataḥ*

(Naradiya Purana)

**Перевод:**

**“The bare necessities of life must be accepted, but one should not superfluously increase his necessities. Nor should they be unnecessarily decreased. One should simply accept what is necessary to help one advance spiritually.”**

9\) Properly follow the vow of Ekādaśī

### Text 27

*sarva-pāpa ??prasamana?? sarva dharmāśraya*  
*kṛṣṇa bhakti karī ekādaśī vrata haya*

**Перевод:**

**By properly following the vow of Ekādaśī all ones sinful reactions are destroyed. Respecting day of Ekādaśī increases ones devotion to Kṛṣṇa.**

**10) Protecting the prestige of Banyan trees.**

### Text 28

*aśvattha tulasī dhātrī go brāhmaṇa vaiṣṇava*  
*tadīya sevana haya bhakti aṅga anubhāva*

**Перевод:**

**By serving and meditating on Banyan tree, tulasī, earth, cow, brāhmaṇa and vaiṣṇava one cultivates the limbs of devotional service.**

**pg 15**

### Text 29

*ei to kahila daśāṅga pravṛtti rūpe nirūpaṇa*  
*niṣedha rūpe punar daśa karaha śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the ten primary limbs of devotional service, which one must follow. Now please hear the next ten limbs, which one must give up.**

**11) Giving up the association of persons, who are averse to Kṛṣṇa**

### Text 30

*śrī kṛṣṇa vimukta saṅga tyāga haya yena*  
*abhaktera saṅga haya dharma vināśana*

**Перевод:**

**One should give up the association of non-devotees, who are averse to Kṛṣṇa. For if one associates with them, his religious principles will be destroyed.**

### Text 31

*agni tape deha jaya pañjare vasati*  
*seha śreṣṭha tabu nahe abhakta saṅgāti*

**Перевод:**

**Even if one has to feel the pain of burning fire or live in a cage, still one should not associate with nondevotees.**

### Text 32

*śrī kṛṣṇa vimukta saṅge nāhi haya yena*  
*agni jvālā haite dukha pāṣaṇḍa saṅgama*

**Перевод:**

**One should be extremely careful not to associate with persons hostile to Kṛṣṇa, because association with the atheist is more painful than the burning fire.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.111)

*varaṁ huta-vaha-jvālā- pañjarāntar-vyavasthitiḥ na śauri-cintā-vimukha- jāna-saṁvāsa-vaiśasam*

pg 16

**Перевод:**

**“It is better to accept the miseries of being encaged within bars and surrounded by burning flames than to associate with those bereft of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Such association is a very great hardship.”**

### Text 33

*bahu śiṣya na karibe śraddhā hīnā dekhi*  
*hetu vādī ye vā gaṇa śāstra mate lekhe*

**Перевод:**

**One should not make too many disciples either by force or temptation. One should not accept unqualified persons as his disciples. This is the verdict of the scripture.**

## Text 34 (a)

*bahu grantha na pāḍibā bhakti grantha vine*  
*mana vicalita haya āgamādi daraśane*

**Перевод:**

**One should not read books other then devotional books, for by studying philosophical literatures ones mind becomes disturbed.**

## Text 34 (b)

*eka kale bahu karma nāhi ārambhe ‘be*  
*vyākhyā vadārtha adhi grantha ??teyagi ‘be??*

**Перевод:**

**One should not begin to perform many activities at a time. One should reject those books that are full of unfavorable explanations and commentaries.**

**15-16) Becoming strait forward in ordinary dealings and maintain equilibrium in loss and gain**

**pg 17**

### Text 35

*lābhālābhau apacaye śoka ??teyagibe??*  
*śoka roṣa krodha mohāviṣṭa na habe*

**Перевод:**

**One should give up lamentation in loss and happiness in gain. One should not be overwhelmed with lamentation, anger, illusion and envy.**

### Text 36

*śoka dite ābhāsa yāhāra citta haya*  
*tāra dehe govinda smaraṇa kaiche rāya*

**Перевод:**

**How can a person, whose mind is afflicted by lamentation remember the lotus feet of Govinda.**

### Text 37

*govinda smaraṇa vinu niṣphala jīvana*  
*ataeva śokādi sadā karibe te jāna*

**Перевод:**

**Ones life is useless without the remembrance of Govinda. Therefore one should always abandon lamentation etc.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.115) it is stated

*śokāmaṛṣaḍibhir bhāvair ākrāntāṁ yasyā mānasam kathaṁ tatra mukundasya sphurtisambhāvanā bhavet*

(Padma-purana)

**Перевод:**

**There is no possibility of awakening Kṛṣṇa Consciousness in the heart of a person, who is overwhelmed with lamentation and anger.**

pg 18

17\) Disrespecting the demigods

### Text 38

*anya deva tāra kabe na karī nindana*  
*nindā haya jāni aparādha kāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One should never disregard the demigods. If one does so he certainly commits offence.**

**18) Causing pain to living entities.**

### Text 39

*bhūtānudvegādāyi ta habe bhakta gaṇa*  
*prāṇī mātra hiṁsā na kariha kakhana*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees should never cause anxiety to other living entities. They should never become envious or violent to anyone.**

### Text 40

*sarva jive putra prāya sneha dekhi-yāra*  
*acire govinda haya suprasanna tāra*

**Перевод:**

**Govinda very pleased with a person, who affectionately treats all living entities as his own son.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.117) it is stated

*piteva putram karaṇo nodvejayati yo janam viśuddhasya hṛṣīkeśastūrṇaṁ tasya prasīdati*

(Mahabharata)

**Перевод:**

**Just as a father is affectionate and merciful towards his son similarly Lord Hṛṣīkeśa soon becomes pleased with that pure hearted person, who is compassionate and does not cause anxiety to any living entity.**

Pg 19

19\) Avoiding offences against the holy name or devotional service.

### Text 41

*sevā nāma aparādha varjiba yatane*  
*paścāte likhiba tāhā kari vivaraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**One should carefully avoid committing offences against the holy name and of the Lord. Later on these offences will be described in detail.**

**20) To not blaspheme Kṛṣṇa and His devotees or tolerate their blasphemy.**

### Text 42

*kṛṣṇa nindā asahiṣṇu habe ta bhakta gaṇe*  
*kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa bhakta nindā na kaibe śravaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees should never tolerate the blasphemy of Kṛṣṇa. They should not here any criticism against Kṛṣṇa and his devotees.**

### Text 43

*ye kare nindana sei asāṅkhya pātakī*  
*seha pātakī yebā śune tāhā thāki*

**Перевод:**

**Any one who blasphemes Lord Viṣṇu is most sinful person and anyone, who hears them is also very sinful.**

### Text 44

*viṣṇu nindaka janera mukha na dekhibe*  
*karṇe hasta diyā sthānāntare palāibe*

**Перевод:**

**Do not see the face of an offender of the feet of Lord Viṣṇu. Rather block your ears with hands and go away.**

pg 20

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu it is stated 1.2.121

*nindāṁ bhagavataḥ śṛṇvaṁs tat-parasya janasya vā tato nāpaiti yaḥ so ’pi yāty adhaḥ sukṛtāc cyutaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who fails to immediately leave the place where he hears criticism of the Supreme Lord or His faithful devotee will certainly fall down, bereft of his pious credit.**

### Text 45

*pravṛtti rupa daśa niṣedha rupa āra*  
*viṁśati aṅga ei karila pracāra*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus explained the twenty limbs of devotional service. The first ten of which are to be followed and the next ten of which are to be avoided.**

**21) Marking the body with tilaka etc.**

### Text 46

*vaiṣṇava cihna dhāraṇa bhakti aṅga hañā*  
*tulasī mālā ūrdhva puṇḍra mudrā dhāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Accepting the symbols of a vaiṣṇava such as wearing tulasī neck bead, marking the forehead and the body with the tilak and decorating the body with the marks of conch, disk etc. are symptoms of devotional service.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.122) it is stated

*ye kaṇṭhalagna tulasī nalinākṣā mālā ya vāhumūla paricihṇita śaṅkha cakrah ye vā lalātaphalake lasadūrdhva puṇḍrā te vaiṣṇavā bhuvanamāśu pavitrayanti*

(Padma Purana)

**Перевод:**

**Those, who always wear beads made with tulasī and lotus seed on their necks, decorate their arms with the marks of conch and disk, and who put tilak on their foreheads are called vaiṣṇavas. They can purify the entire world.**

22\) Marking the body with the holy names

### Text 47-48

*nāma kṣāra dhṛti haya bhakti aṅga lakṣaṇa*  
*kṛṣṇa nāmāṅkitā mudrā śrī gopī candana*  
*tulasī mālā vakṣa dhāraṇa ye kare*  
*kadā cita yama tāre paraśite nare*

**Перевод:**

**Decorating ones body with the holy names of Kṛṣṇa by using Gopīcandana is a limb of devotional service. Anyone who does so as well as wears tulasī beads on his neck is never touched by Yamarāja.**
