In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.123) it is stated

*harināmākṣara yutaṁ bhāle gopī mṛḍaṅkitam tulasī mālikoraskaṁ spṛśeyurna yaṁodbhatāh*

**Перевод:**

**One whose body is decorated with the writings of the holy names, whose forehead is marked with gopīchandana tilak and whose tulasī neck beads is hanging up to his chest, is no longer subjected to the punishment of Yamarāja.**

23\) Accepting flower garlands, which is offered to the Lord.

### Text 49

*nirmala ya dhṛti prasāde puṣpa caṇḍāla*  
*śrī loka kṛtārtha haya ye kare dhāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**A person who accepts flower garlands, flowers and sandal wood paste, which are offered to the Lord, becomes successful in his life.**

**pg 22**

**24) Dancing before the Lord**

### Text 50

*agre tāṇḍava kṛṣṇera kara ye nartana*  
*kakṣā bāḍāya karatāli nāma saṅkīrtana*  
*bhakti bhāve nṛtya kare hṛṣṭa ātma haya*  
*śarīrera pataka panka jaya palāiyā*

**Перевод:**

**One, who happily dance before Kṛṣṇa, with devotion, during congregational chanting of the Lord’s holy name and clap his hands, all sinful reactions from this body are immediately vanquished.**

### Text 51

*sādhāraṇa vṛkṣe yena rahe pakṣi-gaṇa*  
*karatāli sabda haite kare palāyana*  
*kṛṣṇāgra nartane taiche pataka pakṣi yata*  
*dūre palāiyā jaya haiyā ati bhīta*

**Перевод:**

**Just as by clapping hands under tree drive away all the birds from the tree, similarly by dancing before Kṛṣṇa all birds in the form of sinful reactions become frightened and fly away.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.128)

*nṛtyatāṁ śrī pater agre tālikā-vādanair bhṛśam uḍḍiyante śarīrasthāh sarve pātaka pakṣiṇah*

**Перевод:**

**Those, who repeatedly dance before the husband of Lakṣmī, while clapping their hands, all the birds in the form of sin fly away from their bodies.**

pg 23

25\) Offering obeisances to the Lord

### Text 52

*daṇḍavat nati kare śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇe*  
*daśa aśvamedha yājī nahe tāra same*

**Перевод:**

**One, who offers respectful obeisances at the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa is better than a person, who performed ten horse sacrifices.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.129) it is stated

*eko ‘pi kṛṣṇāya kṛtah praṇāmo daśāśvamedhāvabhṛthairna tulyah daśāśvamedhī punareti janma kṛṣṇa praṇāmī na punarbhavāya*

(Naradiya pūraṇa)

**Перевод:**

**The result of once offering obeisances to Śrī Kṛṣṇa can not be compared with the results of performing ten horse sacrifices (after exhausting his piety ) takes birth again, where as the person, who offers obeisances to the Lord does not have to take birth again.**

26\) Standing up to greet the Lord

### Text 53

*abhyutthāna kṛṣṇa dekhi ye kare sambhrame*  
*sarva pataka tāra khaṇḍāye sei kṣaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**One, who immediately stands up with awe and reverence, as soon as he sees the Lord, all his sinful reactions are at once destroyed.**

**27) Following the Lord during procession**

### Text 54

*kṛṣṇa paścād gamana anuvrajyā namo*  
*ratha dola yatra kale paścād te pāya*

*pg 24*

**Перевод:**

**Following the Lords procession during the festival such as ratha yatra and Holi is a limb of devotional service called anuvrajyā.**

28\) Visiting the places of pilgrimage.

### Text 55

*kṛṣṇa sthāne gati haya bhakti aṅga lakṣaṇa*  
*tīrtha kṣetra kṛṣṇālaya karite daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**Visiting the places of pilgrimage is a limb of devotional service. One should visit the holy places and the temples of Kṛṣṇa.**

**29) Circumambulating the Lord.**

### Text 56

*catur vara pradakṣiṇa kṛṣṇālaye kare*  
*sei āvartana punar na bhrame saṁsāre*

**Перевод:**

**One, who circumambulates the temple of Kṛṣṇa four times never travels in the material existence again.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.135)

*viṣṇuṁ pradakṣinī kurvan yastatrāvartate punah tadevāvartanaṁ tasya punarnāvartate bhave*

(Hari-Bhakti-Sudhodaya)

**Перевод:**

**Anyone, who circumambulates the deity or temple of the Lord Viṣṇu never returns in the material world.**

pg 25

30\) Worshiping the Lord

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.137) it is stated

*śuddhi nyāsādi pūrvāṅga karma nirvāha pūrvakam arcanantūpacārāṇāṁ syān mantreṇopapādanam*

(Skanda Purana)

**Перевод:**

**Offering of the materials or ingredients along with mantras to the Lord after one has finished purification of the elements and māntrikā-nyāsa is called** ***arcana*** **or worshiping the Lord.**

### Text 57

*bhakti kari kṛṣṇārcana kare yevājan*  
*śāśvata govinda pade se kare gamana*

**Перевод:**

**One, who worships Lord Kṛṣṇa with love and devotion, certainly goes back to the abode of Govinda.**

**31) Engaging in the service of the Lord.**

### Text 58

*kṛṣṇa paricaryā haya bhakti aṅga pradhāna*  
*sei paricaryā haya aneka vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**Engaging in the service of Kṛṣṇa is one of the principle limbs of devotional service. There are various types of such services.**

### Text 59

*mandira mārjana sevā pada pīṭha samarpaṇa*  
*cātra pādukā puṣpa mālyādi racana*

**Перевод:**

**This services includes cleaning the temple and paraphernalia of the Lord, holding umbrella to the Lord, offering shoes to the Lord and preparing flower garland for the Lord.**

**pg 26**

### Text 60

*kṛṣṇa paricaryāya kānde bahu janmera mālā*  
*triloka pavitra yaiche kare gaṅgā jala*

**Перевод:**

**By serving Lord Kṛṣṇa all ones contamination’s accumulated from time immemorial are at once vanquished just as the water of the Ganges purifies the tree worlds.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.142) it is stated

*yat-pāda-sevābhirucis tapasvinām aśeṣa-janmopacitaṁ malaṁ dhiyaḥ sadyaḥ kṣiṇoty anvaham edhatī satī yathā padāṅguṣṭha-viniḥsṛtā sarit*

**Перевод:**

**By the inclination to serve the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, suffering humanity can immediately cleanse the dirt, which has accumulated in their minds during innumerable births. Like the Ganges water, which emanates from the toes of the lotus feet of the Lord, such a process immediately cleanses the mind, and thus spiritual or Kṛṣṇa consciousness gradually increases.**

32\) Singing the glories of the Lord

### Text 61

*nirantara kṛṣṇa guṇa gaṇa harṣa-mane*  
*viśeṣe śrī kṛṣṇa bhakta ye saba brahmaṇe*  
*rudra gaṇa adhika haya brāhmaṇera gaṇa*  
*ante sei loka sukhe kṛṣṇa loke pāñā*

**Перевод:**

**If the devotees of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. who are already brahmanas always happily sing the glories of Kṛṣṇa, their glorification of Kṛṣṇa id superior than the glorification offered by Lord Śiva and ultimately they return to the abode of Kṛṣṇa.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.144) it is stated

*brāhmaṇo vāsudevākyaṁ gāyamāno’ niśaṁ param hareh sālokyamāpnoti rudra gānādhikaṁ bhavet iti*

(Linga Purana)

pg 27

**Перевод:**

**For brahmins engaging in singing songs is particularly prohibited. But if the brahmins constantly and exclusively sing the glories of the Lord then Lord regard such singing more than the singing of the glories by Lord Śiva.**

33\) Congregational chanting the holy names of the Lord.

### Text 62

*tat saṅkīrtanam trividha nāma*  
*kīrtanaṁ līlā kīrtanaṁ guṇādi kīrtanaṁ ca*

**Перевод:**

**There are three kinds of chanting namely chanting the holy names of the Lord, chanting glories of the Lords pastimes and chanting transcendental qualities of the Lord.**

a) Chanting the holy names of the Lord.

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1,2,146) it is stated

*kṛṣṇeti maṅgalam nāma yasya vāci pravartate bhaṣmī bhavanti rājendra mahāpātaka kotayah*

(Viṣṇu Dharma)

**Перевод:**

**O, best of the kings any one, who utters the most auspicious name “Kṛṣṇa” all his grave sinful reactions are burned to ashes.**

b) Chanting the glories of the Lords pastimes.

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.147) it is stated

*so ’haṁ priyasya suhṛdaḥ paradevatāyā līlā-kathās tava nṛsiṁha viriñca-gītāḥ añjas titarmy anugṛṇan guṇa-vipramukto durgāṇi te pada-yugālaya-haṁsa-saṅgaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O my Lord Nṛsiṁhadeva, by engaging in Your transcendental loving service in the association of devotees who are liberated souls \[haṁsas\], I shall become completely uncontaminated by the association of the three modes of material nature and be able to chant the glories of Your Lordship, who are so dear to me. I shall chant Your glories, following exactly in the footsteps of Lord Brahmā and his disciplic succession. In this way I shall undoubtedly be able to cross the ocean of nescience.**

c) Chanting the transcendental qualities of the Lord.

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.148) it is stated

*idaṁ hi puṁsas tapasaḥ śrutasya vā sviṣṭasya sūktasya ca buddhi-dattayoḥ avicyuto ’rthaḥ kavibhir nirūpito yad-uttamaśloka-guṇānuvarṇanam*

**Перевод:**

**Learned circles have positively concluded that the infallible purpose of the advancement of knowledge, namely austerities, study of the Vedas, sacrifice, chanting of hymns and charity, culminates in the transcendental descriptions of the Lord, who is defined in choice poetry.**

### Text 63-65

*janme janme yevā kare tapasyādi dharma*  
*veda patha jñāna yajña dāna veda karme sakala dharmeṇa phala iha kale jāni yāra mukhe guṇān kīrtana kṛṣṇera śuni paṇḍita saba nirūpila dharma sanātana parama puruṣārtha kṛṣṇa guṇānuvarṇanam*

**Перевод:**

**One, who engages in various pious activities such as undergoing austerity, studying the Vedas, performing sacrifices and giving charity, which are prescribed in the Vedas, attain the results of such activities in this life time. But the learned scholars have ascertained that chanting the transcendental qualities of the Lord is an eternal occupation of the living entities. It bestows one the supreme goal of life.**

**34) Chanting the holy names softly.**

### Text 66

*japa kṛṣṇa nāma mantra su-labhā uccāraṇa*  
*śrī yutera kārikā tāhe karaha śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Uttering the holy names of Kṛṣṇa very softly is called japa. Please hear the explanation of Śrī Rupa Gosvāmī in this regard.**

pg 29

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.149) it is stated

*mantrasya sulaghūcchāro japa ityabhidhīyate*

**Перевод:**

**The soft chanting of the mantra is called japa.**

### Text 67

*kṛṣṇāya nāma ei mantrera raja kāya*  
*catur-varga phala prāpta japa mātra haya*

**Перевод:**

**I offer my respectful obeisances to Kṛṣṇāya namah this is the king of mantras. By chanting this mantra one can attain the four objective of life.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.150) it is stated

*kṛṣṇāya nāma ityeṣa maṅtrah sarvārtha sādhakah bhaktānāṁ japatāṁ bhūpa svarga mokṣa phala pradah*

(Padma Purana)

**Перевод:**

**O, king the mantra “Kṛṣṇāya namah” or “I offer my respectful obeisances to Kṛṣṇa” bestow all benedictions. Those devotees of Hari, who chant this mantra, become liberated and attain Vaikuṇṭha.**

35\) Submitting oneself to Kṛṣṇa.

### Text 68-69

*vijñāpanam haya kṛṣṇe tina rūpa jāni*  
*prārthanātmikā tathā vijñapana vāṇī*  
*dainyavodhikā āra lālasāmayī kahe*  
*ei rūpe vijñāpana tina rūpa haye*

**Перевод:**

**Submitting oneself to Kṛṣṇa is a limb of devotional service and it is divided into three categories namely: a) samprārthanātmikā, very feelingly offering prayers; b) dainyavodhikā, humbly submitting oneself; c) lālasāmayī, desiring some perfectional stage. a) Submission through prayers**

### Text 70-72

*yuvatīra mana yāñā yuvakera? prati*  
*yuvara prema haya yuvati te ati??? loka-lajjā nāhi māne caḍila na caḍe viccheda haile prema atiśaya bade taiche āmāra mati rahu kṛṣṇa preme prārthanātmikā vijñāpana ityādi vidhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“Just as the mind of a young man automatically attracted towards a young woman and mind of a young woman automatically attracted towards young man, they do not care for embarrassment more even their love increases, when they are separated. Similarly let my mind be attached to love of Śrī Kṛṣṇa in the same way.” This is an example of submission through prayers.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.153) it is stated

*yuvatīnāṁ yathā yūni yūnāñca yuvatau yathā mano’bhiramate tadvan mano’bhiramatāṁ tvayi*

(Padma Purana)

**Перевод:**

**“O, Lord just as minds of young men are attracted to a young woman and minds of a young women are attracted to young man, let my mind be properly attached to You in the same way.”**

b) Submission through humility

### Text 73

*āmā sama nāhi pāpī aparādhī āna*  
*lajjayā ki nivediba ohe bhagavāna*

**Перевод:**

**O, Lord there is none more sinful and offender than me. I am so ashamed to even pray to You.**

**pg 31**

### Text 74

*sarva guṇa hīne dayā ācārye tomāra*  
*akiñcanāmā vahi keho nāhi āra*

**Перевод:**

**You are naturally compassionate to those, who have no qualifications and there is no one more unqualified then me.**

### Text 75

*āmi ki kariba dainya tomāra caraṇe*  
*dainya pātra tomāra brahmādi deva gaṇe.*

**Перевод:**

**What humility can I display at your lotus feet. Actually the demigods headed by Brahma possess the quality of humility.**

### Text 76

*āmi caḍa tomāra prabhu ki jāni bhakati*  
*sadāśivādi deva yāra bhakta khyāti*

**Перевод:**

**I am the most insignificant person and I do not know anything about Your devotional service. The demigods headed by Lord Śiva are actually Your renowned devotees.**

### Text 77

*indra candra dik-pāla saba kara gaṇana*  
*tāhā madhye āmi jīva kīṭa kṣudropamā*

**Перевод:**

**Indra the king of heaven Candra the moon god and the Dik-pālas- predominating deities of the four directions are counted amongst Your servants. In compare to them I am the most insignificant creature.**

### Text 78

*kibā nivediba prabhu tomāra caraṇe*  
*ye ucita haya prabhu kara akiñcane*

pg 32

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, O Lord, how can I submit myself to your lotus feet. Do as you please to this poor person.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.154) it is stated

*mattulyo nāsti pāpātmā nāparādhī ca kaścana parihāre’pi lajjā me kiṁ vruve puruṣottama*

(Padma Purana)

**Перевод:**

**O Lord Puruṣottama there is no greater sinner and offender than me, therefore I am even destroy my sinful reactions.**

a) Submission through hankering.

### Text 79

*kabe āmi saba tejī gṛha dhana jāna*  
*tomāra vihāra sthala kariba brāhmaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, when will I give up my house, wealth, followers and wonder through out the places where You performed pastimes.**

### Text 80

*yamunāra tire tire nāma guṇa gāya*  
*tāṇḍava pariba kabe ānandita haya*

**Перевод:**

**When will I happily dance while chanting Your names and qualities on the banks of Yamuna.**

### Text 81

*rāma kṛṣṇa govinda gopāla vanamāli*  
*nāma gaṇa koriyā brāhmaṇo kutūhalī*

**Перевод:**

**When will I eagerly wonder while chanting Your holy names such as Rāma, Kṛṣṇa, Govinda, Gopāla and Vanamāli.**

### Text 82

*ei rupa lālasa karī abhilāṣa*  
*kariba śrī aṅga sevā haiyā tava daśa*

**Перевод:**

**With such intense desire in my mind, when will I become Your humble servant and engaged in Your personal service.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.156) it is stated

*kadāhaṁ yamunā-tīre nāmāni tava kīrtayan udbāṣpah puṇḍarīkākṣa racayiṣyāmi tāṇḍavam*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Lord Puṇḍarīkākṣa, while chanting Your holy name with tears in my eyes, when shall I dance in ecstasy on the bank of the Yamunā?’**

36\) Offering prayers to the Lord.

### Text 83

*śrī kṛṣṇa stava patha jihvāya yevā kare*  
*devādir vandya sei bhava sindhu tāre*

**Перевод:**

**Any one, who offer selected prayers to Śrī Kṛṣṇa becomes worshipable by the demigods and crosses the ocean of material world.**

### Text 84

*gautamīya stava raja karibe paṭhana*  
*nija kṛta stotra kimbā gītādi vacana*

**Перевод:**

**One should recite, prayers described in Gautamya tantra and Bhagavad – Gītā. One should also offer prayers composed by himself.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.157) it is stated

*proktā manīṣibhir gītā stavarājādayah stavāh*

**Перевод:**

**Learned scholars have ascertained Śrīmad Bhagavad - Gītā and the prayers mentioned in gautamīya tantra as prayers to Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

37\) Honouring the remnants of the Lord.

### Text 85

*śrī kṛṣṇa mahāprasāda ye kare bhakṣaṇa*  
*ayuta yajñera phala tāra naya sama*

**Перевод:**

**One, who honors the remnants of Śrī Kṛṣṇa is superior then one, who has performed ten thousand sacrifices.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.160) it is stated

*naivedyamannaṁ tulasīvimiśraṁ vīseṣatah pādajalena siktam yo’śnāti nityaṁ purato murareh prāpṇoti yajñāyutakolipuṇyam*

**Перевод:**

**One, who regularly honors the remnants of Lord Murari mixed with tulāsī leaves attain merit of performing ten thousand vedic sacrifices.**

38\) Drinking the water that has washed the lotus feet of the Lord.

### Text 86-87

*nāhi kare dāna yajña anya śubha kriyā*  
*pādodaka pāna kare ānandita haiyā viṣṇu loka vasati haya śaśvata tāhāra padma purāṇe śuna tāhāra vicāra*

pg 35

**Перевод:**

**If one does not give in charity perform sacrifices or engage in other pious activities, but simply drinks the water that has washed the lotus feet of the Lord with great happiness, then he certainly resides in the abode of the Lord Viṣṇu. This is verdict of Padma Purana.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.161) it is stated

*na dānaṁ na habiryeṣāṁ svādhyāyo na surārcanam tepi pādodakaṁ pītvā prayānti paramāṁ gatim*

**Перевод:**

**Those, who do not execute any pious activities such as charity, performing sacrifices and worshiping demigods, can also attain supreme destination simply by drinking water from the lotus feet of the Lord.**

39\) Smelling incense and flowers that are offered to the Lord.

### TEXT 88-89

*kṛṣṇa datta dhūpa śeṣa aghrana? ye laye gaṇḍa puṣpa nirmalādi dhāraṇa karahe sarva papa nāśa haya tat kṣaṇe tāhārahari bhakti sudhodaye tāhāra vicāra*

**Перевод:**

**A person, who smell the incense, sandalwood paste and flowers, which are offered to the Lord Kṛṣṇa, is at once relieved from the reactions of sinful activities. This is confirmed in Hari Bhakti Sudhodaye.**

**pg 36**

**40) Touching the deity form of the Lord.**

### Text 90-91

*śuci haiyā sa pavitra sa śraddhita māne*  
*śrī mūrti karaye sparśa bhakti tāra bhane?*  
*aśeṣa vidhāna papa tat kṣaṇe khaṇḍāye*  
*saba abhilāṣa pūrṇa tat kṣaṇe se haye*

**Перевод:**

**If a person with purified state touches the deity form of the Lord with faith and devotion then his unlimited sinful reactions are immediately destroyed. All the desires of such a person are fulfilled.**

**41) Looking at the deity form of the Lord.**

### Text 92

*śrī mūrti daraśana phala varāha purāṇe*  
*pṛthivīra prati kaṇa varāha āpane*

**Перевод:**

**The results of seeing the deity form of the Lord are personally described by the Lord Varāha to Mother Earth in Varāha Purāṇa.**

### Text 93

*vṛndāvane govinda rūpa ye karaye daraśane*  
*svapneha na haya tāra yamālaya gamana*  
*(Varāha Purana)*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone, who sees the deity form of Govinda in Vṛndāvana does not go to the abode of Yamāraja even in a dream.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.166) it is stated

*vṛndāvane tu govindaṁ ye paśyanti vasundhare na te yamapuraṁ yānti yānti puṇyakṛtaṁ gatim*

**Перевод:**

**O Vasundhare (earth), those who see Śrī Govinda in Vṛndāvana no longer go to abode of Yamāraj. Rather they attain the same destination, which is attained by the pious people.**

42\) Watching the Lord during aratik and festivals.

### Text 94

*āratira kale kṛṣṇa mukta sandarśana*  
*brahma hatyādi papa tāra khaṇḍāye tat kṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Just by seeing the face of Kṛṣṇa during the aratik, ones sinful reactions such as killing brāhmaṇa is at once counteracted.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.167) it is stated

*kotayo brahma-hatyānāṁ-agmyāgamakoṭayah dahatyālokamātreṇa viṣṇoh sārātrikaṁ mukhaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**Beholding the face of Lord Viṣṇu during aratik destroys millions of grave sins such as killing brāhmaṇa and intimately associating with gurus wife.**

### Text 95-96

*dola yatra ratha yatra rasa mahotsava*  
*ye kare daraśana kṛṣṇera vihāra vaibhava caṇḍālādi daraśana mātre mahā pūta hana bhakti bhāve tāra haya deva tāra sama*

**Перевод:**

**Those, who participate in the festivals of Holi, Ratha-yatra, rasa-līlā pastimes becomes fully purified even if they are born in the family of dog eater. On the strength of devotional service they become equal to the demigods.**

**43) Hearing the names, forms, qualities, pastimes with the Lord.**

### Text 97-98

*sei to śravaṇa haye trividha lakṣaṇa*  
*nāma śravaṇa āra līlādi śravaṇa*  
*guṇa śravaṇa ei trividha kahena*  
*tāhāte sadā haraṇa gosāi varṇena*

**Перевод:**

**The hearing is divided into three categories. According to Rūpa Gosvāmī they are hearing about the holy names, pastimes and qualities of the Lord.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.170) it is stated

*śravaṇam nāma-carita-guṇādīnāṁ śrutirbhavet*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing about the names characteristics and qualities of the supreme Lord is called** ***śravaṇam*** **or hearing.**

a) Hearing about the holy names of the Lord.

### Text 99

*kṛṣṇa nāma mahāmantra ye kare śravaṇa*  
*sarva papa roga śoka khaṇḍāye takhana*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone, who hears the holy names of Hare Kṛṣṇa Mahāmantra, all his sinful reactions, diseases and lamentation are at once destroyed.**

### Text 100

*saṁsāra sarpate daṣṭa naṣṭa yata jāna*  
*kṛṣṇa nāma haye tāhe viśāpa haraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Those, who are bitten by the poisonous snake of material existence the holy names of Śrī Kṛṣṇa takes away the poison from their bodies.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.171) it is stated

*saṁsāra-sarpa-daṣṭa-nāṣṭa-ceṣṭaika-bheṣajam kṛṣṇeti vaiṣṇavaṁ mantraṁ śrutvā mukto bhavennanrah*

**Перевод:**

**The Vaiṣṇava mantra of Hare Kṛṣṇa Mahāmantra is the only remedy of a person, who is unconscious due to being bitten by the snake of material existence. Just by hearing this mantra one becomes liberated.**

b) Hearing about the pastimes of the Lord.

### Text 101-103

*śrī kṛṣṇa caritra līlā bālyādi ācaraṇa*  
*ānandita mana haiyā ye kare śravaṇa*  
*sādhu mukhe vigalita kṛṣṇa līlāmṛta*  
*karṇa pathe kare pāna tāhā parīkṣita*  
*se amṛta pāne kṣudhā tṛṣṇā jaya dūre*  
*kṣudra tṛṣṇā viṣaya bāḍhite nāre tare*

**Перевод:**

**One, who joyfully hears the transcendental characteristics and pastimes of Kṛṣṇa, following in the footsteps of king Parīkṣit, who heard the nectarian pastimes of Kṛṣṇa from the topmost devotee Śukadevā. His hunger and thirst will be mitigated. Such a person will never be disturbed by hunger, thirst or sense enjoyments.**

It is described in Śrīmad - bhāgavatam (10.1.13)

*naiṣātiduḥsahā kṣun māṁ tyaktodam api bādhate pibantaṁ tvan-mukhāmbhoja- cyutaṁ hari-kathāmṛtam*

**Перевод:**

**Because of my vow on the verge of death, I have given up even drinking water, yet because I am drinking the nectar of topics about Kṛṣṇa, which is flowing from the lotus mouth of Your Lordship, my hunger and thirst, which are extremely difficult to bear, cannot hinder me.**

c) Hearing about the transcendental qualities of the Lord.

### Text 104-105

*kṛṣṇa guṇānuvāda vipada vināna*  
*tāhāra śravaṇe yāhāra sadā abhilāṣa*  
*sabāmaṅgala tāra khaṇdaya tat kṣaṇe*  
*vimala bhakti haya śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**A person, who always desires to hear the transcendental qualities of kṛṣṇa, which destroys all one’s dangers, all his inauspiciousness are immediately vanquishes and such a person attains pure devotional service unto the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.173) it is stated

*yas tūttamaḥ-śloka-guṇānuvādaḥ saṅgīyate ’bhīkṣṇam amaṅgala-ghnaḥ tam eva nityaṁ śṛṇuyād abhīkṣṇaṁ kṛṣṇe ’malāṁ bhaktim abhīpsamānaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The person who desires pure devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa should hear the narrations of Lord Uttamaḥśloka’s glorious qualities, the constant chanting of which destroys everything inauspicious. The devotee should engage in such listening in regular daily assemblies and should also continue his hearing throughout the day.**

44\) Expecting for the mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

### Text 106-107

*kṛṣṇa anukampā kṛpā-dṛṣṭi haye jāni*  
*āpanāte kṛpāra bhajana kare māni*  
*ghare thāki kāya mana vākye kare nati*  
*dayā bhāgī mukti padera kṛpā-bale tathi*

**Перевод:**

**One should consider himself a candidate to receive kṛṣṇa’s causeless merciful glance. He should stay at home and offer obeisances to the Lord with his body, mind and speech. Then by the mercy of the Lord he became qualified to attain liberation.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.174) it is stated

*tat te ’nukampāṁ su-samīkṣamāṇo bhuñjāna evātma-kṛtaṁ vipākam hṛd-vāg-vapurbhir vidadhan namas te jīveta yo mukti-pade sa dāya-bhāk*

**Перевод:**

**My dear Lord, one who earnestly waits for You to bestow Your causeless mercy upon him, all the while patiently suffering the reactions of his past misdeeds and offering You respectful obeisances with his heart, words and body, is surely eligible for liberation, for it has become his rightful claim.**

45\) Remembering the lotus feet of the Lord

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.175)

*yathā kathañcinmanasā sambandhah smṛtir ucyate*

**Перевод:**

**To somehow or other concentrate one’s mind on the Lord is called** ***smṛti*** **or remembrance.**

### Text 108

*śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇa yadi māne smṛti kare*  
*sarva kalyāṇa yukta haya sei nāre*

**Перевод:**

**If a person constantly remembers the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, he becomes all auspicious.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.176) it is stated

*smṛte sakala kalyāṇa bhājanaṁ yatra jāyate puruṣantamajaṁ nityaṁ brajāmi śaraṇaṁ harim*

(Viṣṇu Purana)

**Перевод:**

**I take shelter of that unborn eternal Lord Hari, whose very remembrance makes one allauspicious.**

46\) Meditating on the form, qualities and pastimes of the Lord.

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.178) it is stated

*dhyānam rupa guṇa krīḍā sevādeh suṣṭhu cintanam*

**Перевод:**

**To properly remember about the forms, qualities, pastimes and service of the Lord is called dhyāna or meditation.**

a) Meditating on the form of the Lord.

### Text 109-110

*aśeṣa pāpera pāpī yadi seha haya*  
*śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇa padma hṛdaye bhāvāya*  
*sarva papa nāśa haya kṛṣṇa pada dhyāne*  
*ei kathā kahilena nṛsiṁha purāṇe*

**Перевод:**

**Even if one is unlimitedly sinful, but if he meditates on the lotus feet of Sri Kṛṣṇa within his heart then all his sinful reactions are destroyed. This is confirmed in** ***Nṛsiṁha Purāṇa.***

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.179) it is stated

*bhagavaccaraṇadvandva-dhyānaṁ nirdvandvamīritam pāpino’pi prasaṅgena vihitaṁ suhitaṁ paraṁ*

**Перевод:**

**If one meditates on the lotus feet of the Lord than he does not have to suffer the pangs of miseries born from heat or cold. This is verdict of learned scholars. Simply by the remembrance of these lotus feet one become enriched with all auspiciousness.**

b) meditating on the qualities of the Lord.

### Text 111

*ye kare smaraṇa sadā śrī kṛṣṇera guṇa*  
*vaikuṇṭha vasati haya sarva pap hīnā*

**Перевод:**

**One, who constantly meditates on the transcendental qualities of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, becomes freed from all sinful reactions and goes back to Vaikuṇṭha.**

**c) Meditating on the pastimes of the Lord.**

### Text 112-113

*kṛṣṇa līlā carita sarva mādhuryera sāra*  
*adbhuta sarva līlā vrajera vihāra*  
*sei saba carita citte karaye dheyāna*  
*sarva dukha haite parā tārā sabe pāna*

**Перевод:**

**The wonderful characteristics and pastimes of Kṛṣṇa in Braja are the essence of all sweetness. Anyone, who meditates on these pastimes becomes freed from all material miseries.**

**d) Meditating on the service of the Lord.**

### Text 114

*sevā dhyāna tāhe kahe karaha śravaṇa*  
*mānasika upacāra kṛṣṇe samarpaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear how one should meditate on the service of the Lord. One should offer various ingredients to Kṛṣṇa within his mind.**

### Text 115

*brahma vaivartena kathā kāra avadhāna*  
*pratiṣṭhāna pure pūrve sumantu ākhyāna*

**Перевод:**

**Please hear attentively the story of Sumanta, who lived at Pratiṣṭhānapur long long age. This story is found in** ***Brahma Vaivarta Purana.***

### Text 116

*sei pure vipra rahe ati akiñcanā*  
*kona sāmagrī nāi vitaraṇe dāna*

**Перевод:**

**In this city there was a poor brāhmaṇa. He had no assets or wealth to distribute to others.**

### Text 117

*ekadina sei vipra gela paryaṭana*  
*munira sabhāke gelānandita māne*

**Перевод:**

**One day this brāhmaṇa went out for pilgrimage. In the course of his happy travelling soon he arrived at the assembly of sages.**

### Text 118

*sei to sabhāya śune vaiṣṇava dharma cara*  
*mānasa sevate haya siddhi sabākāra*

**Перевод:**

**In that assembly he learnt about the characteristics of vaiṣṇava dharma that by serving through the mind one can achieve perfection.**

### Text 119

*eta śuni sei punar daridra brāhmaṇa*  
*godāvarī tire asi karila snapana*

**Перевод:**

**After hearing in this way the poor brāhmaṇa resumed his traveling to the holy places, He came to the bank of the Godāvarī river and took a bath in the water.**

### Text 120

*snāna tilaka mudrā āsana śuddhi karī*  
*aṅga nyāsa prāṇāyama ācari*

**Перевод:**

**After taking bath, he decorated his body with tilak and various auspicious signs. Then he purified his sitting place, his entire body and exercised his breathing process.**

### Text 121

*mudrā bāndhi dṛḍha āsane samādhi karila*  
*sarvendriya vaśa karī dṛḍha citta haila*

**Перевод:**

**Thereafter he sat in a particular posture with determination and concentrated his mind on the supreme. In this way he controlled his senses and his mind become fixed.**

### Text 122-123

*māne ta nirmāṇa kaila ratna-siṁhāsana*  
*mane mukta pravāla vividha gathana?? pattambara jadathomp?? vicitra cāmara candra tapa?? jhalamala śobhita upara*

**Перевод:**

**He created a beautiful jeweled throne with his mind and decorated it with diamonds, pearls and corals various attractive chamaras were hang on all sides and a beautiful canopy was hung on the top of the throne.**

### Text 124

*ratnāsane vasilā madhye ta govinda*  
*catur-dike sevā kare pāriṣada vṛnda*

**Перевод:**

**The brāhmaṇa then had his beloved Lord Govinda sit on that jeweled throne and had Him served by his associates.**

### Text 125

*manera mānase vipra kare ārādhana*  
*ratna pādya pātre kāra ya pada prakṣālana*

**Перевод:**

**Then he worshipped the Lord with in his mind. At first, he offered water to the Lord in a pot bedecked with jewels.**

### Text 126

*ācamana snigdha jala malayaja gandha*  
*śubha puṣpa dhūpa dīpa kariyā prabandha*

**Перевод:**

**Thereafter he bathed the Lord with pure cool water scented with sandal oil. Then the brāhmaṇa gradually offered fragrant flowers, incense and ghee lamp.**

### Text 127

*divya nista?? bhojya dadhi dugdha kṣīra cena??*  
*pāka āmra rambhā phala sītā miśrera pāna*

**Перевод:**

**In his mind the brāhmaṇa offered various foodstuffs such as sweets, yogurt, milk, sweet rice, curd, ripe mangos, bananas and sugar candy to the Lord.**

### Text 128

*punar pādya pātre deya acamanya baṭī*  
*punar pada prakṣālana kare laiyā jhāri*

**Перевод:**

**Then he again offered water to the Lord to wash hands and mouth. He also offered Him water to wash the feet.**

### Text 129

*punaś ca palanka?? kare apūrva āsana*  
*tāhe vasāiyā kare tāmbūla arpaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Thereafter he made a beautifully decorated bed and after making the Lord sitting on the bed, he offered betel nuts.**

### Text 130

*cāmara laiyā vāyu kare śyāma aṅge*  
*kṛṣṇa pārṣada daśa anugata saṅge*

**Перевод:**

**He fanned the Lord with a camara under the subordination of his associates.**

### Text 131

*aguru kuṁkum gandha karpūra miśāla*  
*śrī aṅge bale?? pāna deya kaṇṭha mālā*

**Перевод:**

**Then he offered sandalwood paste mixed with aguru, camphor and kuṁkum on the transcendental body of the Lord and offered him a flower garland.**

### Text 132

*śveta cātra vibhūṣaṇa caraṇādi krame*  
*nānā ābharaṇa deya ye sāje yekhāne*

**Перевод:**

**He decorated each and every limbs of the Lord with various ornaments beginning from his lotus feet up to the head.**

### Text 133

*caraṇe nūpura bāṅka?? kare samarpaṇa*  
*koṭile kiṅkiṇī vāsa pitā vāśana*

**Перевод:**

**He decorated the Lords feet with ankle bells anklets, his waist with attractive chain and dressed Him with yellow garments.**

### Text 134

*vakṣe hara?? kaustubhamaṇi karṇe te kuṇḍala*  
*tāra balaya?? hate anguri?? jhalamala*

**Перевод:**

**He offered a necklace made of Kaustubha gem to the Lord and decorates His ears with earrings. He also put bangles and rings on the Lords hands.**

### Text 135

*alaka tilaka bhāle candanera bindu*  
*mayūra śikhaṇḍa cura?? śarat-kālera cāṅda*

**Перевод:**

**He decorated the Lords forehead with the marks of tilaka and dots of sandalwood paste. He put peacock feather on the crown of the Lord as He looked like the moon of the autumn.**

### Text 136

*sevā karī mānasika karaye darśana*  
*nava jaladhara tanu cikkaṇa varaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**After serving the Lord in this way within his mind, the brāhmaṇa took darśan of the Lord, who resembled like the beautiful new clouds.**

### Text 137

*darśana arpaṇa punar kare laiyā deya*  
*pādukā yugala nija hate karī neya??*

**Перевод:**

**Then he offered the mirror to the Lord and placed the shoes in front of the Lord with his own hands.**

### Text 138

*tatpara karaye prabhura pada samba hana*  
*vicitra mandira madhye cintaye śayana*

**Перевод:**

**Thereafter the brāhmaṇa massaged the lotus feet of the Lord and put him to sleep with in a wonderful temple.**

### Text 139

*ei rūpe kare sevā sei dvijavara*  
*sevā samādhiya punar cale nija ghara*

**Перевод:**

**In this way that exalted brāhmaṇa regularly serves the Lord within his mind. After completing his service, he returns home.**

### Text 140

*ei rupa mānasa sevā kare dine dine*  
*mahā-sukhe thāke vipra dukha nāhi jāne*

**Перевод:**

**By serving in this way the brāhmaṇa achieves great happiness and does not feel any distress.**

### Text 141

*eka dinera tāhe śuno apūrva kathana*  
*samādhistha haiyā kare mānasa sevana*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear about the incident that took place one day, while he was serving the Lord in his mind.**

### Text 142

*saghṛta paramānna māne karila randhana*  
*suvarṇa nirmita pātre karila besana??*

**Перевод:**

**One day the brāhmaṇa cooked sweet rice with ghee in his mind and after serving it on a golden plate he brought it before the Lord.**

### Text 143

*kṛṣṇe samarpaṇa kaila ānandita haiyā*  
*haste dhari svarṇa pātra urdha kariyā*

**Перевод:**

**He happily offered the sweet rice to Kṛṣṇa while holding it in his raised hands.**

### Text 144

*uṣṇa haya pāche bhāvi angula?? tāhe dila*  
*mānase paramānna sei uṣṇa lagila*

**Перевод:**

**He mentally put his finger in the sweet rice to check if it was too hot and he felt that sweet rice was quite hot.**

### Text 145

*uṣṇānna prabhuke dila akṣepa kare*  
*samādhi haila bhaṅga bhāvita antare*

**Перевод:**

**With lamentation, he offered the hot sweet rice at that time his trance was broken.**

### Text 146

*bāhirera aṅguṣṭha dagdha hai yācche tāhāra*  
*mānasa sevate kṛṣṇa santuṣṭa aparā*

**Перевод:**

**To his utter astonishment, he saw that his finger was burnt, but Lord Kṛṣṇa was very pleased by his service through the mind.**

### Text 147

*sei vipra svadehe pāila kṛṣṇa dhāma*  
*vākya mana agocara haya yei sthāna*

**Перевод:**

**That brāhmaṇa returned to the abode of Kṛṣṇa on that very life. He reached the supreme destination, which is beyond the reach of one’s mind and speech.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.182) it is stated

*mānasenopacārena paricarya hariṁ sadā pare vāṅmanasā ‘gamyaṁ taṁ sākṣāt pratipedire*

**Перевод:**

**By happily serving Lord Hari while offering various ingredients through the minds, many persons have attained the supreme destination, which is beyond the perception of speech and mind.**

47\) Serving the Lord.

### Text 148

*dāsya bhava haya tāhe dvividha lakṣaṇa*  
*kiṁ kara dāsya āra dāsye karmārpaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**There are two kinds of servitorship in the mood of an unalloyed servant and servitorship of offering the fruits of one’s karma to Kṛṣṇa.**

**a) Servitorship in the mood of unalloyed servant.**

### Text 149

*yāra ceṣṭā kāya mana vākyendriya gaṇe*  
*niravadhi ceṣṭā haya kṛṣṇa dāsya karme*

**Перевод:**

**One, who simply endeavors to serve the Lord with his body, mind and speech constantly, his service is known as service on the mood of an unalloyed servant.**

**b) Servitorship of offering the fruits of karma to the Lord.**

### Text 150

*svābhāvika karma japa dhyāna pūjā ādi*  
*kṛṣṇe samarpaṇa haya seha dāsya vidhi*

**Перевод:**

**When one offers the fruits of ones activities, rituals, chanting mantras, meditation and worship to Kṛṣṇa, it is called servitorship of offering the fruits of karma to the Lord.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.183) it is stated

*dāsyaṁ karmārpaṅaṁ tasya kaiṅkaryamapi sarvathā*

**Перевод:**

**Although some people consider that the offering the fruits of one’s activities to the Lord is serving the Lord, but to consider that “I am the eternal servant of the Lord” is actually the platform of servitorship.**

48\) Establishing friendship with the Lord.

### Text 151

*sakhyam dvividham viśvāsa*  
*sakhyam mitra sakhyam ca*

**Перевод:**

**There are two kinds of friendship namely friendship wit faith and friendship with attachment.**

**a) Friendship with faith.**

### Text 152

*drupadera sakhya ta kṛṣṇe viśvāsa rūpe ati*  
*kṛṣṇa bhakta na haya naṣṭa ei kathā śruti*

**Перевод:**

**Drupadi’s friendship with Kṛṣṇa was based on her firm faith in Kṛṣṇa. She was fully convinced that the devotees of Kṛṣṇa never perishes.**

### Text 153

*iti cinti draupadī prāṇa karila dhāraṇa*  
*duryodhanera sahilena?? aśeṣa ganjana??*

**Перевод:**

**With such strong faith, Drupadi continued her life to tolerate it the unlimited torture of Duryodhana.**

pg 52

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.189) it is stated

*pratijñā tava govinda na me bhaktah praṇaśyati iti saṅsmṛtya prāṇān saṁdhāryāmyaham*

**Перевод:**

**Draupadi said to Kṛṣṇa “My dear Govinda, it is your promise that your devotees never perish. Remembering this again and again I am continuing my life.**

b) Friendship with attachment.

### Text 154

*paricāraka rūpe nikaṭe kare sthiti*  
*kṛṣṇa mora bhandhu habe hena yāra mati*

**Перевод:**

**Those, who remain with the Kṛṣṇa as friends or well wishers and serve him according to their own will are situated in friendship with attachment.**

### Text 155

*nikaṭe śayana kare śaṅkā nāhi cite*  
*vayavahara?? karile cāya? bandhura pīḍitā*

**Перевод:**

**They sleep with the Lord and mix with Him fearlessly. They only want to act for the pleasure of their friend.**

### Text 156

*mitra sakhya haya ithi siddha dui pathe*  
*ataeva likhila vidhi rāga māthe*

**Перевод:**

**These are the two types of friendship according to regulative principles and attachment.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.192) it is stated

*paricaryā parāh kecit prāsādeṣu ca śerate manuṣyamiva taṁ draṣṭuṁ vyāvahartuñca*

**Перевод:**

**Some devotees, who are engaged in the service of the Lord consider Him like an ordinary human being and in order to deal with Him as friends, sleep with the Lord in the temple.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1,2,193) it is stated

*rāgānugāṇgatāsya syādvidhimārganapekṣaṇāt mārga-dvayena caitena sādhyā sakhyaratirmatā*

**Перевод:**

**Since this example does not depend on regulative principles this friendship is called the limb of** ***rāgānuga bhakti*** **pure platform of friendship can be achieved through both the regulative path and the path of attachment.**

49\) Surrendering everything to the Lord.

### Text 157

*ātma-nivedanam dvividham dehi*  
*samarpaṇam deha samarpaṇam ca*

**Перевод:**

**There are two types of surrendering to the Lord namely surrendering one’s self and surrendering one’s body to the Lord.**

**a) Surrendering one’s self to the Lord.**

### Text 158

*vākya mana deha dite ye karma karibe*  
*saba kṛṣṇa pada padma arpaṇa karibe*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever activities a person performs with his body mind and speech should be offered at the lotus feet of the Kṛṣṇa.**

**b) Surrendering one’s body to the Lord.**

### Text 159

*nija deha samarpaṇa karaye ye jāna*  
*tāra deha rakṣa kare prabhu sanātana*

**Перевод:**

**The eternal Lord protects the body of one, who surrenders his body at the lotus feet of the Lord.**

### Text 160-161

*prāṇa āra sukha dukha dāra dhana gṛha*  
*gurupadāśraya kale samarpila deha kṛṣṇera haila deha bhāra lāge tāre rakṣa karena kṛṣṇa sarvatra bhaktere*

**Перевод:**

**At the time of taking initiation, a devotee surrenders his life, wife, wealth, house, happiness and distress at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. Then the body of the devotee becomes the property of Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa protects his devotees in all respects.**

### Text 162

*nija dehera rakṣa hetu bhaktera cintā hai*  
*apāna boli Kṛṣṇa rākhena sarva ṭhāñi*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee does not worry at all for the protection of his body for he knows that Kṛṣṇa always protect His devotees.**

### Text 163

*tāra sākṣi prahlāda ambarīṣa vibhīṣaṇa*  
*sarvatra karila rakṣa śāstre vivaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**There are many examples of these in the scriptures where in the Lord fully protected His devotees like Prahlāda, Ambarīṣa, and Vibhīṣaṇa.**

### Text 164-165

*vikraya koriyā pasura tattva nāhi laya*  
*ye laya ki niyā paśu tāhāra bhāra haya*  
*taiche deha kṛṣṇārpaṇa kare yevā jāna*  
*tāre rakṣa kare kṛṣṇa boliyā apāna*

**Перевод:**

**A person, who sells his pet animal no longer worries for its maintenance. It becomes the responsibility of the buyer. Similarly, one who surrenders his body to Kṛṣṇa is fully protected by Him.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.197) it is stated

*cintāṁ kuryānna rakṣāyair vikrītasya yathāpaśoh tathārpayan harau dehaṁ viramedasya rakṣanāt*

**Перевод:**

**Just as person does not think about maintenance of his sold animals similarly one should surrender his body to Śrī Hari and remain aloof from protecting it.**

50\) Offering one’s most favorable items to the Lord.

### Text 166

*ātma priya miṣṭa yevā sakala haite*  
*sei dravya tyāga kare śrī kṛṣṇera prīte*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee gives up his most favorable foodstuff or item for the pleasure of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 167

*kṛṣṇārpaṇa karī kare bhakta gaṇe dāna*  
*kṛṣṇa prīte sei dravya aganita?? pāna*

**Перевод:**

**Such a devotee offers his favorable item to Kṛṣṇa and then distributes it among the devotees of the Lord. As a result of pleasing Kṛṣṇa he receives it back unlimited times.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.199) it is stated

*yad yad iṣṭatamaṁ loke yac cāti-priyam ātmanaḥ tat tan nivedayen mahyaṁ tad ānantyāya kalpate*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever is most desired by one within this material world, and whatever is most dear to oneself—one should offer that very thing to Me. Such an offering qualifies one for eternal life.**

It is also described in Bhagavad - Gītā (9.27)

*yat karoṣi yad aśnāsi yaj juhoṣi dadāsi yat yat tapasyasi kaunteya tat kuruṣva mad-arpaṇam*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever you do, whatever you eat, whatever you offer or give away, and whatever austerities you perform—do that, O son of Kuntī, as an offering to Me.**

51\) Fully endeavoring to please Kṛṣṇa

### Text 168

*loukika vaidic kriyā śāstra mata yata*  
*bhakti anukūle kriyā karibe vidhi mata*

**Перевод:**

**One should perform worldly and vedic activities prescribed in the scriptures and which are favorable for devotional service.**

**52 ) Fully surrendering to Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 169

*śaraṇāpatti laibe śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇe*  
*kṛṣṇa daśa āmi kāya vākya māne*

**Перевод:**

**One should take shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa with one’s body, mind and speech while considering himself as an eternal servant of Him.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.201) it is stated

*tavāsmīti badan vācā tathaiva manasā vidan tatsthānamāśritastanvā modate śaraṇāgatah*

**Перевод:**

**“O my Lord, I belong to You” – a surrendered person speaks in this way, feels in this way in his heart, takes shelter of the Lord’s abode with his body and becomes happy.**

53\) Serving Tulasī.

### Text 170

*tulasī darśana mātre aśeṣa pāpa nāśe*  
*deha pavitra haya tulasīra paraśe*

**Перевод:**

**Just by seeing Tulasī one’s unlimited sinful reactions are destroyed. By the touch of Tulasī one’s body becomes purified.**

### Text 171

*tulasī vandana kaile rāgera vināśa*  
*sincita?? tulasī dekhi yama pāya trāsa*

**Перевод:**

**By offering prayers to Tulasī one’s diseases are cured. Even Yamaraja is afraid of one, who bathes Tulasī.**

### Text 172

*tulasīra vṛkṣa āni ye kare ropaṇa*  
*viṣṇu pada gati haya karile secana*

**Перевод:**

**One, who grows Tulasī plant and bathes her attains the lotus feet of Lord Viṣṇu.**

### Text 173

*tulasīra pātre pūjā kare janārdana*  
*tāhāra vimukti haya na jaya kathana*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone, who worships Lord Janārdana with tulasī leaves is certainly liberated. There is no doubt about it.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.203) it is stated

*yā dṛṣṭā nikhilāgha-saṅgha-śamanī spṛṣṭā vapur pāranī rogāṇāmabhivanditā nirasanī sitāntakatrāsini pratyāsatti-vidhāyainī bhagavatah kṛṣṇasya saṅropitā nyastā taccaraṇe vimukti-phaladā tasyai tulasyai namah*

(Skanda Purana)

**Перевод:**

**I offer my humble obeisances to Tulasī by seeing whom all one’s body is purified, by praying whom one frightens Yamarāja, by planting whom one develops attachment for Kṛṣṇa and by offering her leaves at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa one attains Love of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 174

*navadhā bhakti krame sevā tulasīra karibe*  
*tulasīra nāma kathā śravaṇe śunibe*

**Перевод:**

**One should serve tulasī by hearing, chanting etc. in the same way one engaging in nine types of devotional service.**

### Text 175

*tulasī tulasī nāma karibe kīrtana*  
*tulasī devīra mūrti karibe smaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One should chant the names of tulasī and remember the form of tulasī with his mind.**

### Text 176

*vṛkṣa sevā pūjā karibe śāstra mate*  
*pradakṣiṇa vandana stuti bhakti bhava rite*

**Перевод:**

**One should worship tulasī plant, circumambulate her, offer prayers to her with devotion. This is the injunction of scriptures.**

**54) Studying the scriptures.**

### Text 177

*śāstra śabde ithi kahi bhakti grantha prati*  
*vaiṣṇava śāstre sadā karibe pirīti*

**Перевод:**

**The word śāstra refers to the devotional scriptures. One should always keep faith and devotion in the vaiṣṇava literature.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.206) it is stated

*śāstra mātra samākyātaṁ yad bhakti pratipādakam*

**Перевод:**

**The scriptures, which establish pure devotional service to the Lord is called Śāstras.**

### Text 178

*vaiṣṇava śāstra pade śune karāya śravaṇa*  
*kṛṣṇa prasanna take?? saphala jīvana*

**Перевод:**

**One, who studies, hears or explains the vaiṣṇava literature to others makes his life successful.**

pg 60

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.207) it is stated

*vaiṣṇavāni tu śāstrāṇī ye śravaṇti paṭhanti ca dhanyāte māhavā loke tesaṁ kṛṣṇah prasīdanti*

**Перевод:**

**A person who hears, study and preaches the vaiṣṇava literature is glories in this world. Lord Kṛṣṇa is very pleased with him.**

Śrīmad - bhāgavatam is crest jewel among all scriptures, this is explained in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.210)

*sarva-vedānta-sāraṁ yad brahmātmaikatva-lakṣaṇam vastv advitīyaṁ tan-niṣṭhaṁ kaivalyaika-prayojanam*

**Перевод:**

**This Bhāgavatam is the essence of all Vedānta philosophy because its subject matter is the Absolute Truth, which, while nondifferent from the spirit soul, is the ultimate reality, one without a second. The goal of this literature is exclusive devotional service unto that Supreme Truth.**

55\) Residing at Mathurā.

### Text 179

*trailokyera varti tīrtha vahe mathurā maṇḍale*  
*paramānanda kṣema?? haya mathurā vāsa kaile*

**Перевод:**

**All the holy tīrthas of the three worlds are present in the area of Braja Maṇḍala. One, who resides here becomes ecstatic.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.212)

*trailokyavarti tīrthānāṁ sevanāddurlabhā hi yā parānandamayī siddhir-mathurā- sparśamātratah-iti*

**Перевод:**

**The ecstatic love of Lord which is the ultimate goal of life and which is not attained by visiting all the holy places of the three worlds simply achieved by the touch of land of Mathurā.**

56\) Serving the Vaiṣṇava.

### Text 180

*sarva ārādhana parā kṛṣṇa ārādhana*  
*tāhā haite vāda haya bhaktera sevana*

**Перевод:**

**Among all of the worships worship of Kṛṣṇa is the highest, and worshipping the devotees is even higher than the worship of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 181

*kṛṣṇa pūje āra na pūje bhakta gaṇa*  
*tadīya na pūje sei mūḍha acetana*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who worship Kṛṣṇa and does not worship His devotees is simply a fool and senseless.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.214) it is stated

*ārādhanānāṁ sarveṣāṁ viṣṇor ārādhanaṁ param tasmāt parataraṁ devi tadīyānāṁ samarcanam*

(Padma Purana)

**Перевод:**

**“\[Lord Śiva told the goddess Durgā:\] ‘My dear Devī, although the Vedas recommend worship of demigods, the worship of Lord Viṣṇu is topmost. However, above the worship of Lord Viṣṇu is the rendering of service to Vaiṣṇavas, who are related to Lord Viṣṇu.”**

### Text 182

*arcayitvā tu govindaṁ tadīya na arcayet tu yah*  
*na sa bhāgavata jñeyo kevalam dāmbhika smitah*

**Перевод:**

**One, who worships govinda, but does not worship his devotees is simply a proud person and certainly not a devotee.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.217) it is stated

*yeṣāṁ saṁsmaraṇāt puṁsāṁ sadyaḥ śuddhyanti vai gṛhāḥ kiṁ punar darśana-sparśa- pāda-śaucāsanādibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Simply by our remembering you, our houses become instantly sanctified. And what to speak of seeing you, touching you, washing your holy feet and offering you a seat in our home?**

57\) Serving the Lord with ones capacity.

### Text 183-184

*ye mata vibhāva nija sāmarthya anusāre*  
*kṛṣṇa yatra mahotsava kṛṣṇa niyā kare kṛṣṇa loke mahotsava tāra nityā haya pada miya?? uttarā khaṇḍe ei kathā kāya*

**Перевод:**

**It is described in the Padma Purana Uttarā Khaṇḍa that a devotee who celebrated various festivals of Kṛṣṇa according to his own capacity, participates in the festival of Kṛṣṇa in His abode.**

**58) Following the vow of Kartika Vrata**

### Text 185

*bhakta vatsala kṛṣṇa abhīṣṭa phala dena*  
*bhakta vatsala taiche?? kārtike vrata hañā*

**Перевод:**

**Just as Lord Kṛṣṇa is affectionate towards His devotees and fulfill all their desires similarly Kartika Vrata (Dāmodara Vrata) is affectionate towards the devotees and fulfill all their desires.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.221) it is stated

*yathā dāmodaro bhaktavatsalo vidito janaiḥ tasyāyaṁ tādṛśo māsah svalpamapyukukārakah*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Dāmodara is known an affectionate towards His devotees and due to this quality he awards multi flied results to His devotees insignificant services rendered by the devotees.**

59\) Celebrating the appearance day of the Lord.

### Text 186

*bhādre kṛṣṇāṣṭamī tithi rohiṇī sahite*  
*vrata vidhi upavāsa karibe yatnete*

**Перевод:**

**One should properly celebrate the appearance day of Lord Kṛṣṇa which occurs in the month of Bhādra (August -September) by fasting and holding festival.**

**60) Serving the deity of the Lord with love and devotion**

### Text 187

*kṛṣṇa nāma kṛṣṇa sevā parāyaṇā jāna*  
*tāre kṛṣṇa mukti ca ādi dena bhakti prema*

**Перевод:**

**One, who is attached to chanting the holy names of Kṛṣṇa and serving His feet is rewarded loving devotional service by Kṛṣṇa rather than mere liberation.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.225) it is stated

*mama nāma-sadāgrāhī mama sevāpriyah sadā bhaktistamai pradātavyā na tu muktiḥ kadācana*

**Перевод:**

**A person who is always attached to my service and constantly chant my holy names, I give him devotional service, not liberation which is devoid of devotional service.**

61\) Relishing Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam.

### Text 188

*eita jagate yata rasika bhakta gaṇa*  
*bhāgavata rasāmṛta sadā kāra pāna*

**Перевод:**

**O all the devotees, If you really wish to relish the nectar, then please always relish the nectar of Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam.**

### Text 189

*veda kalpa tarura phala śrīla bhāgavata*  
*śukadevā mukha haite se phala galita*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam is the ripen fruit of vedic desire tree. This fruit has come down through the mouth of Śrīla Śukadevā Gosvāmī.**

### Text 190

*amṛta dāva saṁyuktā rasamaya phala*  
*tāhā pane haya jāni kṛṣṇa prema bala*

**Перевод:**

**This nectarian fruit one should full of juice and by drinking it one receives the strength of love of Kṛṣṇa**

### Text 191

*ājanma prabhṛti pāna kāra punaḥ punaḥ*  
*tvagasthi?? rahita phala amṛta drava yena*

**Перевод:**

**One should repeatedly drink it for whole lifetime. This fruit is just like nectar and without any skin and seed.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.226) it is stated

*nigama-kalpa-taror galitaṁ phalaṁ śuka-mukhād amṛta-drava-saṁyutam pibata bhāgavataṁ rasam ālayaṁ muhur aho rasikā bhuvi bhāvukāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O expert and thoughtful men, relish Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, the mature fruit of the desire tree of Vedic literature. It emanated from the lips of Śrī Śukadeva Gosvāmī. Therefore this fruit has become even more tasteful, although its nectarian juice was already relishable for all, including liberated souls.**

62\) Associating with pure devotees.

### Text 192-193

*kṛṣṇāsakta citta yevā prema bhakta gaṇa*  
*tāṅra saṅge saṅga eka lava mātra hañā*  
*tāṅra kāche svarga ādi kimbā mukti gati*  
*tulanā na karī tāra ei ta yukti*

**Перевод:**

**Associating with devotees whose minds are attached to Kṛṣṇa and who are full of ecstatic love even for a moment can not be compared with heavenly pleasure or liberation. This is a fact.**

### Text 194

*nimisera tribhāga kāla tāre lava kaṇa*  
*tulyama lave nāpi ityādi vacana*

**Перевод:**

**Word** ***lava*** **refers to the fraction of a second. Various statements regarding this are found in the scriptures.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.228) it is stated

*tulayāma lavenāpi na svargaṁ nāpunar-bhavam bhagavat-saṅgi-saṅgasya martyānāṁ kim utāśiṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The value of a moment’s association with the devotee of the Lord cannot even be compared to the attainment of heavenly planets or liberation from matter, and what to speak of worldly benedictions in the form of material prosperity, which are for those who are meant for death.**

63\) Congregational chanting of the holy names of Hari.

### Text 195

*sarvatra abhaya vañca lāgi yāra mana*  
*se karuka sarvadā hari nāma saṅkīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**Let anyone who desires constant fearlessness in his mind loudly chant the holy names of Hari**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.230-232) it is stated

*etan nirvidyamānānām icchatām akuto-bhayam yogināṁ nṛpa nirṇītaṁ harer nāmānukīrtanam*

**Перевод:**

**O King, constant chanting of the holy name of the Lord after the ways of the great authorities is the doubtless and fearless way of success for all, including those who are free from all material desires, those who are desirous of all material enjoyment, and also those who are self-satisfied by dint of transcendental knowledge.**

*gītvā ca mama nāmāni vicarena mama sannidhau iti brambīmi te satyam krīto ‘ham tasya cārjuna*

**Перевод:**

**O Arjuna I am telling you the truth that anyone who chants my holy names and lives with me certainly buys me. There is no doubt about it.**

*yena janma-śataiḥ pūrvaṁ vāsudevaḥ samārcitaḥ tan-mukhe hari-nāmāni sadā tiṣṭhanti bhārata*

**Перевод:**

**“O descendant of Bhārata, the holy names of Lord Viṣṇu are always vibrating in the mouth of one who has previously worshipped Vāsudeva perfectly for hundreds of lifetimes.”**

64\) Residing in Mathurā

### Text 196

*mathurā maṇḍale kare ekadina vasati*  
*avaśya tāhāra haya govinde bhakati*

**Перевод:**

**Just by residing at Mathurā even for a day one certainly develops devotion to the lotus feet of Govinda.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.237) it is stated

*aho madhupuri dhanyā vaikuṇṭhācca garīyasī dinamekaṁ nivāsena harau bhaktiḥ prajāyate iti*

**Перевод:**

**Alas! Mathura, which is superior than vaikuṇṭha, is certainly glories for if one resides there even for a day he attains devotion to Hari.**

### Text 197

*cātuḥ ṣaṣṭi bhakti aṅga śāstra nirūpaṇa*  
*mahimādhikya śraddhā dvārā dekhi ye pañcama*

**Перевод:**

**The sixty-four limbs of devotion service are thus described in the scriptures. Among them the last five limbs are more glories and gateway to faith.**

### Text 198-199

*te karaṇe punarukti adhikya varaṇa*  
*śrī mūrti daraśana śrī bhāgavata mahimā*  
*kṛṣṇa bhakta prastuti nāma mahātmya*  
*mathurā maṇḍale vāsa pañca punar uktā*

**Перевод:**

**That is why the five most important limbs such as worshipping the deity of the Lord, hearing Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam, associating with the devotees, chanting the holy names and residing at Mathurā are repeated to stress this point.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.93) it is stated

*aṅgānām pañcakasyāsya pūrvaṁ vilikhitasya ca nikhila śtreṣṭhyavodhāya punarapyatra kīrtanam*

**Перевод:**

**Although these five concluding limbs of devotional service are included among the other limbs of devotional service yet in order to show their superiority the author has once again mentioned them in the concluding section.**

### Text 200

*cātuḥ ṣaṣṭi bhakti aṅga sādhana prakaraṇa*  
*śrī yutera anusāre karila varṇana*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus explained the sixty-four limbs of devotional service according to the description of Śrīla Rupa Gosvāmī Pranhu.**

### Text 201

*kona aṅgera kona sthale phala śruti dekhi*  
*sādhāraṇa janera pravṛtti hetu likhi*

**Перевод:**

**We find that the results of some limbs are describes here and there. Actually they are meant to develop interest for the ordinary human beings.**

### Text 202

*cātur-varga phala śuni sādhāraṇa jāna*  
*bhakti aṅga sādhane pravṛtta asi hañā*

**Перевод:**

**When the ordinary human being hear that the results of executing limbs of devotional service are religiously, economic development, sense gratification and liberation. They become attracted to the execution of devotional service.**

### Text 203

*kṛṣṇa kathā līlā guṇa śravaṇa smaraṇa*  
*rati upajāyate asi śrī kṛṣṇa sādhana*

**Перевод:**

**By hearing, chanting and remembering the wonderful topics and pastimes of kṛṣṇa one develops attachment for them.**

### Text 204

*sei saba phala tāra haya jāni dūre*  
*kṛṣṇa karme rati asi haya jāni pare*

**Перевод:**

**The temporary irrelevant results of devotional service are not its principle results.**

### Text 205

*mukhya phala kṛṣṇe rati jāniha kāraṇa*  
*phala śruti bahir mukhera pravṛtti lakṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Know it for certain that the principle fruit of devotional service is to develop attachment for Kṛṣṇa. However, the description of the temporary fruits is simply meant to attract people.**

### Text 206

*tat tat guṇa śravaṇe āge śraddhā asi haya*  
*śraddhā haile rati tāhe upajaya*

**Перевод:**

**By hearing, the glories of the limbs of devotional service one develops faith in them and from faith gradually one develops attachment for the Lord.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.245) it is stated

*keṣāñcit kvacidaṅgānāṁ yat kṣudraṁ ṣruyate phalaṁ vahirmukha puavṛttyaitat kintu mukhyaṁphalaṁ ratih*

**Перевод:**

**The insignificant results that sometimes we hear about some of the limbs of devotional service are not their only result. These results have been described to bring the materialistic people to the path of devotional service. In fact attachment to kṛṣṇa is their principle result**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.3.12) it is stated

*satāṁ prasaṅgān mama vīrya-saṁvido bhavanti hṛt-karṇa-rasāyanāḥ kathāḥ taj-joṣaṇād āśv apavarga-vartmani śraddhā ratir bhaktir anukramiṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**In the association of pure devotees, discussion of the pastimes and activities of the Supreme Personality of Godhead is very pleasing and satisfying to the ear and the heart. By cultivating such knowledge one gradually becomes advanced on the path of liberation, and thereafter he is freed, and his attraction becomes fixed. Then real devotion and devotional service begin.**

### Text 207

*jñāna āra vairāgya bhakti aṅga nāhi hañā*  
*prathame svalpa mātra upayukta keha kaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Although knowledge and renunciation are not the limbs of devotional service. Still some people say that they help a practitioner a little in the beginning.**

### Text 208

*jñāna ithi kahe aikya brāhmaṇa viṣayā jñāna*  
*brāhmaṇa jñāna upayukta vairāgya ākhyāna*

**Перевод:**

**Cultivation of knowledge in this regard refers to the impersonal knowledge of oneness and renunciation is to help achieve that impersonal knowledge.**

### Text 209

*bhakti praveśe e duira akiñcit kāraṇa*  
*brāhmaṇa jñāna upayukta vairāgya ākhyāna*

**Перевод:**

**These two items are insignificant as far as entering into the kingdom of devotional service is concerned in fact by cultivating this items one becomes distracted from the path of devotional service.**

### Text 210

*tāhāra kāraṇa śuno sāvadhāne*  
*ei dui kāṭhinya hetu likhila pramāṇe*

**Перевод:**

**Please hear carefully the reason for this. The scriptures have described them has being very hard.**

### Text 211

*tarka vicārādi dukha kariyā sahana*  
*kāṭhinya hetu jñāna vairāgya, yoga kaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**The miseries of mental speculation and arguments render ones heart hard. That is why cultivation of knowledge and renunciation is practiced by the hardhearted people.**

### Text 212

*sukumārā svabhāva bhakti tat hetu kaiche habe*  
*ataeva jñāna vairāgya aṅga na jānibe*

**Перевод:**

**By nature the devotional service is very soft, therefore it cannot grow in the hard heart, so do not count knowledge and renunciation as limbs of devotional service.**

### Text 213

*śrī kṛṣṇa madhura līlā bhāvanādi mayā ataeva bhakti yoga śubha yāra haya*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore a person whose heart is completely pure can render devotional service such as hearing chanting and remembering the sweet pastimes of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.248-249) it is stated

*jñana vairāgyayor bhakti praveśāyopayogitā iṣat prathamameveti nāṅga tvamucitaṁyayoh yadubhe citta kaṭhinya hetu prāyah sataṁ mate sukumāra svabhaveyaṁ bhaktistadheturīritā*

**Перевод:**

**It is only in the beginning of entering into the path of Bhakti that there is slight, i.e. very little utility of jñāna and vairāgya. Therefore it is not correct to say that jñāna and vairāgya are aṅgas of pure Bhakti. Those Sadhus who are well-conversant with the true nature of Bhakti say that both these two jñāna and vairāgya are often the cause of hardening the heart, whereas the bhakti is tender by nature and its own cause – nothing else can be the cause of bhakti.**

### Text 214

*ataeva dekha kahena prabhu bhagavāna*  
*mora bhakta yoga uktā mat niviṣṭa prāṇa*

**Перевод:**

**That is why Lord Kṛṣṇa has said that my devotees are fully in connection with and devoted to me.**

### Text 215

*prāya tāra jñāna vairāgya dūre kare*  
*tāra śreyaḥ haya seha haye adhikāre*

**Перевод:**

**I always protect such devotees from the clutches of impersonal knowledge and dry renunciation so that he becomes eligible to attain devotional service.**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.250)

*tasmān mad-bhakti-yuktasya yogino vai mad-ātmanaḥ na jñānaṁ na ca vairāgyaṁ prāyaḥ śreyo bhaved iha*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, for a devotee engaged in My loving service, with mind fixed on Me, the cultivation of knowledge and renunciation is generally not the means of achieving the highest perfection within this world.**

### Text 216

*jñāna vairāgya ādi yevā sādhanera phala*  
*cātur-varga phalādi se jāniha sakala*

**Перевод:**

**The result of cultivation of knowledge practice of renunciation is certainly to attain four objects of life.**

### Text 217

*kṛṣṇa bhakti haile se āpane asi haye*  
*kona bhakta svargādi yadi vā vañcaye*

**Перевод:**

**If one engages in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa all these irrelevant fruits automatically come without any extra endeavor.**

### Text 218

*karma tapa jñāna vairāgya eva haya phala*  
*bhakti bale anāyāse pāya se sakala*

**Перевод:**

**By the strength of devotional service a person can easily achieve the fruits of austerity, knowledge, renunciation and fruitive activities.**

### Text 219

*sakāma bhaktira bale sālokyādi gate*  
*sevā nista?? rūpe haya tāhā sabāra mukti*

**Перевод:**

**Those who engage in devotional service with material desires attain liberation from this world and go back to the same planet with the Lord as a result of his firm service.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.252) it is said

*yat karmabhir yat tapasā jñāna-vairāgyataś ca yat yogena dāna-dharmeṇa śreyobhir itarair api sarvaṁ mad-bhakti-yogena mad-bhakto labhate ’ñjasā svargāpavargaṁ mad-dhāma kathañcid yadi vāñchati*

**Перевод:**

**Everything that can be achieved by fruitive activities, penance, knowledge, detachment, mystic yoga, charity, religious duties and all other means of perfecting life is easily achieved by My devotee through loving service unto Me. If somehow or other My devotee desires promotion to heaven, liberation, or residence in My abode, he easily achieves such benedictions.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.254) it is stated

*rucim udvahatastatra janasya bhajane hareh viṣayeṣu gariṣṭo’pi rāgah ptāyo viliyate.*

**Перевод:**

**Should a person attain Ruci or a taste in the service of Lord Hari, Then all his earthly attachments, however hard, shall soon melt away.**

**The symptoms of renunciation.**

The renunciation are two kinds namely *yuktā vairāgya* and *phalgu vairāgya*

Yuktā vairāgya.

### Text 220-222

*nija sukha viṣayādi katiya tejana??*  
*indriya viṣayā nija karaye damana kāma krodha lobha moha mada mātsarya ādi kṛṣṇa sambandhe kare karma anya viṣayā tyaji sevopayukta?? kare karma yei saba jāna yukta vairāgya dharma ta sabāra kaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Those who gives up all endeavors for material enjoyments, controls their senses, gives up lust, anger, greed, illusion, pride and envy and engage in activities only in relation to Kṛṣṇa as well as those who directly engage in the service of Kṛṣṇa are known to be situated in the platform of*****Yukta Vairāgya*****.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.255) it is stated

*anāsaktasya viṣayān yathārham upayuñjataḥ nirbandhaḥ kṛṣṇa-sambandhe yuktaṁ vairāgyam ucyate*

**Перевод:**

**“When one is not attached to anything, but at the same time accepts everything in relation to Kṛṣṇa, one is rightly situated above possessiveness. This is called Yuktā vairāgya.”**

Phalgu Vairāgya.

### Text 223-227

*sevopayukta?? dravye prapañca jñāna karī*  
*sevā dharma tyāga kare yoge bhaje hari sevā dravya saṅgrahe viṣayā balī jñāna sarva viṣayā caḍi bhaje bhagavāna sarva karma tyāga kare viṣaye virāga kaṇa sukha nāhi vañca sarva bhoga tyāga viṣayā jñāna karī sea tyāga kare mumuksu sakala jñāna vairāgya ācare phalgu vairāgya nāma tāhā kare kaṇa bhakti aṅgātve nirūpaṇa tāra nāhi hañā*

**Перевод:**

**Those who consider the ingredients of the Lord’s service to be material and thus give up the propensity of serving the Lord, consider the accumulation of material object, give up all kinds of activities and remain aloof from material enjoyments and cultivate knowledge and practice renunciation in order to get liberation are known to be situated in the platform of phalgu vairāgya. As such it is not considered a limb of devotional service.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.256) it is stated

*prāpañcikatayā buddhyā hari-sambandhi-vastunaḥ mumukṣubhiḥ parityāgo vairāgyaṁ phalgu kathyate*

**Перевод:**

**“One who rejects everything without knowledge of its relationship to Kṛṣṇa is not complete in his renunciation, such renunciation is called phalgu vairāgya.”**

### Text 228

*kṛṣṇa mahāprasādādi na kare grahaṇa*  
*bhoga boliyā nirmalya?? karaye tejana??*

**Перевод:**

**Such people do not accept Mahāprasād or remnants of the Lord and flower garlands that have been offered to the Lord considering them to be the part of material enjoyment.**

### Text 229

*yei hetu phalgu vairāgya adhyātmika jñāna*  
*yei jñāne kare brāhmaṇu bhāva sandhana bhakti aṅgatve anupa?? yogi ei saba hañā punaḥ punaḥ karilena aṅgatve varaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**That is why false renunciation and impersonal knowledge by which one desires to merge into Brahman are not counted among the limbs of devotional service. In fact they have repeatedly been rejected.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.257) it is stated

*proklena lakṣaṇe naiva bhakteradhi kṛtasya ca aṅgatve su nirasthe ’pi nityādyakhila karmaṇaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**While describing the symptoms of devotional service the performance of ones occupational duties, cultivation of knowledge and false renunciation have not been included among the limbs of the devotional service.**

### Text 230

*dhana vyāpāre kimbā śiṣyādi dvārāte*  
*śrī mūrti adhika sevā karaye tāhāte*  
*uttama bhaktira aṅga seha nāhi hañā*  
*jñāna karmādi anāvṛttādi?? saithilya?? hañā*

**Перевод:**

**If one worships the deity form of the Lord for the sake of money or through hired priest than such worship can not be called the limb of devotional service. It must be freed from impersonal knowledge and fruitive activities.**

### Text 231

*paricaryā ādi rūpa tulasyādi arpaṇa*  
*dhana vyāpārādye uttama hāni hana*  
*ataeva vivekādi bhakti aṅga nāhi haya*  
*sa vai manaḥ kṛṣṇa ityādi yāhe naya*

**Перевод:**

**By engaging a hired priest one misses the oppurntunity of personally offering tulasī leaves to Kṛṣṇa hence it has been prohibited. Therefore they are not limbs of devotional service because one cannot fix his mind on Kṛṣṇa by such activities.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.259) it is stated

*dhana śiṣyādibhir dvārairyā bhakti rupapadyate viduratvāduttamatā hānyā tasyāś ca nāṅgatā*

**Перевод:**

**Since the devotional service that is executed by wealth or hired priet is situated far away from pure devotional service, it cannot be counted as the limb of pure devotional service.**

### Text 232

*sei bhakti haya jāni sudṛḍha sādhane*  
*eka aṅga sādhaye keho keho bahu krame*

**Перевод:**

**Pure devotional service is attained through practice with firm determination. Some practice one limb while others practice more than one limb.**

### Text 233-234

*sva vāsanānusāre niṣṭhā citte yaje*  
*kṛṣṇa niṣṭhā haiyā mātra kona aṅge bhaje eka aṅga sādhi keho sukṛtārtha hañā keha bahu aṅga sādhi hayata pāvana*

**Перевод:**

**According to his own desire and with firm faith in Kṛṣṇa one practices a limb of devotional service and attain perfection whereas others practice many limbs and become purified.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.264) it is stated

*sā bhaktireka mukhyāṅgāritānaikāṅgikāthavā sva-vāsanānusāreṇa niṣṭhātah siddhi-kṛdbhavet*

**Перевод:**

**Whether one practices one limb or many limbs of devotional service, it will award perfection according to the desire of the practician.**

### Text 235-239

*parīkṣita kṛtārtha nāma līlādi śravaṇa*  
*śukadevā nityā mukta śrī kṛṣṇa kīrtana. smaraṇa kṛtārtha haila prahlāda daitya pati bhajane pāila lakṣmī śrī govinda pati pūjane kṛtārtha haila pṛthu raga naranātha vandanete pāila akrūra jagannātha dāsya bhāve hanumāna pāila rāmacandra sakhya arjuna vāsa kaila kṛṣṇa candra ātma ātmīya deha karī samarpaṇa balirāja kṛtārtha haila khyāta tribhuvana*

**Перевод:**

**Maharāja Parīkṣit attained perfection by hearing the holy names and pastimes of the Lord, Śukadevā Gosvāmī became eternally liberated soul by chanting the glories of the Lord, Prahlāda Maharaja attained perfection by remembering the Lord, by serving His lotus feet Laksmi received Govinda as Her husband, King Pṛthu attained perfection by worshiping the Lord, by offering prayers Akrūra attained the lotus feet of the Lord of universe, Hanuman attained the lotus feet of Rāmacandra by serving, Kṛṣṇacandra became controlled by the friendship of Arjuna and by surrendering everything including his self and relatives King Balī attained perfection and became renowed into three worlds.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.265) it is stated

*śrī viṣṇoh śravaṇe parīkṣidabhavad vayāsakih kīrtane prahlādah smaraṇe tadaṅghri bhajane lakṣmīh pṛthuh pūjane akrūrastvabhivandane kapipatir dāsye ‘tha sakhye ‘rjunah sarvasyātma-nivedane balirabhūt kṛṣṇāptir eṣāṁ parā*

**Перевод:**

**Parīkṣit by hearing the topics of Hari from Śrīmad - bhāgavatam , Śukadevā by reciting them, Prahlāda by remembering Hari, Lakṣmī by serving the lotus feet of Hari, Pṛthu by worshiping Him, Akrūra by offering prayers to Him, Hanumān by serving Him, Arjuna by maintaining friendship with Him and Balī by surundering everithing to the Lord attained perfection. Each one of the attained the lotus feet of the Lord simply by practicing one principle limb.**

### Text 240

*ei ta kahila mātra eka aṅga lakṣaṇa*  
*eka aṅga mukhya pūrṇa anya aṅga ye hañā*

**Перевод:**

**This is the description of the examples of practicing one pricple limb of devotional service and attainment of perfection.**

### Text 241

*aneka aṅga śubha bhagavata navame*  
*ambarīṣa karilena prabhura caraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**Execution of many limbs of devotional service is described in the nine canto of Śrīmad - bhāgavatam wherein Maharaj Ambarisa exibited his expertise in this regard.**

### Text 242-250

*sarvendriya kṛṣṇa karma sadā ācaraṇa*  
*bahu aṅga bhakti haya tāhāte darśana kṛṣṇa pada yuge mati sadā anugata vākye kṛṣṇa līlā guṇa varṇe abirata?? haste paricaryā śrī mandirādi saṁskāra karṇe kṛṣṇa kathā vinu nāhi śune āra kṛṣṇa mūrti kṛṣṇa laya śrī guru darśana nirabadhi?? yāra niṣṭhā haya dunayana?? kṛṣṇa bhakta gaṇa saha dehe āliṅgana aṣṭāṅga praṇipāta dehera kāraṇa kṛṣṇe datta gandha puṣpa nāsāya grahaṇa rasa naya tulasyādi nirmalya?? bhakṣaṇa caraṇera kārya kṣetre tīrtha sthāne gati mastakera kārya sadā pada padme nāti sarva kamya kāmanā kariyā parityāga kṛṣṇa dāsye kāmanā satata anurāga bahu aṅga lakṣaṇa ei ambarīṣera vacana navama skandha bhagavāne vyāsera likhana*

**Перевод:**

**Ambarīṣa Maharaja engaged all his senses in the service of Kṛṣṇa. He fixed his mind comstantly at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, used his speech in glorifying the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa, engage his hands in cleaning the temple of the Lord, used his ears in cleansing the temple of the Lord, used his ears in gearing the topics of Kṛṣṇa, engaged his eyes constantly in seeing the deity form, temple of the Lord and sporitual master, engaging his body in embracing the devotees of the Lord as well as offering obeisances to the Lord, used his nose in smelling the flowers that has already been offered to the Lord, engaged his tongue in honouring offered tulasī feaves, engaged his legs in visiting the holy places, used his head in offering obeisances at the lotus feet of the Lord, gave up all material desires and constantly aspired for the service of the Lord. This is the example of practicing many limbs of devotional service. This is described by Vyasadeva in ninth canto of Śrīmad – Bhāgavatam.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.266-268) stated

*sa vai manaḥ kṛṣṇa-padāravindayor vacāṅsi vaikuṇṭha-guṇānu-varṇane karau harer mandira-mārjanādiṣu śrutiñcakārācyuta-sat-kathodaye mukunda-liṅgālaya-darśane dṛśau tad-bhṛtya-gātra-sparśe ’ṅga-saṅgamam ghrāṇaṁ ca tat-pāda-saroja-saurabhe śrīmat-tulasyā rasanāṁ tad-arpite pādau hareḥ kṣetra-padānusarpaṇe śiro hṛṣīkeśa-padābhivandane kāmaṁ ca dāsye na tu kāma-kāmyayā yathottama-śloka-janāśrayā ratiḥ iti*

**Перевод:**

**“King Ambarīṣa fixed his mind on the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa, engaged his words in describing the abode of the Lord, his hands in cleansing the temple of the Lord, his ears in hearing the pastimes of the Lord, his eyes in seeing the form of the Lord, his body in touching the body of the devotee, his nostrils in smelling the flavor of the flowers offered to the lotus feet of the Lord, his tongue in tasting the tulasī leaves offered to Him, his legs in traveling to the holy place where His temple is situated, his head in offering obeisances unto the Lord, and his desires in fulfilling the desires of the Lord... and all these qualifications made him fit to become a mat-para devotee of the Lord.”**

Also in Śrīmad - Bhāgavatam (10.10.38) it is stated

*vāṇī guṇānukathane śravaṇau kathāyāṁ hastau ca karmasu manas tava pādayor naḥ smṛtyāṁ śiras tava nivāsa-jagat-praṇāme dṛṣṭiḥ satāṁ darśane ’stu bhavat-tanūnām*

**Перевод:**

**Henceforward, may all our words describe Your pastimes, may our ears engage in aural reception of Your glories, may our hands, legs and other senses engage in actions pleasing to You, and may our minds always think of Your lotus feet. May our heads offer our obeisances to everything within this world, because all things are also Your different forms, and may our eyes see the forms of Vaiṣṇavas, who are nondifferent from You.**

### Text 251

*sādhana bhaktira aṅga cātuḥ ṣaṣṭi kaṇa*  
*eka aṅga bahu aṅga kaila nirūpaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the sixty four limbs of sandhana bhakti or regulative devotional service and the performance of oe or more limbs of devotional service.**

In Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu (1.2.269) it is stated

*śāstroktayā prabalayā tattanmaryāda yānvitā vaidhī bhaktiryam kaiś can maryādā mārga ucyate*

**Перевод:**

**Since these limbs of regulative devotional service are based on awe and reverence, many learned scholars call these scriptural injunctions as path of awe and reverence.**

### Text 252

*śrī govinda pada dvandvam tāpa-traya vinaśanaṁ*  
*praṇamya likhita gāṅthe vaidhī bhakti kriyā mayā*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the symptoms of regulative devotional service under the shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī Govinda which destroy the three fold miseries of material existence.**

### Text 246-248

*śrī guru govinda kṛṣṇa bhakta vṛnda gaṇa*  
*śrī caitanya nityānanda kariyā smaraṇa abhirāma sundarānanda panuya gopāla kṛpā dṛṣṭi kāra prabhu parama dayāla śrī gopāla caraṇa śaraṇa abhilāṣa sādhana aṅga bhakti kahe naynānanda daśa*

**Перевод:**

**Remembering the lotus feet of my spiritual master, Śrī Govinda, the devotees of Kṛṣṇa, Caitanya, Nityānanda, Abhirāma, Sundarānanda and Panuya Gopal and desiring their shelter and mersifull glance I Nayanānanda Ṭhākura write the desriptions of Sādhana Bhakti.**

**End of chapter 5.**

Kṛṣṇa Bhakti Rasa Kadamba

## Chapter VI

*kalinda tanayā tira nipamuladhi daivatam dhamantam muralinada ajasram tam aham bhaje*

**Перевод:**

**I repeatedly offer my humble obeisances to Lord Kṛṣṇa who is playing on his flute, standing beneath a kadamba tree an the bank of jamuna.**

*jaya jaya śrī kṛṣṇa caitanya avatāra jaya rāma nityānanda ananta pracāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to the wonderful incarnation of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and all glories to Śrī Nityānanda who is non different from Lord Balarāma and Lord Ananta.**

*jaya jaya advaita candra gaura bhakta vṛnda jaya abhirāma saha śrī sundarānanda*

**Перевод:**

**All glories to Śrī Advaita Ācārya and all glories to all the devotees of Gauranga. All glories to Śrī Abhirāma Ṭhākura and Śrī Sundarānanda Prabhu.**

*catuhsasti bhakti aṅga kaila nirūpaṇa sevā nāma aparādha tāhāte varjana*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the sixty four limbs of devotional service. But one should carefully avoid committing offenses against the holi names and devotional service.**

*sevā nāma aparādha sastre ye vā kahe karibe se sava tyāga kahi śuna āge*

**Перевод:**
