**One should give up the offenses against the holi names and the devotional service as described in the scriptures. Now I will explain them as follows:**

Offenses against the execution of devotional service.

*asva dolaya capiya pādukā diyā pāya kṛṣṇa tīrtha kṣetra gṛhe jīva jāna jaya*

**Перевод:**

**To visit a holi place or a temple while riding on a horse or a palanquin as well as putting on shoes is an offense against the service of the Lord.**

*krsnotsava kṛṣṇa mūrti pathete dekhiyā dambhe pathe cali jaya nati na kariyā*

**Перевод:**

**If after seeing a procession of the Lord or a festival of the Lord one does not offer obeisances, but proudly walks away certainly commits an offense.**

*asuci ucista mukhe pranama kāraṇa ekahaste pranama sarva dharma vināśana*

**Перевод:**

**If one offers obeisances to the Lord in a contaminated state or offers obeisances with one hand then all his religious principles are destroyed.**

*agre pradakṣiṇa agre pada prasarana saksate prayanka bandhana akale darśana*

**Перевод:**

**It is an offense to circumambulate from the front, stretch the feet before the Lord and sit before the Lord with hands around his knees. One should not see the Lord in an odd time.**

*śayana bhajana mithya kathā alapana ucchabhasa grāmya kathā virodha rodana*

**Перевод:**

**One should not lye down, eat, speak lie, speak loudly, indulgein gossiping, oppose other and cry in lamentation before the Lord.**

*manusyeta krura bhasa nigraha kāraṇa śrī kṛṣṇa samipe nahe e saba ācaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One should not speak harsh language and subdue others in front of Lord Kṛṣṇa. These are offenses against the service of the Lord.**

*paraninda parastuti ucchabhasa ādi pūjā kāle nāhi dibe avarana kambaladi*

**Перевод:**

**One should not blaspheme, glorify or use harsh language to others at the time of worship. Also one should not put on blanket etc. while worshipping the Lord.**

*adhovayu parityaga śrī viṣṇu mandire janme janme bistha bhoji haya sei nāre*

**Перевод:**

**A person who passes air within the temple of Lord Viṣṇu certainly becomes a stool eater birth after birth.**

*samarthe gauna rūpe karaye arcana anivedita dravya je vā karāya bhakṣaṇa kāle prāpta miṣṭa phala na kare arpaṇa haniyukta anna ādi kare nivedana uccahsane thāki kare śrī kṛṣṇa pujana pujankale kare anyera abhivadana arma paurusa kare ātma sauryata gurura mahimā guṇa karaye gopita devatara nindā kare jīva saba jāna sevā aparādhī haya sei saba gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**It is an offense to worship the Lord in a simple way inspite of having tha capacity of worshipping oppulently, to eat unoffered foodstuffs, to not offer sweet and ripen seasonal fruits, to offer low quality foolstyff, to worship Kṛṣṇa while sitting on a raised platform, to greet others while worshipping the Lord, to glorify oneself before the Lord, to exhibit glories of spiritual master and to blaspheme the demigods.**

*agana anusāre ei sevaparadha varnila varāha purāṇe śuna ea saba kahila*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the offenses against the service of the Lord according to the vedas. Now please hear about those described in Varāha Purāṇa.**

*raja grhe siddha anna bhakṣaṇa yevā kare śrī mūrti karaye sparśa gṛha andha kare pada prakṣālana ādi vidhi na kariyā śrī mūrti karaye sparśa hathatkare jayia śabda na kariyā dvare kapata ghucaya kukura ādi ucchista dena devataya pūjā kāle mauna bhaṅga anya alapana mālā mutra tyagarthe vā karaye gamana mālā mutra uparodhe pūjā na karibe ataeva pūjā kāle savadhana habe gadha puṣpa na diyā dhūpa dipa samarpaṇa nindita puspe kiva karaye pujana*

**Перевод:**

**It is an offense against the Lords service to eat foodstuff by prepared non devotees, to eat previously boiled rice, to touch the deity in a dark room, to enter the deity room without washing one's hands and feet, to touch the deity suddenly, to enter the deity room without making a sound, to offer foodstuff to the Lord which has been eaten by animals like dog, to break one's silence during the worship of the Lord, to discuss other topics while worshipping the Lord, to pass stool or urine during worship of the Lord, to worship the Lord while suppressing the urge of passing stool or urine, to offer incense or ghee lamp without first offering sandalwood paste and flowers. One should carefully avoid these offenses during the worship of the Lord.**

*nindita kon puṣpa kara avadhana agama tantradi mate sunaha pramana*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear the names of the condemned flowers that are described in Agamas and Tantras.**

*rakta puṣpa kṛṣṇa puṣpa nirgandha durgandha krimi viddha bhume patita ati nindā*

**Перевод:**

**Dark red flowers, dark black flowers, fragrance less flowers, bad smell flowers, insects eaten flowers and the flowers that are have fallen on the ground are the most condemned flowers for offering to the Lord.**

*bhume patita dibe sephali bakula korna madhye dibe padma campaka phula kantake nindita puṣpa jhinti ādi kare kantakera madhye se ketaki dite pari*

**Перевод:**

**Sephali flowers and Bakula flowers can be picked up from the ground and offer to the Lord. Lotus and Campaka flowers can be offerdeven when are buds, any flowers with thorns should not be offered to the Lord. Although Ketaki is a thorny it can be offered to the Lord.**

*bhadra mase ketakira karebe varṇana pūrve rakta puṣpa jāni haiyache dusta naya iha bahi anya puṣpa na dibe niscaya*

**Перевод:**

**One should however not offer ketaki flower in the month of August. It has alreadybeen discribed previously that one should not offer dark red flowers to the Lord. But one can offer red lotus, red oleander and red baka flower.**

*paridhuia vastre anita puṣpa yata tridalera nyuna puṣpa nahe abhimata*

**Перевод:**

**Flowers that is plucked in an used cloth and flowers with out stalk should not be offered to the Lord.**

*bama naste speista āra sudre vā anita snāna kari tola puṣpa baḍai nindita*

**Перевод:**

**Flowers touched by ones left hand, flowers brought by a sudra and flowers plucked wearing the wet cloth after taking bath are very condemned.**

*urna tantu kesa sparse chinna bhinna dala basi puṣpa haya jāni nindita sakala*

**Перевод:**

**It is prohibited to offer a flower garland strung with woolen thread, the flowers that have touched ones hair torn flowers and stale flowers.**

*tulasīra pātra vilva gaṅgā jala sadā vasi dusta kabhu naya pavitra sarvadā*

**Перевод:**

**Tulasi leaves, Bael leaves and the water of the Ganges never become stale or old rather they are always pure.**

*akanda dhustura udra almali na dibe sirisa kunruchi kṛṣṇa kutaja varjibe*

**Перевод:**

**One should not offer flowers like sun plant stramonium, china rose, silk cotton, sirisa, kunruci, kṛṣṇa and kulaja.**

*bherendara pātre puṣpa na kara shapana curi kari phala puṣpa na kara grahaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Do not place flowers in a castor oil leaf. Do not offer stolen fruits or flowers to the Lord.**

*ityadi kahila dr nindita puṣpa nāma sevā aparādha puva kara avadhana*

**Перевод:**

**I have thus described the list of sevā aparadhas. Now hear again about some more sevā aparadhas.**

*danta dhavana pratah kriyadi nāhi kari śrī viṣṇu pujana karme nahe adhikari*

**Перевод:**

**One is not qualified to worship Lord Viṣṇu unless he brushes his teeth and completes his morning duties.**

*śrī gaṅgā kari puna na kariyā snāna kṛṣṇa paricaryā kari adhaḥ pate jāna*

**Перевод:**

**If ones does not take a bath after indulging in sexual intercourse and engages in the Lord's service, certainly goes to hell.**

*rajovarti nari saṅga kimbā sparśa kari śrī kṛṣṇa pūjane sei nahe adhikari*

**Перевод:**

**One should not worship Lord Kṛṣṇa after touching or having intercourse with a women who is in her menstrual period.**

*deepa sparśa kari hasta dhauta na kariyā mahat pātakī hoya paricaryā kariyā*

**Перевод:**

**It is an offense to not wash once hand after offering a ghee lamp to the Lord during the Lord's worship.**

*mrta deha sparśa kimbā tad vahaka chuiya saba dekhi puna snāna jīva na kariyā visnu seva kare kimba sri murti sparsana pirti saha saba mamsa se kare bhaksana*

**Перевод:**

**If one touches carries or sees a dead body and then engages in the service of visnu or touches deity of the lord without taking a bath immediately eats the flash of the dead with his father.**

*rakta vastra nila vastra dhagdha vastra pari asuci maline nahe puja adhikari*

**Перевод:**

**It is an offence to worship the lord by wearing red, blue, burnt, unclean or old cloth.**

*adhauta vastra kimba pariya das hina suci viddha vastra kimba grihasthe kopina citra vastra bahuvasa na kari dharana eka vastre nahe kabhu sri murti pujana*

**Перевод:**

**Various names of the Supreme Lord are Rama, Narayana, Mukunda, Madhusudana, Krsna, Kesava, The killer of Kamsa, Hari, Vaikuntha and Vamana.**

*sarva avatara nama maha phala kana tathapi visesa phala karaha sravana*

**Перевод:**

**Although it is said that by chanting the names of Lord’s various incarnations one achieves great results yet please hear the special fruits one obtains by chanting these names.**

*hari krsna rama ei ekatre smarana sahasra asvamedha nahe tahara sama*

**Перевод:**

**The result one obtains by chanting the holy names of Hari, Krsna and Rama together cannot be obtained by performing thousand horse sacrifices.**

In Brhad Vasistha samhita it is stated

*krsna krsneti rameti hari iti uktva tatah parama rajasuya sahasranam phalam apnoti manavah*

**Перевод:**

**If a person chants the most auspecious names of the supreme Lord such as Krsna, Rama and Hari obtains more results ten performing one thousand rajasuya sacrifices.**

*rajasuyadika phala pravarta karana mukhye phala krsne rati purasartha sadhana*

**Перевод:**

**To obtain attachment for Krsna which is the goal of the life is the principle root of chanting the holy names. All other fruits are secondary and meant for bringing one to the path of reaching the goal of life.**

*ataeva mahaprabhu sri krsna caitanya teena nama prakasiya jagata kaila dhanya*

**Перевод:**

**Therefor Mahaprabhu Sri Krsna Caitanya has made these world glorious by manifesting the three names Hare Krisna and Rama**

*nandasuta sri caitanya hoila avatara balarama nityananda agraja yahara*

**Перевод:**

**Krsna the son of Nanda Maharaj has appeared as Sri Caitanya and Balarama the elder brother of Krsna has appeared as Nityananda.**

*purvadasa sakha guru varga priyagana sangopange kaliyuge avatara hona*

**Перевод:**

**All the previous servants friends elderly persons and beloved ones of the Lord have appeared in the age of Kali as his associates.**

*panca rasera bhakta gana sangeta laiya harinama pracarila jivera lagiya*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Caitanya accopained by his devotees who belong to the five different types of rasas preached the holy names of Hari for the benefit of the living entities.**

*sambodhana harinama karila pracara brahamanda purana agni purana sloka ara*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord preached the holy names of Hari that are mentioned in Brahamanda Purana and Agni Purana.**

In Brahamanda Purana it is stated:

*hare krsna hare krsna krsna krsna hare hare iti japta pramucheta pataka na atra samsayah Hare Krsna Hare Krsna Krsna Krsna Hare Hare*

**Перевод:**

**Any sinful person who chants these holy names is at ones liberated. There is no doubt about it.**

In Agni purana it is stated as follows:

*hare rama hare rama rama rama hare hare sapaca api japat nityam muchyate srinu bhargana Hare Rama Hare Rama Rama Rama Hare Hare*

**Перевод:**

**O Bhargana! If a dog eater constantly chants these holy names he will at ones be liberated.**

*Purana duiyera sloka ekatra ganthiya hari nama pracarila jivea lagiya*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Caitanya has accumulated these holy names of Hari from the two Puranas and preached for the benefit of the living entities.**

*hare krsna rama ei namera artha suna sadasiva sambada taya karaha sravana*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear the meaning of the holy names of Hare Krsna and Rama as described by Lord Siva to the supreme Lord.**

*siva kahe suno prabhu ohe sanatana tava nama kirtane kritarha sarvajana*

**Перевод:**

**Siva said please hear O eternal Lord! Just by chanting your holy names everyone can become fully satisfied.**

*tava nama gane ami jagate pradhana svagana sahite puta kahi vidya mana*

**Перевод:**

**I am the topmost personality in this world by dint of chanting your holy names and as such I’m fully purified with my associates.**

*sarva jivera papa tomara name hare ataeva trigagati tava nama kare*

**Перевод:**

**since your holy names can destroy the sinful reactions of all living entities, therefore the people of the three world chant your holy names.**

*sarva jivera papa tapa dukha dure kare hagati tumara nama hoila sri hari*

**Перевод:**

**Because the sinful reactions and material miseries of all the living entities you have become renowed in this world as Lord Hari.**

*sakalera mana kiba karaha harana ei hetu hari bali trijagate kana*

**Перевод:**

**Since you attract the minds of everyone you are renowed as “Hari“ within the three worlds.**

It is stated in Padma Purana as follows:

*tat nama kirtanad visnoh putah pujyo janairaham tvam hamsi sarva jantunam manastena harih smrtah*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Siva said “O Lord Visnu! Just by chanting your holy names I have become pure and worshipable. Because you attract the minds of all living entities you are known as Hari**

Elsewhere it is stated as follows

*sarvesam jangamadinam devadinam visesatah hatati asau mana nityam tato harir iti smrtah*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, since You always attract the minds of all animate and inanimate beings particularly the demigods, you are known as Hari.**

*panca sloki harinama karah sravana bhagavat tattva jnata yahate se hana*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear about the holy names of the Lord described in the Panca sloka verses, knowing which one will realize the absolute truth.**

*sat cit ananda vigrahrupa avidya harana ajnata maya karma yahate khandana*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme Lord possess and eternal body which is full of knowledge and bliss. He destroys evetyone’s ignorance. He vanquishes the bondage of fruitive activities born of illusion.**

*avidyavidya karma yahate se hare purna brahma bhagavana hari bali tare.*

**Перевод:**

**Since that Lord removes one’s nescience and activities born of ignorance he is called the Supreme Brahman, the personality of Godhead.**

As stated in the scriptures as follows

*vijnapya bhagavad tattvam cit ghanananda vigraham harati avidyam tat karyam atih harirdi smrtah*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Personality of Godhead is known as Hari because He reveals the knowledge of His devotees. Because His body is eternal, full of knowledge and bliss and because He takes away everyone’s ignorance.**

*rama namera artha suna kahe tantrasare rama name purna brahma kahena vicare*

**Перевод:**

**Now please hear the meaning of the holy name “Rama” as described in the tantras. The holy name Tama refers to the Supreme Brahman.**

*ramana karaye atma rama hana yate satyanada cinmayatma sei ananteta ei hetu rama nama param brahma hana taraka brahma bali rama name kana*

**Перевод:**

**The self satisfied souls take pleasure in Him, therefore He is known as Rama, He is eternal, full of knowledge and bliss. The holy name Rama delivers everyone. Hence He is the supreme Brahman.**

As stated in the tantras

*amante yogino ’nante satyānande cid-ātmani iti rāma-padenāsau paraṁ brahmābhidhīyate*

**Перевод:**

**“The mystics derive unlimited transcendental pleasures from the Absolute Truth, and therefore the Supreme Absolute Truth, the Personality of Godhead, is also known as Rāma.”**

*caturveda adhyana phala sri visny smarane tadrk sahasra nama phala haya rama name*

**Перевод:**

**By remembering the holy name of Visnu one obtains the result of studying the four vedas, and by chanting the holy name Rama one obtains the results of chanting Visnu’s name for one thousand times.**

As stated in Padma Purana as follows

*visnor ekaika namapi sarva vedadhikam matam tadrin nama sahasrena sri rama nama sammatam*

**Перевод:**

**By chanting the holy names one achieves more piety then studying all the vedas. And by chanting thousand such names of Visnu whatever. Piety one obtain is equal to that obtained by chanting the name of Rama once.**

It is stated in the Padma Purana as follows

*rāma rāmeti rāmeti rame rāme manorame sahasra-nāmabhis tulyaṁ rāma-nāma varānane*

**Перевод:**

**“\[Lord Śiva addressed his wife, Durgā:\] ‘O Varānanā, ‘I chant the holy name of Rāma, Rāma, Rāma and thus enjoy this beautiful sound. This holy name of Rāmacandra is equal to one thousand holy names of Lord Viṣṇu.’**

It is stated in the scripture as follows

*rakarocharna deva bahir gachhanti patakah punar prabesa kale tu makarastu kavata kam*

**Перевод:**

**The alphabet “ra” from Rama means to drive away all the sins and the alphabet “ma” from Rama refers to the door which cheeks the sins to reoccur.**

*punarapi kahi suna vyakhyantara kari ramu kriyayam ghainta sadhana tahari gopa gopi laiya krsna karaye ramana rama sabde kahi krsna vrajendranandana*

**Перевод:**

**Another explanation is this. The word Rama is derived from the verb ramu which means to enjoy. Lord Krsna enjoy His pastimes with the cowherd boys and girls. Therefore the name Rama refers to Lord Krsna the son of Nanda Maharaja.**

*kona bhakta kahe rama rohini tanaya rameti loka ramanat bhagavate kaya*

**Перевод:**

**Some devotee say that the name Rama refers to Balarama the son of Rohini. This is confirmed in SB(10.2.13) where it is said “rameti loka ramanat”**

*garbha-saṅkarṣaṇāt taṁ vai prāhuḥ saṅkarṣaṇaṁ bhuvi rāmeti loka-ramaṇād balabhadraṁ balocchrayāt*

**Перевод:**

**The son of Rohiṇī will also be celebrated as Saṅkarṣaṇa because of being sent from the womb of Devakī to the womb of Rohiṇī. He will be called Rāma because of His ability to please all the inhabitants of Gokula, and He will be known as Balabhadra because of His extensive physical strength.**

*puna kahimarma vyakhya arthantara kari aiche vyakhyatantramate kahila vicari*

**Перевод:**

**Please hear some confidential explanation of the word Rama as described in the tantras.**

*ra kare kahiye radha ma kari krsna rama tina rupe purna kare vraja manas kama*

**Перевод:**

**The alphabet “ra” from the word Rama refers to Srimati Radharani and the alphabet “ma” refers to Krsna and Balarama. The Supreme Lord fulfill His Vraja pastimes in this three forms.**

*ataeva harinama vraja upasana punah krsna nama vyakhya suna savajana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore chanting the holy names of Hari is the highest type of worship in the mood of vraja. Now here further about explanations of Krsna’s names.**

*kr va caka krsi sabda nr vrtti na kara nrvrtti kahiye nityananda sukha yara*

**Перевод:**

**The alphabet “kr” from the word Krsna refers to the Supreme Brahamana and the alphabet na refers to eternal bliss.**

*dui aikya parama brahma saksat bhagavana sakara paramananda sri krsna akhyana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore the word Krsna refers to the Supreme Brahmana the personality of Godhead. He is full of eternal bliss and is known as Sri Krsna.**

As stated in ....????

*krisibhur vacakah*

**Перевод:**

**In Brhat Gautamya tantra it is stated as follows “ krsi sabdo hy sattvartho” Has to be translated.**

The explanation of the above verse is given as follows”bhavanti asmat” has to be translated.

In Vishnu purana “brihat tad brihan (has to be translated)

*ananda sukhera karta gokula mandale gokula nanda krsna nanda sute bale*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna the son of Nanda Maharaja is the reservour of pleasure and He gives happiness to all the ingabitants of Gokula.**

One of Pancasloki

*anandaika sukhasvami syamah kamala locanah gokulanandana nanda nandanah krsna visate*

**Перевод:**

**The lotus eyed blackish Personality is the son of Nanda Maharaja. He is the absolute enjoyer of all happiness. He gives pleasure to the inhabitants of Gokula. He is known as Krsna.**

*sarva nama madhye krsna nama srestha jani prabhasa purane dekha bhakti granthe suni*

**Перевод:**

**It is stated in the devotional literature Prabhasa Purana that among all the holy names of the Lord the name Krsna is the best.**

*visnor sahasranama tribara pathane sei phala krsna nama ekada smarane*

**Перевод:**

**The result one obtains by chanting the three thousand names of Visnu is obtained simply by chanting the name of Krsna once.**

It is stated in Prabhasa Purana as follows

*sahasra namnam punyanam triravrtya tu yat phalam ekavrtya tu krsnasya namaikam tat prayachati*

**Перевод:**

**The piety one obtains by chanting the three thousand names of Visnu is obtained just by chanting the name of Krsna only once.**

*hare krsna rama ei nama yajna sara kaliyuge mahaprabhu karila pracara*

**Перевод:**

**Sri Caitanya has prached that in Kaliyuga the highest sacrifice is the chanting of the holy names of Hare Krsna and Rama.**

*kala kala niyama nai e nama japite yaite thekite pathe bhaksana kalete*

**Перевод:**

**There is no consideration time for chanting these holy names. One can chant while walking, sitting or eating.**

*sarva kala sarva dese karibe kirtana krsna nama laite nai kalakala niyama*

**Перевод:**

**One should chant the holy names at any place or at any time. There is no consideration of proper of improper times regarding the chanting of Krsna’s names.**

In Brihat Naradiya Purana it is stated as follows:???

*vrajaṁs tiṣṭhan padaikena yathaivaikena gacchati yathā tṛṇa-jalaukaivaṁ dehī karma-gatiṁ gataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Just as a person traveling on the road rests one foot on the ground and then lifts the other, or as a worm on a vegetable transfers itself to one leaf and then gives up the previous one, the conditioned soul takes shelter of another body and then gives up the one he had before.**

*santa dasya sakhya vatsalya bhakta gana madhurasrta bhaktadi sabhara sadhana*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting the holy names of the Lord is the only engagement for the five types of devotees who serve the Lord in the mood of Santa, Dasya, Sakhya, Vatsalya and Madhurya.**

*ataeva mahaprabhu sri krsna caitanya sarva bhakte harinama kaila vitarana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore Sri Krsna Caitanya Mahaprabhu has distributed this holy names of Hari to all the devotees.**

*bhakta bhava angi kari ape avanite japi japaila nama ei ta jagete*

**Перевод:**

**He appeared in this world in the mood of a devotee. He personally chanted this holy names and induced others to chant.**

*sarva bhaktera adhikara ei hariname nistha haile prapti haya sadhananu krame*

**Перевод:**

**Every living entity is eligible to chant the holy names of Hari. If one keeps firm faith while chanting then he attains the goal of life.**

*dasya rasa bhakta yata japi harinama rasaladi dasa sange prapti vrajadhama*

**Перевод:**

**Those devotees who chants the holy names of Hari being absorbed in the mood of a servant and associate with the Lord’s servants such as Rasala.**

*sakhya bhakta japi nama sakha anugate rama krsna prapti haya vrajera sahite*

**Перевод:**

**Those who chant the holy names being absorbed in the mood of a friend and under the subordination of a Lord’s friends attain the association of Krsna and Balarama in Vraja.**

*vatsalya rasera bhakta sadhananusare nandasuta prapti tara haya nandisvare*

**Перевод:**

**According to their sadhana the devotees who are in the mood of a parents attain the association of Krsna, the son of Nanda Maharaja at Nandagrama.**

*madhura rasera bhakta o nama japiya radha krsna pada prapti gopi sanga paiya*

**Перевод:**

**By chanting these holy names the devotees who are under the shelter of Madhurya rasa attain the lotus feet of Radha Krsna in the association of the Gopis.**

*vasudeva bhakta gana o nama japite sri krsna carana prapti mahisi sahite*

**Перевод:**

**If the devotees of the Lord Vasudeva chant the holy names they will achieve the lotus feet of Krsna in the association of the Lord’s queens.**

*vasananusvare haya siddi krsna name raganuga ganera prapti haya vrindavane*

**Перевод:**

**According to one’s desire one attains perfection in the chanting of the holy names of the Lord. Devotees who follow the path of attachment attain the abode of Vrndavana.**

*sakama bhaktera haya vanchita kamana dharma artha svarga bhoga ye kare vasana*

**Перевод:**

**Those who chant the holy names with the aim of fulfilling material desires such as religiousity, economic development and sense gratification can easily achive them.**

*Krsna name sarva siddhi nama cintamani nama nami abheda purane ei suni*

**Перевод:**

**The holy names of the Lord is like touchstone and it bestows all perfections to a chanter. It is said in the purana that the Supreme Lord and His holy names are non different.**

*krsna hana parama brahma sabda brahma nama ataeva nama nami duita pradhana*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna is the Supreme Brahman and His holy names are also Brahman in the form of sound vibration. Therefore the Supreme Lord and His holy names are nondifferent.**

In Padma Purana it is said as follows

*nāma cintāmaṇiḥ kṛṣṇaś caitanya-rasa-vigrahaḥ pūrṇaḥ śuddho nitya-mukto ’bhinnatvān nāma-nāminoḥ*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The holy name of Kṛṣṇa is transcendentally blissful. It bestows all spiritual benedictions, for it is Kṛṣṇa Himself, the reservoir of all pleasure. Kṛṣṇa’s name is complete, and it is the form of all transcendental mellows. It is not a material name under any condition, and it is no less powerful than Kṛṣṇa Himself. Since Kṛṣṇa’s name is not contaminated by the material qualities, there is no question of its being involved with māyā. Kṛṣṇa’s name is always liberated and spiritual; it is never conditioned by the laws of material nature. This is because the name of Kṛṣṇa and Kṛṣṇa Himself are identical.’**

*ye yaiche bhaje krsna tare taiche hana karu putra karu mitra pati priyajana*

**Перевод:**

**As one worships Krsna he rewards them accordingly. He becomes someone’s son, friend, husband or beloved.**

*taha dekha bhagavate mallayuddha kale yara yena mati taiche dekhe rangasthale*

**Перевод:**

**This was evident during the wretling that took place in Mathura which is described i SB. According to one’s qualification one saw the Lord differently.**

*mallagana dekhe krsnere vajrasama jani naraloka dekhe yena narasrestha jani*

**Перевод:**

**The wrestlers saw Krsna like the thunder. The ordinary human beings saw Him like the best of the himan beings .**

*strigana dekhaye yena kandarpa murtimana sabhara ramanigana dekhi murcha pana*

**Перевод:**

**The cowherd women saw the Lord as the personification of cupid and the gopis of the assembly fell unconcious simply by seeing Him.**

*gopagana dekhe krsna sei sakha vara dusta gana dekhi bhaya bhavita antara*

**Перевод:**

**The cowherd boys saw Him as the best friend. The miscraents saw Him as the personification of fear.**

*rajagana dekhe yena sabhari sasana ama sabhara danda karta gopa besa hana*

**Перевод:**

**The kings saw Krsna as their chastiser, who has now appeared in the form of a cowherd boy.**

*vasudeva devaki mane sishu duijana na karila hena putre lalana palana*

**Перевод:**

**Vasudeva and Devaki considered the Lord as their child and lamented that they could take care of Him properly.**

*tattva jnani bhakta dekhe para tattva jnana vrisnigana dekhe parama devata samana.*

**Перевод:**

**The cultivator of spiritual knowledge saw Him as the absolute truth and the members of Vrsni dynasty saw Him as the supreme Lord.**

*yara yena mati tara kache taiche hana bhakt vatsalya bhava abhakte damana*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Krsna presents Himself to His devotees accordingly to their desires. He displays affection towards the devotees and vanquishes the nondevotees.**

*ataeva harinama cintamani sama ye ye rupe bhaje tare temana hana*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore the holy names of the Lord is like the touchstone. As the devotees worship Him, He rewards them accordingly.**

In SB (10.43.17) it is stated as follows

*mallānām aśanir nṛṇāṁ nara-varaḥ strīṇāṁ smaro mūrtimān gopānāṁ sva-jano ’satāṁ kṣiti-bhujāṁ śāstā sva-pitroḥ śiśuḥ mṛtyur bhoja-pater virāḍ aviduṣāṁ tattvaṁ paraṁ yogināṁ vṛṣṇīnāṁ para-devateti vidito raṅgaṁ gataḥ sāgrajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The various groups of people in the arena regarded Kṛṣṇa in different ways when He entered it with His elder brother. The wrestlers saw Kṛṣṇa as a lightning bolt, the men of Mathurā as the best of males, the women as Cupid in person, the cowherd men as their relative, the impious rulers as a chastiser, His parents as their child, the King of the Bhojas as death, the unintelligent as the Supreme Lord’s universal form, the yogīs as the Absolute Truth and the Vṛṣṇis as their supreme worshipable Deity.**

*dhyana yajna arcana vidhi chila yugantare kaliyuge nama binu nahika nistare*

**Перевод:**

**In the three previous yugas the process for attaining perfection were meditation, sacrifice and temple worship. But in Kaliyuga there is no means of deliverence other then chanting the holy names of the Lord.**

*prasanga paiya ithe karila varnana namaparadha madhye harinama kathana*

**Перевод:**

**While describing the offences against the chanting of the holy names I have thus described the glories of the holy names.**

*sadhana bhaktira madhye vaidhira sadhana catuh sasti bhaktira anga likhilama krame*

**Перевод:**

**while describing sadhana bhakti I have thus mentioned the sixty four limbs of devotional service in regard to the description of vaidhi bhakti or regulatibe devotional service.**

*sri caytanya nityananda sri rupera carana abhurama sundarananda kariya smarana sri parnigopala pade kari abhilasa e dasa nayanananda karila prakasa*

**Перевод:**

**Remembering the lotus feet of Sri Caytanya Mahaprabhu, Sri Nityananda Prabhu, Sri Rupa Goswami Prabhu, Sri Abirama Thakura, Sri Sundarananda Prabhu, I Nayanananda Thakura write this book.**

*Krsna bhakti rasa kadamba ye kare sravana se jana acala bhakti paya prema dhana.*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who hears Krsna Bhakti Rasa Kadamba certainly obtains uflinching devotion to the Lord and the Treasury of love of God.**
